# Afterglow feature history

The detailed record of every feature: what it does, where it lives, its tunable numbers, storage keys and tests. CLAUDE.md is the short guide; this is the reference. Add a bullet here (under its area or at the end) when a feature is finished. Everything below is merged into `main` as of 2026-10-03 unless it says otherwise; most of it still awaits testing on a real phone.


Milestones 5 to 10 are merged into `main`. Milestones 6.5 (Discover playback), 7 (taste from watch behaviour), 8 (Discover feed intelligence), 9 (festival discovery loop) and 10 (data architecture) still await on-device testing in Expo Go. Milestone 11 (catalogue files) is merged. The first real festival, EDC Orlando 2026 (from the official lineup poster the user sent), is in `catalogue/afterglow.json`: 110 acts, no set times or clips yet. Austin City Limits 2026 (`acl-26`, from the official poster the user sent on 2026-09-29) is there too: 138 acts in poster order (Friday, Saturday, Sunday), each with a note of its day and weekend ("Friday, weekend 1 only", "Saturday and Sunday"); start 2026-10-02, end 2026-10-11, `weekends` Oct 2 to 4 and Oct 9 to 11; location "Austin, Texas" (the poster names no venue); no set times, clips or poster link. It reuses `steve-aoki` (also at EDC) and the demo `levity` (the user confirmed it's the same dubstep trio). Sound tags for the clearly electronic acts (Skrillex, The Chainsmokers, RÜFÜS DU SOL, Sofi Tukker, Fcukers) and, since the wider sounds exist, 55 well-known pop/indie/rock/hip-hop acts, all set by Claude from general knowledge; the lesser-known rest are empty. Cyclops Cove 4 (`cyclops-cove-4`, Dec 4 to 6 2026, Sunset Cove Amphitheater, Boca Raton; official poster from the user) has 52 acts in poster order with each act's day as its note ("Friday", "Saturday and Sunday" for SUBTRONICS solo); b2bs are one act (EDC's convention), and the three record-label stage takeovers are listed as acts like EDC's Insomniac Records Takeover; VYHARA confirmed by the user. Eternal NYE 2026 (`eternal-nye-26`, Dec 30 and 31, Orlando Amphitheater, Orlando; official poster) has 34 acts in poster order (headliners, then the poster's "ABC by tier" rows); only KNOCK2 (Dec 30) and ZEDS DEAD (Dec 31, "Last festival set before hiatus") have days; the poster's unnamed mystery partners are notes ("B2B with a surprise guest" on DETOX UNIT and EPROM) and its lone "????" act is left out. Existing artists are reused by exact name (e.g. subtronics, taiki-nulight, troyboi, kompany, avello, ravenscoon, rl-grime). Sound tags for well-known bass acts were set by Claude; the rest are empty. Countdown NYE 2026 (`countdown-nye-26`, Insomniac's "Alien Invasion" New Year's Eve; name, dates and venue from the user: Dec 31 2026 and Jan 1 2027, NOS Events Center, San Bernardino) has the 71 acts on its poster in the poster's alphabetical order (the user says 75+ and 5 stages; stage names and the rest aren't known yet) (`whereWhen` in `festivals/format.ts` shows "dates, location" without a stray comma). `edc.test.ts` now checks that EDC artists join My Sound after liking LEVITY rather than naming two, since closer bass acts from other festivals can take the 40 map spots. Milestone 12 (rate artists directly) is merged. Screen smoke tests are merged. Festival dates and set notes are merged. "I'm going" is merged. Sharing your plan is merged. Lineup search is merged. The end of the Discover feed is merged. The lineup sound filter is merged. Start over is merged. Saved clips are merged. First-time tips in Discover are merged. Similar sound on the lineup is merged. Sharing a clip is merged. Liked clips on Your clips are merged. My Sound per festival is merged. Live now (during the festival) is merged. Rating from the map is merged. Ratings in the lineup are merged. Artist search on the Festivals tab is merged. Clips from festival cards are merged. A fix for the swipe tip is merged. "Sounds like" on Tune cards is merged. "Why this match" on artist pages is merged. "Leaning …" on My Sound is merged. The first-run welcome is merged. Listen links are merged. Choosing what to discover (festival picker and your mix), the ambient video glow, the Lineup/Profile tabs with the notifications bell, sound breakdowns, tucked-away festivals, wider sounds, festival reminders and two-weekend festivals are merged (PR #41). The full-day route and the Festivals tab update (artist or festival search, tiles, request a festival) day-by-day lineups, ratings taken at their word, your weekend of a two-weekend festival, Live now from the route, picks by day before set times, searching My Sound, tuning one day at a time, and sharing your plan as a picture are built (branch `claude/set-to-set`; the user batches work before merging). Built so far:
- **Discover**: full-screen vertical feed, one clip per page; tap to pause, double-tap to like, Like/Save/Skip rail, progress bar, haptics. Clips with a `Clip.source` play a section of an official YouTube upload in YouTube's own embedded player (`react-native-webview`), with the video on top and the clip info and Like/Save/Skip below it, never over the player. Clips without one, or whose video can't play, show the placeholder "stage scene" drawn in code (beams, beat-synced glow, crowd). The 10 YouTube sources in `data/mock/clips.ts` were verified by the user on 2026-09-28 (public, official channel, right artist, plays embedded).
- **Discover playback (Milestone 6.5)**: only the on-screen clip plays, and only while Discover is the visible screen and the app is in the foreground (another tab, a pushed page, or a backgrounded app pauses video and stage alike; coming back resumes unless you paused). Rules are pure in `features/discover/playback.ts` and tested. The feed only talks to `ClipPlayer` (still from the video while loading, fades into the player once it plays; picks the player per provider); YouTube details stay in `YouTubePlayer`/`youtube.ts`/`media.ts`, so a native video source can slot in later. A vertical swipe that starts on the video moves the feed (`feedPager.ts`). Optional vertical footage: `source.vertical: true` (official Shorts) gets a tall 9:16 player with the rail beside it (`videoLayout.ts`); no Shorts are in the feed yet. `ClipPlayer.onPlayed` reports only live, actually-playing seconds: any watch-time signal must use it and nothing else.
- **My Sound**: bubble map. Top match pinned at centre, others placed by sound direction and distance by match, label-aware collision (no overlaps), pan with momentum, pinch zoom, double-tap recenter, bubbles trail and wobble when panned, reshape animation when taste changes, glass artist sheet.
- **Festivals** tab, festival pages (lineup sorted For you / Billing, set times when published, optional lineup poster with full-screen zoom viewer).
- **Artist** pages: match, sound tags, where they play, clips (open in Discover), similar artists.
- **Taste engine** v1 and **persistent reactions** (AsyncStorage).
- **Watch behaviour (Milestone 7)**: how a real clip was watched is a quiet, passive taste signal. `features/taste/watch.ts` (pure) keeps one session per visit to a clip, fed only by `ClipPlayer.onPlayed` spans, and tracks *coverage* (merged parts of the clip), so repeated reports or seeking back never count the same seconds twice. On leaving the clip: ~all of it covered = `through`, a full pass plus half again = `replayed`, 1 to 4s played then swiped away = `quick`. Loading, hidden, background or stage-placeholder playback gives nothing; leaving Discover or opening the artist mid-clip excuses a short watch. Per clip the strongest watch is kept (through twice on separate visits = replayed; never beyond). Engine weights: through 0.3, replayed 0.5, quick -0.2 (like 1, save 1.3, skip -0.8); a skip cancels the watch on that clip, a like/save cancels a quick swipe, and all passive signal per artist is capped to [-0.3, 0.6], below one like or skip. Watches spill over to similar sounds like likes. My Sound's subtitle counts watched clips.
- **Discover feed ranking (Milestone 8)**: `features/recommend/feed.ts` (pure, deterministic, no randomness) orders the feed from the live taste (which already carries likes/saves/skips/watching and spill-over) plus what only the feed knows. Each artist is in one lane: `personal` (match >= 0.75), `explore` (below that, or unknown, but sound-similar >= 0.3 to one of your top 3 artists), `discover` (a different sound, or no signal at all; unknown artists first). Lanes take turns in a fixed pattern P P E P E D (empty explore turns fall back to personal). Every artist gets one clip before anyone's second, never the same artist twice in a row (feasibility guard at the tail). Blocks: fresh clips, then clips of artists you turned down (more skips than likes+saves), then clips seen in the last 6h (seen longest ago first), then skipped clips. Each item carries a `Reason`; `recommend/copy.ts` turns it into a quiet note after the match ("Close to ILLENIUM", "A different sound", "New to you"; strong matches get none). `useRankedFeed` keeps the clip on screen and the next one fixed and re-ranks the rest as you react. Viewing history (`state/seen.tsx`, clip -> last seen ms, bounded to 500) is marked when a clip is on screen while the feed is live; it never feeds the taste engine.
- **Festival discovery loop (Milestone 9)**: Discover is always focused on one festival (default: the first with clips) and only shows artists on its lineup. Its header ("Solstice 2026 ›", "Live sets from the lineup · dates") opens the festival page. Under each clip, the artist's place there (set time, or "On the lineup, set time to come") is a link to the festival page, with a small star if you starred them there. The artist button opens the artist in that festival's context (must-see star works). Festival pages have a "Discover the lineup" pill (`/?festival=<id>`) that refocuses Discover; jumping to a clip (`/?clip=<id>`) moves the focus if that artist doesn't play the current festival, and artist pages opened from a festival pass it along. Relationships are pure in `features/festivals/links.ts` (playsAt, lineupClips, defaultFocus, focusForClip, nextFocus, festivalLine, lineupReach) and tested.
- **Data architecture (Milestone 10)**: three layers. *Data* (`src/data`): plain-data models in `models.ts` (Artist, Festival, SetTime/FestivalSet, FestivalLineup, FestivalPhase, Clip, ClipSource, ArtistSimilarity, CatalogSnapshot, CatalogSource), mock records in `data/mock/` (the only mock data; `MOCK_SNAPSHOT`, `MOCK_CATALOG`, `mockSource`), `validate.ts` (cleans a snapshot and lists what it dropped; the mock has no problems), `catalog.ts` (`createCatalog`: indexed lookups, `clipsOf`, `festivalsOf`, `lineupOf`, `phaseOf`; separately published lineups replace a festival's lineup/sets), `festival.ts` (setFor, stageName, festivalPhase). *Recommendation logic*: pure modules taking data as arguments (taste engine, watch, similarity, feed, plan, sound-map layout, festival links); `features/taste/similarity.ts` holds `soundSimilarity` and `similarityWith(provided)` (catalogue scores first, sound tags as fallback), passed through `useTaste().similarity` so every "similar sound" agrees. *Presentation*: screens and components read catalogue data only via `useCatalog()` (`state/catalog.tsx`, `CatalogProvider`, starts with the mock catalogue; given a `source` it loads, validates and swaps in). Golden snapshot tests (`data/__tests__/golden.test.ts`, run `npx jest --ci`) prove the mock data computes exactly what it did before; `architecture.test.ts` enforces the layer rules.
- **Catalogue files (Milestone 11)**: real festivals, artists, set times and clips come in as a JSON catalogue file (format v1, guide in `docs/catalogue.md`, working example `docs/catalogue-example.json`). `data/file.ts` (pure) parses it forgivingly (YouTube links of any kind incl. Shorts -> vertical, "25:00" times, `artist`/`stage` keys, https-only images, safe ids, clips under 3 min) and never throws, returning plain-language problems. `data/merge.ts`: `mergeSnapshots` (file records first, same id wins) and `buildCatalog` (mode `add` on top of the demo data or `replace`; a file that leaves no festival with clips keeps the old data). `catalogue/afterglow.json` is bundled (`data/bundled.ts`, `BUILT_IN`; empty today). Optional `CATALOG_URL` (`config/data.ts`, https only, empty by default) points at a hosted static file: `data/remote.ts` `refreshCatalog` shows the saved copy (AsyncStorage, keyed to the link) then a fresh one, and never lets a broken, oversized or unreachable file replace good data. Problems go to the Expo terminal as a "Catalogue" warning in dev only. Discover falls back to the first festival with clips if its festival disappears.
- **Big lineups**: My Sound shows only artists the engine is informed about (prior or signal, incl. spill-over), best first, at most `MAP_MAX` = 40 (`artistsOnMap` in `sound-map/layout.ts`), so it grows as you react. "Your festival" shows the best `EXPLORE_SHOWN` = 8 explore picks plus "N more to explore in the full lineup below". Discover opens on the festival with the most clips (`defaultFocus`). An artist may have no `sound` (shows as New; never reached by spill-over). EDC sound tags were set by Claude from general knowledge for 74 acts and left empty for 35; the user may correct them.
- **Artist ratings (Milestone 12; five steps since Milestone 62)**: rate an artist without a clip, "Love their sound" or "Not for me" (`features/taste/ratings.ts`, pure; `state/ratings.tsx`, AsyncStorage `artistRatings.v1`). The engine counts love like a save (1.3) and pass like a skip (-0.8) for spill-over to similar sounds; for the rated artist itself the answer is taken at its word: love is at least `RATED.love` = 0.8 (a strong match, so loved artists land in your plan and route), pass at most `RATED.pass` = 0.3 (below explore, so they leave it). `computeTaste(..., similarity, ratings)`, empty ratings change nothing. The feed treats a pass as turning the artist down (cooled) and never penalises a love. Artist pages have `RateArtist` pills. Festival pages have a "Tune your lineup" pill opening `tune/[id]` (modal): lineup artists one at a time in billing order minus rated ones (`features/festivals/tune.ts`, queue fixed at start), Love / Not for me / Don't know them, undo, progress, a finished state; card slides out toward the answer (off with reduced motion). My Sound's subtitle counts rated artists (`useTaste().rated`).
- **Screen smoke tests**: `src/app/__tests__/screens.test.tsx` renders the real routes, providers and catalogue with `expo-router/testing-library` (`renderRouter`) and exercises Festivals, EDC's page, Solstice's timetable, the Tune flow (love, undo, skip, pass), artist rating, My Sound and Discover focus. Setup in `test/setup.ts` mocks AsyncStorage, haptics, the web view, worklets, and patches reanimated's mock at `react-native-reanimated/mock` (expo-router's testing library re-mocks reanimated with it). With @testing-library/react-native 14, `render` and `fireEvent` are async: always `await` them. The virtualized feed only renders clips near the top in tests (no scrolling). Discover clears handled `clip`/`festival` params with the screen's own `navigation.setParams`, which works on a cold-start link; the global `router.setParams` throws before the navigation container is ready.
- **Festival dates and set notes**: `Festival.startDate`/`endDate` ("YYYY-MM-DD"; catalogue keys `start`/`end`, checked as real days, end not before start) drive a countdown (`features/festivals/when.ts`, pure: "In N days" under 60, then months, "Tomorrow", "Happening now", "Over"; calendar days in local time) on the Festivals card and festival page, via `lib/useToday` (refreshes on foreground). `Festival.notes` (artist id -> short text; catalogue lineup entries `{ "artist", "note" }`; dropped for artists not on the lineup) shows after the set time via `setLine` in lineup rows, artist pages and under Discover clips. EDC has its dates and the poster's notes (10 sunset sets, SAN HOLO wholesome riddim set, SKULL MACHINE = Black Tiger Sex Machine x Kai Wachi).
- **I'm going**: festival pages have an "I'm going" / "Going" pill (`GoingPill`); `state/going.tsx` (AsyncStorage `going.v1`, planning only, never fed to the taste engine). The Festivals tab groups festivals (`groupFestivals` in `features/festivals/going.ts`, pure): "Going" first (in progress or soonest start first, then undated, then finished), then a "More festivals · N" row that shows or hides the rest (collapsed by default, local state; festivals you're not going to stay out of the way); with nothing going, every festival shows with no headings. My Sound's festival chips also list festivals you're going to first.
- **Share your plan**: a share glyph beside "Your festival" opens the system share sheet (`Share.share`, built into React Native) with `planText` (`features/plan/share.ts`, pure): before set times, Must see and Strong match lists; after, day by day ("11:40 PM  ILLENIUM, Main Stage", close calls as "or ...", clear/pinned clashes as "(clashes with ...)") plus "Set time to come"; ★ for starred picks; explore picks left out; "Made with Afterglow". Hidden when nothing is planned.
- **Lineup search**: festival pages with more than `SEARCH_FROM` = 12 acts get a "Find an artist" field above the full lineup that filters as you type (`matchesName` in `features/festivals/search.ts`, pure: case, accents and punctuation ignored, spaces optional), with a clear button and "No one on the lineup matches …". The page scroll keeps taps with the keyboard up and dismisses it on drag.
- **Sound filter**: under the search on the same big lineups, chips "All sounds, Melodic bass 9, Dubstep 20, …" narrow the lineup to one sound (`lineupSounds`, `carriesSound`, `filterLineup` in `search.ts`: an artist is a sound from `SOUND_MIN` = 0.5 of it; a sound is offered when at least 3 artists carry it; shown only when two or more are). Works together with the name search; tapping the chosen chip again shows everyone. Artists without sound tags only show under All sounds.
- **Start over**: once you've reacted or rated, My Sound shows a quiet "Start over" pill beside the title. It confirms with a system alert, then clears reactions, ratings and viewing history (each provider's `clear()`), and the map reshapes a beat later (`reshapeSoon`, the same step as coming back to the tab). Festivals you're going to and starred sets stay: they're plans, not taste.
- **Your clips** (saved and liked): once you've saved or liked a clip, My Sound shows a bookmark pill with the count beside the title ("Your clips, N", each clip counted once) that opens `saved.tsx` (stack push, titled "Your clips"): a Saved section, then a Liked section (liked but not saved), in catalogue order (`yourClips`, `listedClips`, `openedWith` in `features/discover/saved.ts`, pure), each row with the YouTube still (or the artist gradient), artist, clip title, festival and length (`formatDuration` in `festivals/format.ts`). Tap a row to play it in Discover (`/?clip=<id>`); the bookmark unsaves and the heart unlikes (both taste signals, as in Discover), and the row stays for that visit so it can be undone. Empty state explains how to fill it.
- **First-time tips**: Discover's header shows one quiet tip at a time beside the festival name (`FeedHint`, fades in after ~1s, off with reduced motion): "Swipe up" (chevron), then "Double-tap" (heart); screen readers get the full sentence. Each is done for good once you've done it: swipe when you move the feed yourself (`onViewableItemsChanged`, so a jump to a clip from a link doesn't count; Skip does), like on any like or save. History from before this visit is checked once when everything has loaded (`features/discover/hints.ts`, pure: `currentHint`, `alreadyKnown`: more than one clip seen before now = swiped, any like or save = reacted), so people who used the app before never see them. Stored in `state/hints.ts` (AsyncStorage `hints.v1`, a hook, not a provider); nothing shows until hints, reactions and viewing history have loaded. Start over doesn't bring them back.
- **Similar sound on the lineup**: an artist page opened from a festival shows "Similar sound at <festival>" from that lineup only (falls back to the whole catalogue, titled "Similar sound", when no one there is close), and tapping one keeps the festival context (set, notes, must-see star). `mostSimilar(artist, candidates, similarity, { max: 3, min: 0.3 })` in `features/taste/similarity.ts` (pure) is shared by both.
- **Share a clip**: video clips have a small share glyph at the end of the credit line under the player (never over it) that opens the system share sheet with `clipShareText` (`features/discover/share.ts`, pure): "ARTIST, clip title", the YouTube link to that moment (`media.externalUrl`, with the start time), "Playing <festival>, <dates>" when the clip's festival line applies, and "Found on Afterglow". Drawn-stage clips have no link, so no share.
- **My Sound per festival**: chips under My Sound's subtitle ("Everyone", then each festival; `SCOPE_H` = 44 added to the header) narrow the map to one lineup (`mapCandidates(artists, lineup)` in `sound-map/layout.ts`, pure, then the usual `artistsOnMap`); changing scope closes the sheet and draws a fresh map (`SoundMap` keyed by scope: fitted, centred, bubbles bloom in). With fewer than `SPARSE_MAP` = 5 of its artists on the map, a glass note says how many of the lineup are placed and offers Tune your lineup (or, when all are rated, to react to clips). `/sound?festival=<id>` opens scoped (param cleared with the screen's `navigation.setParams`); Tune's finished state links to it ("See it on My Sound").
- **Live now**: during a festival with set times, "Your festival" shows a glowing card above the timetable: "Now · Friday", who your plan says to watch right now (both names for a close call, "Something new" for a discovery) with stage and end time, then "Next" with time and stage, or "That's everything on your plan tonight." The timetable opens on tonight. `features/plan/live.ts` (pure): `festivalNight` (nights run noon to noon like set times, `DAY_BREAK`; night one is `startDate`; needs `endDate` and exactly one `days` entry per date, otherwise no live view) and `liveNow(festival, schedule, now)`. `LiveNow.tsx` ticks with `lib/useNow` (every minute and on foreground) and is only mounted from the first day to the morning after the last. Not visible until a festival has real dates and set times (EDC needs set times).
- **Rate from the map**: My Sound's artist sheet has the `RateArtist` pills (Love their sound / Not for me) and shows the live match, so a rating's effect is visible at once; closing the sheet calls `reshapeSoon`, so the map reshapes around it a beat later (the bubbles keep showing the last-seen taste until then, as on returning to the tab).
- **Ratings in the lineup**: `ArtistRow` takes an optional `rating`; the festival page's full lineup passes it. Love adds a small glow heart after the name ("you love their sound" for screen readers); pass dims the row to 50% and adds "Not for you" to its detail line.
- **Find any artist**: the Festivals tab has a "Find an artist" field under its title; while typing, the festival cards give way to matching artists (`searchArtists` in `festivals/search.ts`, pure: name starts first, then a word start, then anywhere; up to 30) as `ArtistRow`s with "Plays <festivals>", match and your rating, opening the artist (in the festival's context when they play just one). "No artist matches …" when none. The field is the shared `components/SearchField` (also used by the festival page's lineup search).
- **Clips from festival cards**: a Festivals card whose lineup has clips shows a small glow "▶ N" pill (N from `lineupReach`) that opens Discover focused on it (`/?festival=<id>`); the rest of the card still opens the festival page. For screen readers it's a "Discover the lineup" action on the card.
- **Sounds like (Tune)**: each Tune card adds "Sounds like X and Y" under the match: up to two artists you love (rating) or strongly match (informed, >= `TIER_AT.strong`), at least `SOUNDS_LIKE_MIN` = 0.4 alike (`soundsLike` in `features/festivals/tune.ts`, pure, via `mostSimilar`). Answers given in the same session count straight away. No line for artists with no sound tags or no close favourite.
- **Why this match**: under the match on artist pages, a quiet line says what it rests on (`features/taste/why.ts`, pure: `artistSignals` counts your likes, saves, clips watched through and skips on their clips plus your rating; `whyLine`): "From your 2 likes and 1 save", "From your rating and 1 like" (the rating itself is the pill right below, so it's only named); with no own signal "From sounds you like" (informed: prior or spill-over) or "A first guess. Rate them or react to their clips to sharpen it."
- **Your lean**: once you've reacted or rated, My Sound's subtitle ends "Leaning melodic bass and dubstep." instead of "Bigger means a closer match." (`soundLean` in `features/taste/lean.ts`, pure: informed artists pull towards their sounds by how far their match is above 50%; the strongest sound, plus a second with at least `SECOND_SHARE` = 25% of the pull; computed over every artist, from the taste the map is showing).
- **First-run welcome**: `welcome.tsx` (full-screen modal, no swipe to dismiss), three steps with a Skip on each: hello ("Live sets from the festivals you're going to, and a plan built from the sound you love."), "Where are you going?" (festival checklist using the real `useGoing`, with countdowns; Continue / Not sure yet), then "Tell us what you love" → Tune your lineup for the first festival you picked (`router.replace` to `/tune/<id>?then=festival`, whose finished button then opens the festival page) or "Start watching" (`router.dismissTo` Discover, focused on that festival when it has clips; `watchFestival`), or "Start with the sets" when none picked. `features/welcome/FirstRun.tsx` (in the root layout, renders nothing) opens it once navigation is ready and reactions, ratings, going, seen and `state/welcome.ts` (AsyncStorage `welcomed.v1`) have loaded, only for a newcomer (`isNewcomer` in `features/welcome/firstRun.ts`, pure: no reactions, ratings, going or seen clips); anyone with history is marked welcomed instead, so Start over never brings it back. Going and ratings providers now expose `hydrated`. Screen tests set `welcomed.v1` in `beforeEach`; the Welcome tests remove it.
- **Listen links**: somewhere to hear an artist before rating them. `listenLinks(name)` in `features/taste/listen.ts` (pure): a YouTube search for "<name> live set" and a Spotify search, as plain links to the services' own search pages (nothing fetched or copied). Artist pages have a "Listen" section with YouTube and Spotify pills (`Linking.openURL`); Tune has "Listen on YouTube" beside "Don't know them" (outside the card, so VoiceOver reaches it), and the same card is still there when you come back.
- **Choose what to discover**: Discover's header ("Solstice 2026 ⌄") opens a sheet (`discover-focus.tsx`, iOS form sheet) with "Your mix" (every clip from every lineup, ranked as usual; header "Your mix", "A bit of everything, from every lineup"), then festivals you're going to (soonest first, ones with no clips shown dimmed), then others with clips; festivals you're not going to with no clips aren't offered. "Open <festival>" at the bottom reaches the festival page. Picking goes back with `/?festival=<id>` (`mix` for the mix). Pure in `features/discover/focus.ts` (`MIX`, `startFocus`, `nextDiscoverFocus`, `mixContext`, `focusChoices`). Discover opens where you left it (`state/discoverFocus.ts`, AsyncStorage `discoverFocus.v1`), else the first festival you're going to with clips, else the one with the most clips; it waits for that and `going` to load before choosing (a `festival` link param is cleared only once decided). In the mix a clip's festival line and star use a festival you're going to where the artist plays, else the clip's own. A clip link keeps the mix. The end of the feed offers "Try your mix" (and in the mix: other festivals and watch again).
- **Ambient glow behind videos**: behind a video player (landscape or vertical) the drawn stage is replaced by `AmbientBackdrop`: the clip's own still (`media.posterUrl`) with expo-image, scaled up and blurred (`blurRadius` 40), darkened, with night fades top and bottom so the header and clip info stay readable. It breathes slowly (scale/opacity) while the clip plays, still with reduced motion. Colours come from the still, not the live picture (the YouTube player can't be sampled in Expo Go). Clips without video keep the stage.
- **Five tabs**: Discover, Lineup, My Sound, Festivals, Profile (`GlassTabBar` now stretches to the screen with flexible items). The festival page's body is `features/festivals/FestivalPage.tsx` (`festival`, `topSpace`, `bottomSpace`, optional `above`), used by `festival/[id].tsx` (adds the back button) and the **Lineup** tab (`(tabs)/lineup.tsx`): "Your lineup" for the festivals you're going to (`lineupChoices` in `festivals/going.ts`, soonest first, chips to switch when more than one), otherwise "Lineups" with chips for every festival and a nudge to tap I'm going. **Profile** (`(tabs)/profile.tsx`): festivals / artists rated / clips kept, a "Your sound" card (top three sounds from `tasteMix`, opens the breakdown), "Going" (your festivals soonest first, as they are for you, with "Weekend 2 ·" and the countdown), a Your clips row, and a "Coming soon: your festival profile" card.
- **Notifications bell**: top right of the Festivals title (`components/NoticeBell`, glowing count of unread). Notices are worked out on the device (`features/notifications/inbox.ts`, pure: for festivals you're going to, "X is happening now", "X starts in N days / tomorrow" within `SOON_DAYS` = 7, "Set times are out for X" until it's over; ids stable so read stays read). `state/notices.tsx` (`NoticesProvider`, AsyncStorage `noticesRead.v1`). `notifications.tsx` (stack push) lists them, new ones glowing for that visit, and marks all read; each opens the festival. Likes and followers need accounts.
- **Sound breakdowns**: `features/taste/lean.ts` adds `toShares` (weights -> whole percentages adding to 100, largest remainder, largest first, zero-rounding sounds dropped), `tasteMix` (the same pull as `soundLean`, so the chart and "Leaning …" agree) and `artistMix` (the artist's `sound` weights). `SoundBreakdown` (features/taste) draws ranked bars in the one glow colour (to scale, full track = 100%; the top bar brighter; grows in, off with reduced motion; each row read as "Dubstep, 62 percent"). Artist pages show "Their sound" as a section after the hero (it replaced the sound tag chips). My Sound's chips row starts with a "Breakdown" chip opening `your-sound.tsx` (form sheet; `?festival=<id>` for the chosen scope): "62% melodic bass" headline, then the bars, from the live taste; a first-guess note before any signal.
- **Wider sounds**: `SoundTag` now also has `pop`, `indie`, `rock`, `hip-hop` (appended to `SOUND_TAGS`, labels Pop / Indie / Rock / Hip-hop), so ACL-style lineups work in the map, sound filter, breakdowns, Tune and similarity. The map ring is `RING_PLACE` in `sound-map/layout.ts` (sixths of a turn from the top): the six electronic sounds keep their original places (so the golden tests are unchanged) and the new ones sit in gaps (rock 0.5 by melodic bass/dubstep, hip-hop 3.5 by trap, indie 4.5, pop 5.5 between house and melodic bass). Catalogue files accept the new tags (`docs/catalogue.md`). Sound names around the map skip a label that would sit within `REGION_GAP` (72 × 22) of a stronger one (most artists pulling that way), kept in the original order.
- **Festival reminders (phone notifications)**: `expo-notifications` (added with `EXPO_OFFLINE=1 npx expo install`, since api.expo.dev is blocked in cloud sessions; local notifications work in Expo Go, only server push was removed from it, and it logs a "not fully supported in Expo Go" warning). `features/notifications/reminders.ts` (pure): `festivalReminders(festivals, going, now)` gives "X is a week away" and "X is tomorrow" at `REMINDER_HOUR` = 10 local time, 7 days and 1 day before `startDate`, only future ones; wording (and id) changes once set times are out; ids start with `REMINDER_PREFIX`; `reminderChanges` works out what to cancel and schedule, never touching other notifications. `ReminderSync` (root layout, renders nothing) syncs whenever `going` changes, one sync at a time, asks permission only after a change you make (tapping I'm going, or picking festivals in the welcome), never on the sync when the app opens, and never again if refused; renders nothing on web (`Platform.OS`), where the module doesn't exist, and opens the festival when a reminder is tapped (`useLastNotificationResponse`). The notification handler shows banners in the foreground without sound. Tests mock the module in `test/setup.ts` with an in-memory schedule (`__scheduled`).
- **Two-weekend festivals**: optional `Festival.weekends` (`[{ start, end }]`, 2+, in order; catalogue key `weekends`, which also fills `start`/`end`; bad lists are dropped with a plain problem). `festivals/when.ts`: `runsOf` (weekends, or one run from start to end) and `nextRun` (the run on now or next: index, count, days until); `countdown` says "Weekend 2 in 4 days" / "Weekend 2 tomorrow" between weekends instead of "Happening now", unchanged for one-run festivals. The bell's now/soon notices use the current run ("ACL weekend 2 starts in 3 days"), and reminders come a week before the first weekend and the day before each ("ACL weekend 2 is tomorrow"); ids for one-run festivals are unchanged. Live view still needs one `days` entry per date.
- **Full-day route**: once set times are out, "Your festival" opens on **Full day** (a `Full day` / `Your picks` toggle in `Timetable`; Your picks is the old picks-and-clashes view). `features/plan/route.ts` (pure): `dayRoute(input, day)` / `festivalRoute(input)` cut the day into slices at every set start/end and find the best path exactly (dynamic programming): value per minute = match (known), `DISCOVERY_VALUE` 0.3 (no signal), `MUST_SEE_VALUE` 3 (starred); below `MIN_VALUE` 0.15 a break beats the set; `SWITCH_COST` 12 per stage change; stints under `MIN_STINT` 15 min fold into a neighbour; no breaks at the ends. Changing stages takes `WALK_MINUTES` = 10 (`walks` after tidying): leaving early anyway means leaving 10 min sooner, otherwise you arrive 10 min into the next set; a must-see is never cut short for a walk (the next stop shrinks, or goes if only a sliver is left), and you leave early to be there for the start of a must-see; the stop records `walk`, shown as "Head to Bass Tent · 10 min walk". Leaving before a set ends reads "First 50 min, leave at 10:50 PM". Each stop also lists up to `ALSO_ON_MAX` = 2 other sets on while you're there that you'd enjoy (starred, or known and at least `ALSO_ON_MIN` = 0.5), best first (`alsoOn`), shown under the card as "Also on SUBTRONICS  Bass Tent  96%", each opening the artist, with a small star on the line itself to star them there and have the route switch. Stops carry from/to on the festival clock, the set's own start/end, match, pinned, known; `stintLabel` gives "Whole set", "First 45 min", "Last 20 min". `RouteList` draws the timeline: "Head to <stage>" when you change stages, stage, stint and end time, match or "New to you" (dashed), must-sees glowing, breaks quiet ("10 min break" up to 20 min, else "Break until …"). The share glyph on Your festival sends the route once set times are out (`routeText` in `plan/share.ts`: "My <festival>, set to set", per day "10:00 PM  TAPE B, Bass Tent (whole set)", ★ for must-sees, no breaks); before, `planText` as before. `clockTime` (route.ts) formats festival-clock minutes. During the festival the Live now card follows the route (`liveRoute` in `plan/live.ts`: the stop you should be at now and the next one; `LiveNow` takes `route` and shows "Main Stage, leave at 12:15 AM" when the route leaves early, "(head over)" when the next set is on another stage, "A break right now." in a gap); without a route it uses the schedule as before.
- **Festivals tab update**: the search says "Artist or festival" and, while typing, lists matching festivals first (`searchFestivals` in `festivals/search.ts`: name matches ranked like artists, then place matches) as rows, then artists; "No festival or artist matches …". A glass toggle beside the bell switches cards and square two-column tiles (`FestivalTile`: countdown or "Set times out", name, "N you'll love", three faces, ▶ clips pill; tinted by your top match's colour), remembered in `state/festivalsView.ts` (AsyncStorage `festivalsView.v1`). **Request a festival**: "Missing a festival? Request it" under the list, and "Can't find it? Request "<search>"" when no festival matches, open `request.tsx` (form sheet: name, optional lineup link, Send). It sends by email to `REQUEST_EMAIL` (`config/app.ts`, empty for now) with `requestMailto`, otherwise through the share sheet (`requestText`); pure in `features/festivals/request.ts`; sent requests are kept in `state/requests.ts` (AsyncStorage `festivalRequests.v1`) and a thank-you replaces the form.
- **Day by day**: optional `Festival.artistDays` (artist id -> days from `days`; catalogue lineup entries `{ "artist", "days": ["Friday"] }`; checked in `validate.ts`). ACL, Cyclops Cove and Eternal NYE carry them (ACL's notes now keep only the weekend: "Weekend 1 only"). `features/festivals/days.ts` (pure): `daysOf` (set times win, else artistDays, in festival order), `dayList` ("Saturday and Sunday"), `lineupDays` (a festival's days worth filtering by: at least half the lineup has a day and more than one day is used), `playsOn`. Lineup rows, the line under Discover clips and artist pages show the day before set times ("Friday · Weekend 1 only", "Friday, set time to come"). Big lineups get "All days · Friday 51 · …" chips (`components/FilterChips`) above the sound chips, combined with search and sound. **My Sound's header row** is now: Breakdown, one scope pill ("Everyone ⌄" / festival name, opens `sound-scope.tsx`: a form sheet with Everyone, Going, More festivals, which returns via `/sound?festival=<id>` or `everyone`, `EVERYONE` in `sound-map/scope.ts`), then that festival's day chips; picking a day narrows the map to that day's acts (map keyed by scope and day).
- **Your weekend**: optional `Festival.artistWeekends` (artist id -> the one weekend they play; catalogue lineup entry `"weekend": 1`; unlisted artists play every weekend; checked against `weekends`). ACL's 80 "Weekend N only" acts carry it. `features/festivals/weekend.ts` (pure): `playsWeekend`, `validWeekend`, `forWeekend(festival, w)` (only that weekend's acts, notes, days and sets; start/end narrowed to that weekend; no `weekends`), `runLabel` ("Oct 9 to 11"). `state/weekends.tsx` (`WeekendsProvider`, AsyncStorage `weekends.v1`, planning only) gives `forYou(festival)`; the festival page (and Lineup tab), Tune, the Festivals tab (cards, tiles, search), My Sound's festival scope and breakdown, the bell and reminders all use it. The welcome's "Where are you going?" step asks which weekend under a ticked two-weekend festival. The festival page shows "Your weekend" chips (Every weekend / Weekend 1 · Oct 2 to 4 / …) under the pills, and "Your plan and the lineup show weekend 2: 98 of 138 acts."
- **Picks by day (before set times)**: when `lineupDays(festival)` has days, "Your festival" shows a By day / Ranked toggle (By day first). `features/plan/byDay.ts` (pure): `picksByDay` gives each day's must-sees and strong matches (starred first, then must-see, then match) plus up to `EXPLORE_PER_DAY` = 3 explore picks (known first), and planned picks with no day ("Day to come"). Sharing follows it: `planText` lists picks day by day when the lineup has days. `ByDayList` draws a glass card per day ("2 for you", PickRows with tier or "You starred this one.", "Worth a look"). Names inside plan sentences are now the artist's name as written (`plainName` no longer guesses capitals: "The Xx", "Dj Snake").
- **Search My Sound**: a magnifier chip starts the row under My Sound's subtitle; it swaps the row for a `SearchField` ("Artist or sound, like dubstep") and Done. `features/sound-map/search.ts` (pure): `soundsMatching` (a word of the sound's name starts with the search, from 2 letters: "dub", "bass", "hiphop"), `mapSearch(candidates, query)` (everyone matching by name or carrying a matched sound, `SOUND_MIN`), `extraMatches` (matches not on the map join it, best first, up to 15). `SoundMap` takes `matchIds`: matching bubbles pop (focus springs to `MATCH` 2.8 with the bloom spring, plus their own glow), the rest fade, and the camera glides to fit the matches (only when the matches change; back to fit when the search ends). A glass note says "14 riddim artists, 12 new to your map. Tap one to see more." or "No artist or sound matches …". Searches the current scope and day.
- **Tune one day**: on a lineup with days (`lineupDays`), Tune shows "All days 98 · Friday 30 · …" chips (with how many are left to rate) until the first answer, then the day is fixed; the subtitle says "ACL 2026 · Sunday · 1 of 45". `tune/[id]?day=Friday` opens on a day; the festival page's Tune pill passes the lineup's chosen day. The queue is the lineup's billing order filtered by `playsOn`; an empty day says "You've rated everyone playing Sunday at …".
- **Share your plan as a picture**: the share glyph on Your festival opens `plan-card/[id].tsx` (modal): a poster-like card (night gradient with a soft corner glow, AFTERGLOW, festival name, dates and place, then sections, "Made with Afterglow"), "Share image" (`react-native-view-shot` `captureRef` to a PNG, then `expo-sharing` `shareAsync`; both in Expo Go, added with `EXPO_OFFLINE=1 npx expo install`) and "Share as text" (the old text). `planCard` in `plan/share.ts` (pure): the route per day once set times are out ("10:00 PM  TAPE B  Bass Tent", ★ for must-sees), else picks by day when the lineup has days, else Must see / Strong match; at most `CARD_LINES` = 7 per section plus "and N more". `features/plan/useFestivalPlan.ts` computes picks, schedule, route, text and card once for both screens (follows your weekend). Tests mock view-shot and sharing in `test/setup.ts`.
- **End of the feed**: after the last clip, `FeedEnd` (the FlatList footer, one page tall) says "You've seen every clip from <festival>" and offers Tune your lineup (if artists are left to rate), See your festival, Discover <other festival> (`otherFestivalsWithClips` in `links.ts`, most clips first, up to 2) and Watch again from the top. It counts as reached only when fully on screen (scroll offset >= count x page height, in the scroll worklet), which sets `activeIndex = count` so the last clip stops and its watch is recorded; the last clip coming back into view leaves it. Skip and video swipes can reach it (the pager's page count includes it).
- **Music connections (Milestone 43, branch `claude/milestone-43-music-connections`, awaiting review and device testing)**: the foundation for reading a Spotify or Apple Music library into taste. Full guide: `docs/music-integrations.md`. Summary:
  - *Layers*: screens → `MusicConnectionsProvider` / `useMusic()` (`state/music.tsx`, in the root layout) → the `MusicService` interface (`features/music/service.ts`: `availability`, `connect`, `fetchLibrary`, `disconnect`, `MusicServiceError` kinds `expired`/`denied`/`network`/`rate-limited`/`unavailable`/`unknown`) → adapters in `features/music/spotify/` and `features/music/apple/` (nothing outside `features/music/` imports them; architecture test) → normalised `MusicLibrarySnapshot` / `MusicArtistSignal` (`models.ts`) → `combineLibraries` (`library.ts`) → `importedMusic` (`imported.ts`, matched to the catalogue by folded name) → the engine's imported layer. `services.ts` wires the real adapters; tests pass their own.
  - *Taste*: `computeTaste(..., imported)` keeps library signal as its own capped layer and returns it as `imported` (per-artist share). Library strength (`strength.ts`: followed 0.5, saved songs 0.15 per doubling up to 0.45, top rank 0.35 to 0.1, recent 0.2, max 1) × `IMPORT_WEIGHT` 0.6 (below one like); spills to similar artists like a like; unmatched library artists count only through genres that clearly map to a sound (`genres.ts`; "electronic", "dance", "edm" ignored) at half weight, reaching artists ≥ 0.5 alike, at most `IMPORT_SOUND_CAP` 0.3; all imported signal per artist ≤ `IMPORT_CAP` 0.6, halved once you've reacted to or rated that artist. Empty import changes nothing (golden tests unchanged). `useTaste()` adds `importing` (services with a snapshot) and `importedFrom` (artist → services).
  - *Spotify*: see Milestone 44 below. Scopes: `user-library-read`, `user-follow-read`, `user-top-read`. Reads Liked Songs (up to 1000), followed artists, top artists medium and short term. Refreshes before expiry and once on 401; a refused refresh clears the session and reports `expired`; 403/404 on a part is a gap; every part refused is `denied`; only `api.spotify.com` links are followed.
  - *Apple Music*: REST reader (`apple/api.ts`: library songs, library artists with catalog, heavy rotation, recently played) and normaliser; `followed` is always unknown (listed as a gap). MusicKit authorisation is the `AppleMusicAuthorizer` boundary (`apple/musicKit.ts`); this build ships `noMusicKit`, so Apple Music says "Needs the full version of Afterglow" and never pretends to connect. Developer token fetched from `EXPO_PUBLIC_APPLE_MUSIC_TOKEN_URL` (a service the user hosts; no backend here). 401 → `unavailable` (developer token), 403 → `expired` (user token removed).
  - *Storage and privacy*: tokens only in `expo-secure-store` (`features/music/secrets.ts`, this device only, while unlocked; with `features/accounts/vault.ts` (saved account passwords) the only files importing it). AsyncStorage `musicLibrary.v1` holds normalised snapshots and status only (no song titles, album names, playlists or account details). Pure transitions in `store.ts`: a failed sync never replaces the last good snapshot; expired marks disconnected but keeps the import until you disconnect; disconnect removes the service's snapshot and token; a snapshot is stored only under its own provider. A connected library older than `MUSIC_RESYNC_MS` (24h) is re-read on launch. `privacy.ts`: every field classified (`MUSIC_FIELD_PRIVACY`); all library data is private; only `shareableMusicProfile` (Afterglow artist ids/names and top sounds, each part only with explicit consent, nothing by default) may ever feed a future profile.
  - *UI*: Profile has a "Music connections" row opening `music.tsx` (stack push): the explanation, a card per service (Not connected / Reading your library… / Connected · N artists · N on Afterglow · synced … / Couldn't sync… / Sign-in expired… / not available), Connect, Sync now, Try again, Connect again, and "Disconnect & remove imported music data" (confirmed with an alert). Copy is pure in `features/music/copy.ts` (`connectionView`, `musicNote`, `syncedAgo`). My Sound: subtitle starts "Shaped by your Spotify library…", and a glass note (after search and the sparse-festival note) says when a library is syncing, failed or expired, when fewer than `LITTLE_LIBRARY` = 3 of your artists are on Afterglow (newcomers only), or offers "Connect music" to a newcomer when a service is available. Artist pages' why line: "From your Spotify library and 1 like". Start over keeps connections and says so.
  - *Tests*: fixtures with realistic Spotify and Apple Music responses and a fake fetch (`features/music/__tests__/fixtures.ts`); no real accounts. `test/setup.ts` mocks `expo-secure-store` (in-memory, `__items`) and `expo-web-browser` (`openAuthSessionAsync`). `src/app/__tests__/music.test.tsx` mocks `features/music/services` with test doubles.
  - *Needs the user*: a Spotify app (Client ID, redirect URI, test users; dev mode needs the owner on Premium, max 5 users) and a development build; for Apple Music a MusicKit key, a token service and a MusicKit native module in a development build. Expo Go can show the screens and states but can't complete either sign-in.
- **Real Spotify connection (Milestone 44, branch `claude/milestone-44-spotify`, stacked on Milestone 43's branch, awaiting review and a real sign-in on a phone)**. User setup and test checklist: `docs/spotify-setup.md`; internals: `docs/music-integrations.md`.
  - *Sign-in*: `spotify/auth.ts` (pure): PKCE S256 (`makePkce`, 64-char verifier), random `state`, `spotifyAuthRequest`, `readAuthReturn` (a code counts only at the exact redirect address with the same state; `mismatch` otherwise), `redirectUriFrom`. `spotify/signIn.ts` opens it with `expo-web-browser` `openAuthSessionAsync` (`preferUniversalLinks` for an https redirect); `expo-auth-session` was removed. `expo-crypto` supplies random bytes and SHA-256.
  - *Redirect*: `afterglow://music-auth` by default (`SPOTIFY_REDIRECT_URI` in `signIn.ts`); `EXPO_PUBLIC_SPOTIFY_REDIRECT_URI` (`SPOTIFY_REDIRECT_SETTING` in `config/music.ts`) may set an https universal link instead, for when Spotify's dashboard refuses the scheme; `app.config.ts` (a function spreading `app.json`, so EAS can still write `app.json`) then adds `ios.associatedDomains` and an Android App Link. Uncertain as of 2026-09-29: whether new Spotify apps accept custom schemes (documented yes, some reports no).
  - *Expo Go*: Spotify's availability is `needs-full-app` there (`needsOwnApp` from `Constants.executionEnvironment === StoreClient`). Development build: `expo-dev-client` (so a plain `npx expo start` now targets the development build; `npm run go` / `npx expo start --go` for Expo Go, `npm run dev` for the build), `eas.json` profiles `development` (dev client, internal), `preview` (internal), `production`. No bundle identifier is set yet: EAS asks for it on the first build.
  - *State* (`state/music.tsx`): `connecting` (providers whose sheet is open; a second tap returns `cancelled`); `sync` returns the running read's promise to a duplicate request; a generation counter drops a sign-in or read that finishes after a disconnect (and forgets what that sign-in stored); on hydrate, providers with no saved state have their secure-store sign-in forgotten (keychain outlives an uninstall); on launch, connected libraries with no snapshot or older than 24h are re-read. Connect is disabled until hydrated.
  - *Screen* (`music.tsx`, `copy.ts`): `connectionStatus` = unavailable / not-connected / connecting / connected / syncing / synced / reauthorize / failed; `connectionView` gives pill `label`, `headline` ("Connected to Spotify", "Spotify needs you to sign in again", "Couldn't sync your library"), `detail`, `stats` (Last synced "12 minutes ago", artists analyzed, on upcoming festivals via `features/music/reach.ts`: festivals not over or undated), `notes` (empty library, nothing on Afterglow, parts not shared), `primary` (Connect Spotify / Sync now / Try again / Sign in again). `connectProblem` says once why a sign-in didn't finish (cancel says nothing). Disconnect's alert mentions spotify.com/account/apps.
  - *Taste corrections*: `startingPrior(MOCK_TASTE, imported)` (engine.ts, used by `useTaste`): the built-in starting guess steps aside once a library names ≥ `PRIOR_YIELDS_AT` = 3 Afterglow artists (`LITTLE_LIBRARY` = the same). Library spill (similar library artists + unmatched sounds) ≤ `IMPORT_SPILL_CAP` = 0.3 and combined as `own + spill × (1 − own/IMPORT_CAP)`, so sounding alike never ties with being in the library. Weights, `IMPORT_CAP` and own-share unchanged; golden snapshots unchanged. Artist pages lifted only by spill say "Close to artists in your Spotify library" (`whyLine(..., nearLibrary)`).
  - *Tests*: `spotifyAuth.test.ts` (RFC 7636 vector, state/redirect checks, the sheet with mocked `expo-crypto`), `spotify.test.ts` (Expo Go, failed exchange, network, expired), `reach.test.ts`, `src/state/__tests__/music.test.tsx` (provider races), `features/taste/__tests__/coldStart.test.ts` (a messy Spotify library end to end against `BUILT_IN.catalog`), and the screen tests for every state.
- **Memories, quick upload and social profiles (Milestone 45, branch `claude/milestone-45-memories-social`, stacked on Milestone 44's branch, awaiting review and phone testing)**. Guides: `docs/memories-and-profiles.md` (internals, privacy, rules), `docs/firebase-setup.md` (the user's Firebase setup and test steps).
  - *Upload*: press and hold around a clip in Discover (`Gesture.LongPress`, `HOLD_MS` 450, raced against the taps so a tap never waits; `withTestId('hold-<clip>')`; not on the YouTube player itself, which takes its own touches) pauses the clip and opens Record / Choose (`features/memories/useAddMemory.ts`; ActionSheetIOS, Alert on Android). A third first-time tip, `hold` ("Hold to add yours", plus glyph), done on first use or for anyone with a memory. `device.ts`: `pickVideo` (expo-image-picker, `MAX_CLIP_SECONDS` 60 since 2026-10-01 (the user wants 30 s to 1 min clips, fit for Discover; was 180), iPhone trimming), the moment from the new expo-media-library `Asset` API (`getCreationTime`, `getLocation`; the old top-level functions throw in SDK 57) or now + here (expo-location, foreground only), coordinates reverse-geocoded to city/region and dropped; `deviceMemoryFiles` keeps files in `<documents>/memories` by name (expo-file-system `File`/`Directory`/`Paths`), stills via expo-video-thumbnails, Save to Photos via `Asset.create`.
  - *Guess* (`features/memories/guess.ts`, pure): `nightOf` (festivalNight, else weekday name), `guessFestivals` (date +4, city +2, region +1, going +1; place alone off-dates ignored), `festivalConfidence`, `guessArtists` (sets on at that minute, your full-day route first; `GRACE_MINUTES` 10; before set times that night's lineup; the whole lineup after), `artistConfidence`, `reasonLine` ("On your plan · Main Stage", "Bass Tent until 1:30 AM", "Playing Saturday").
  - *Tagging* (`memory-tag.tsx`, modal, no swipe-down; `?id=` retags): video preview (expo-video), "Sat, Dec 5 · 10:30 PM · Boca Raton" (`momentLine`), festival chips (guesses first, then all, then Not at a festival), night chips, the top 3 artist guesses as rows ("Probably" on a confident one), Someone else (search) and Not sure. Cancelling a fresh recording asks first. Steps back once (a `leaving` ref; saving clears the draft).
  - *Memories* (`state/memories.tsx`, AsyncStorage `memories.v1`, names only; orphan files tidied on launch only when the saved list read back whole): `memories.tsx` (Profile → Memories; festival → night → set via `organize.ts`, untagged first with a Tag badge, `MemoryTile` 9:16 stills with length and a glow dot when shared, + to add), `memory/[id].tsx` (full-screen modal, tap to pause; Tag, Save to Photos, Send via expo-sharing, Share to profile, Delete), artist pages' "Your memories".
  - *Profiles* (Firebase JS SDK: Auth email/password with AsyncStorage persistence, Firestore, Storage; `config/social.ts` `FIREBASE_CONFIG` from `EXPO_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_*`, empty = not set up). `SocialService` boundary (`features/social/service.ts`, `SocialError` kinds with plain copy), adapter `features/social/firebase/service.ts` (only place importing firebase; `getReactNativePersistence` read through a cast because its types are missing, firebase-js-sdk issue 9316), `services.ts` wiring, `__tests__/fakeSocial.ts` in-memory double (test/setup.ts uses it, not set up, for every screen test). `state/social.tsx` (`SocialProvider`, inside Memories and the other providers): user, profile, `saveProfile` (published festivals = going, sounds = top 3 of `tasteMix`, only when switched on), auto-republish 3 s after they change (`publishedChanged`), `share`/`unshare` with progress (`memory.sharedId`/`sharedBy`), blocks, report, `deleteAccount` (then `forgetShares`); late answers after an account switch are dropped. Screens: Profile tab header shows your photo/name/@handle (Edit profile, Find people) or Create/Finish your profile; `account.tsx` (modal: New here / Sign in, community guidelines checkbox required, forgot password), `profile-edit.tsx` (photo after first save, name, @handle with live availability, bio 160, Show festivals I'm going to, Show my sound, Sign out, Delete account with password), `u/[handle].tsx` (public profile, shared memories grid, share link `afterglow://u/<handle>`, ••• Report / Block), `shared/[id].tsx` (player; Report, or Remove from profile if yours), `people.tsx` (search by name or handle prefix, blocked hidden). `validate.ts`: handles 3 to 20 `[a-z0-9._]`, reserved words, `obscenity` for handles/names/bios. Share confirmation says Afterglow doesn't add where you filmed it but files may carry location.
  - *Rules*: `firestore.rules`, `storage.rules`, `firestore.indexes.json` (memories by uid, capturedAt desc; by festivalId, createdAt desc), `firebase.json`; `scripts/firebase-rules.test.mjs` (+ `scripts/firebase-rules.json`) runs 44 checks against the emulator (Java is available in cloud sessions; install firebase-tools and @firebase/rules-unit-testing in a scratch folder), all passing. `scripts/` is ignored by ESLint.

Screen tests share `src/app/__tests__/appRoutes.tsx` (`renderApp`, every route). `test/setup.ts` mocks expo-file-system (in-memory `__files`), expo-image-picker, expo-location, expo-media-library, expo-video-thumbnails, expo-video and the social services.

- **Community clips in Discover (Milestone 46, merged in PR #47, awaiting phone testing)**. Guide: "Community clips in Discover" in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`.
  - *Data*: `ClipSource` is now `YouTubeClipSource | CommunityClipSource` (`provider: 'community'`, videoUrl, thumbUrl, handle, uid, sharedId, capturedAt, vertical); catalogue files still accept only YouTube. `SharedMemory`/`ShareInput` carry optional `width`/`height` (rules: numbers ≤ 20000). `SocialService` adds `festivalMemories(festivalId, max)` and `recentMemories(max)` (Firebase: `where festivalId ==` + `orderBy createdAt desc`, new index).
  - *Pure* (`features/community/clips.ts`): `communityClip` (needs a known artist, a festival, https video, handle; title "From the crowd"; length clamped to 180, default 30; upright unless the size says landscape), `communityClips` (newest first, deduped, no blocked people, hidden videos or your own; for a festival only its videos of lineup artists; at most `COMMUNITY_MAX` 40), `rememberedClips`, `rememberArtist` (bounded `ARTIST_MEMORY_MAX` 500). Ids `community:<sharedId>`.
  - *State* (`state/community.tsx`, `CommunityProvider` inside Reactions, above Social): AsyncStorage `community.v1` = `{ enabled (default on), hidden, artistOf }`; `useCommunityClips(festival | 'mix')` fetches per focus (fresh 5 min, again on foreground, one at a time, failures keep what was there), only when profiles are set up, you're signed in and it's on. `useTaste` adds stand-in clips for community clips you reacted to that aren't loaded, so reactions keep counting (`useCommunityArtists`, optional context).
  - *Playback*: `media.ts` is a union (`YouTubeMedia` / `VideoMedia` with `community`); `ClipPlayer` picks `YouTubePlayer` or the new `NativeVideoPlayer` (expo-video: loops, `timeUpdate` every 0.25 s feeding `playedSpan`/`canCountWatch`, restarts with `replay()` when it comes on screen, 15 s load timeout → fail). The expo-video mock in `test/setup.ts` has `addListener` and `replay`.
  - *Feed*: `useRankedFeed(activeIndex, focus, community)`; keeping the clip on screen and the next is tied to the focus, so late community clips join from the third clip on without moving you.
  - *Card*: "Filmed by @handle" (opens `/u/<handle>`, excuses the watch), ••• menu (`features/community/menu.ts`: Report this video → reasons → hidden at once; Hide this video; Block @handle, confirmed); no Save (ActionRail's `onSave` is optional) and no share link; a failed video says "This video isn't available right now." Community clips are remembered for taste when on screen or reacted to. `useTaste().tasteClips` (catalogue clips plus those stand-ins) feeds the artist page's why line, so a like on a shared video reads "From your 1 like"; a liked community clip is kept whole (`community.v1` `kept`, `keepClip`, bounded `KEPT_MAX` 200, read back through `cleanKept`), and `useClipPool()` (catalogue clips plus kept ones while signed in, minus blocked people and hidden videos, via `keptClips`) feeds Your clips and the counts on Profile and My Sound; its rows show the video's still and "From the crowd · @handle". Opening one goes back to Discover with `router.dismissTo` (it used to stack a second tab navigator with `navigate`); Discover finds it through `useClipPool().find`, ranks it in with the first feed, and keeps it in the feed for that focus while you watch.

Rules: create only as yourself with the id `me_to`, not yourself, both profiles exist, not if `to` blocked you; never updated; deleted by either side. Indexes: follows (from, createdAt desc), (to, createdAt desc), memories (uid, createdAt desc). Account deletion removes follows both ways. Emulator: 38 checks pass. `fakeSocial` mirrors it (`follows` map).

Copy: `followersLabel`, `followingLabel` in `social/copy.ts`.

Header "Following" / "Videos from people you follow"; `FollowingEmpty` (signed out / no one yet / nothing new, Find people or Watch your mix); `FeedEnd` takes `following`. What to discover lists Following under Your mix.

  - *Backend*: Firestore `likes/{memoryId}_{uid}` (`memoryId`, `owner`, `uid`, `createdAt`). `SocialService` adds `like(memoryId, owner)`, `unlike`, `likeCount`, `likedBy(memoryId, max)`, `activity(max)` (`Activity` in `social/models.ts`: follows to you and likes on your videos, newest first). Rules: create as yourself with id `memoryId_me`, the memory exists and `owner` is its uid, not your own, not if the owner blocked you; deleted by liker or owner. Indexes: likes (memoryId, createdAt desc), (owner, createdAt desc). Unsharing removes a video's likes; account deletion removes likes both ways. Emulator: 44 checks pass. `fakeSocial` has `likes`.
  - *One like*: a like on `community:<sharedId>` (Discover, Your clips, or the heart on `shared/[id]`, which also remembers the owner and, for a known artist, keeps the clip) is the public like. `community.v1` adds `owners` (clip → sharer, bounded `OWNERS_MAX` 500, set by `remember`/`rememberOwner`) and `sent` (account → clip ids); the provider exposes `hydrated`. `features/community/likes.ts` (pure: `wantedLikes`, `likeChanges`, `sharedIdOf`, `withOwner`); `LikeSync` (root layout, renders nothing) sends or takes back one change at a time once signed in with a profile, skipping failures until next launch. Without a profile likes stay private (the shared video says so). Likes are per phone, not per account; Start over takes public likes back.
  - *UI*: `shared/[id].tsx` has a heart with the count for others' videos (adjusted for your own like) and, on yours, "N likes" opening `likes/[id].tsx` (modal: Liked by, PersonRows with Follow, blocked hidden). `likesLabel` in `social/copy.ts`.
  - *Bell*: `Notice` now has `href` (festival notices link to the festival) and kinds `follow` / `like`; `features/notifications/activity.ts` (pure `activityNotices`: follow notices, likes grouped per video "@a and 2 others liked your video of HEDEX", blocked and deleted accounts left out, newest first). `NoticesProvider` moved inside `SocialProvider`; reads `activity(30)` plus profiles and your shared videos on sign-in, foreground and `refresh()` (the notifications screen calls it); the list is festival notices then activity. No push notifications.

- **Web Beta (Milestone 49, merged in PR #47; the app icon merged in PR #48; LIVE at https://afterglowmusic.web.app since 2026-09-30 (PR #51 fixed the deploy: `.firebaserc` names `afterglowmusic`, the workflow falls back to it and reads the Firebase values as variables or secrets; `workflow_dispatch` republishes main). afterglowmusic.app: Cloudflare DNS has A `@` 199.36.158.100 (DNS only, not proxied) and TXT `hosting-site=afterglowmusic`; Firebase Hosting shows it Connected since 2026-10-01 (the cloud session's proxy can't reach it to check). Still to do by the user: add it to Firebase Auth's authorized domains. The Firebase web settings are now built into `src/config/social.ts` (project `afterglowmusic`; environment variables still win when set), so the website build has accounts without repository variables)**. Guide for the user: `docs/web-deploy.md` (Firebase Hosting, GitHub secrets, afterglowmusic.app). The same app runs in a browser (`react-dom`, `react-native-web`; `npm run build:web` → `dist/`).
  - *Starting on web*: `metro.config.js` points `obscenity` at its CommonJS build on web (its ES module wrapper crashed the web bundle). `features/memories/device.web.ts` replaces the phone layer (no expo-media-library / file system on web): `pickVideo` returns `app-only` and `useAddMemory` says adding videos is in the iPhone app; no files. The Firebase adapter uses `getAuth` (browser persistence) on web, React Native persistence on phones. Spotify already reports `not-on-this-device` without a secure store; reminders render nothing on web.
  - *Dialogs*: React Native's `Alert` does nothing in a browser, so every alert and action sheet goes through `lib/dialog.ts`: `showAlert` (same arguments as Alert.alert; on phones it calls Alert.alert with exactly those arguments) and `showActionSheet` (ActionSheetIOS on iPhone, Alert on Android, the app's sheet on web). `components/DialogHost.tsx` (root layout, last) shows web dialogs one at a time as an overlay (glass card, Cancel on the left of two buttons, last in lists; backdrop and Escape cancel). Always use these, never Alert/ActionSheetIOS directly.
  - *Sharing*: `lib/share.ts` `shareMessage(message, title?)` → `shared` / `copied` / `dismissed` / `failed`: the share sheet on phones; on web `navigator.share`, else copied to the clipboard with a "Copied" dialog. Used by clip, profile, plan-text and request sharing. Profile links are now `https://afterglowmusic.app/u/<handle>` (`WEB_URL` in `config/app.ts`). The plan card hides "Share image" on web (capturing needs the phone) and keeps a phone width (at most 420).
  - *YouTube on web*: `features/discover/YouTubePlayer.web.tsx` hosts the same `playerHtml` page in a same-origin `srcdoc` iframe, with a shim for `window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage` (messages to the parent, filtered by frame), commands through `contentWindow.afterglow`, and the same playback/watch rules (`shouldPlay`, `playedSpan`, `readPlayerReport`). If a play hasn't started after `AUTOPLAY_GRACE_MS` 1.5 s (browsers need a tap before sound), it mutes and plays. Not verifiable from the cloud session (its proxy blocks YouTube): test on a real browser.
  - *Layout*: Discover is one column at most `FEED_MAX_WIDTH` 520 wide, centred (unchanged on phones); the tab bar is `width: 100%` up to 480, centred; GlassCircleButton and NoticeBell put their icon in its own View (the positioned blur painted over it in browsers). The hold tip isn't shown on web.
  - *Version*: `lib/version.ts` `versionLine` / `VERSION_LINE` ("Afterglow 1.0.0 · build 3f2a9c1", from `expoConfig.version` and `EXPO_PUBLIC_BUILD_ID`) at the bottom of Profile. The Profile "Coming soon" card (Festival crews) was replaced by your crews in Milestone 52.
  - *Deploy*: `firebase.json` `hosting` (public `dist`, every path → `/index.html`, `/_expo/static/**` and `/assets/**` cached a year, HTML `no-cache`). `.github/workflows/website.yml`: on pull requests and pushes to main: `npm ci`, tsc, `jest --ci`, `npm run build:web` (Firebase config from repository variables, `EXPO_PUBLIC_BUILD_ID` = the commit), then `FirebaseExtended/action-hosting-deploy` (live from main, a 7-day preview per PR) only when the `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` secret exists.
  - *Checked in a real browser* (Chromium via Playwright against the exported site, in the scratchpad, not committed): every tab and page renders with no errors at phone and desktop sizes, mouse-wheel paging, browser back/forward, the web dialog (Escape cancels, confirm works), the version line.
  - *Tests*: `src/lib/__tests__/web.test.tsx` (dialogs on phone and web, DialogHost, sharing fallback, version line, web Memories). With @testing-library/react-native 14 `unmount()` is async too: await it.
- **Set reminders (Milestone 50, merged in PR #49, awaiting phone testing)**: phone notifications before your sets, from the full-day route.
  - *Pure* (`features/notifications/setReminders.ts`): `dayStarts(festival)` (local midnight per festival day; only when `startDate`/`endDate` span exactly `days.length` dates, like the live view), `canRemindSets` (set times out + days that line up), `setReminders(festival, route, now)`: one reminder per route stop where you move (the first set of a night, after a break, or a different stage from your last set), `SET_LEAD_MIN` 10 min before you need to leave (the stop's `walk`, or `WALK_MINUTES` after a break on another stage), future only; title "★ ILLENIUM at 11:30 PM" (★ for must-sees), body "Head to Main Stage in 10 min (a 10 min walk)." or "Be at Bass Tent in 10 min."; ids `SET_PREFIX` (inside `REMINDER_PREFIX`, so `reminderChanges` tidies them with the festival reminders) + festival, day, artist and leave minute, so a moved set replaces its reminder. `soonest` keeps at most `SET_REMINDERS_MAX` 40 (iPhone holds 64 per app).
  - *State*: `state/setReminders.tsx` (`SetRemindersProvider` inside Plan; AsyncStorage `setReminders.v1`, festival ids; planning only; `changed` counts switches you flip).
  - *Sync*: `ReminderSync` computes `festivalRoute` for festivals switched on (your weekend, live taste and must-sees) and schedules festival + set reminders together. Opening the app and your own changes (going, the switch) sync at once and may ask for permission; a route that moved because of likes or stars resyncs 600 ms later and never asks.
  - *UI*: `features/plan/SetReminderToggle.tsx` ("Remind me before each set", a switch) above the Full day route in `Timetable`, only when `canRemindSets` and not on web. No festival in the catalogue has both dates and set times yet (the demo Solstice has no dates), so it appears once real set times arrive.
  - *Tests*: `features/notifications/__tests__/setReminders.test.ts`, `features/plan/__tests__/setReminderToggle.test.tsx`.

  - `useGoers(festivalId)` (`features/social/useGoers.ts`: up to `GOERS_MAX` 200, cached per festival for 5 min at module level, `forgetGoers` for tests; undefined when signed out or not set up). `FriendsGoing` (under the festival page's pills: up to 4 faces, the line, opens `going/[id].tsx`: People you follow, then Also going, PersonRows with Follow, and a note that only people who show their festivals appear). Nothing shows when no one else is going.

- **Festival crews (Milestone 52, merged in PR #49, awaiting phone testing)**: planning a festival with friends. Guide: "Festival crews" in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`.
  - *Backend*: Firestore `crews/{id}` (`festivalId`, `name`, `owner`, `members` up to `CREW_MAX` 12, `createdAt`; the id is the invite) and `crews/{id}/picks/{uid}` (`artists` up to 100, `updatedAt`). `SocialService` adds `createCrew`, `crew`, `myCrews` (members array-contains you, no new index), `joinCrew` (transaction; `crew-full`), `leaveCrew` (takes your picks; an owner hands it to the next member; the last one out deletes picks and crew), `renameCrew`, `removeFromCrew` (owner; their picks go), `setCrewPicks`, `crewPicks`; error kinds `crew-full`, `not-found`. Rules: get by anyone signed in, list only your crews; create as sole member with a profile; join adds only yourself (profile, ≤ 12, not if the owner blocked you); leave removes only yourself; the owner renames, removes people, deletes; picks read by members, written by that member. Account deletion leaves every crew. Emulator: 53 checks pass. `fakeSocial` mirrors it (`crews`, `picks`). `mapping.ts` `crewFromDoc`, `crewPicksFromDoc`; `validate.ts` `cleanCrewName`, `crewNameProblem` (1 to 40, friendly).
  - *Pure* (`features/crews/together.ts`): `crewTogether(picks, members, lineup)` (shared = starred by 2+, most of you then billing order; per member, ignoring people who left and artists off the lineup), `whoLine` ("Both of you", "All 4 of you", "Maya and you"), `crewMembersLine`, `sharedCountLine`, `firstName`, `crewPath`, `picksChanged`.
  - *State* (`state/crews.tsx`, `CrewsProvider` inside Notices): your crews (loaded on sign-in and foreground), create/join/leave/rename/remove, and your stars at each crew's festival sent `CREW_SYNC_MS` 2 s after they settle, only when changed (remembered per session; a failed send retries on the next change). Stars are still never taste.
  - *UI*: `features/crews/CrewCard.tsx` under who's going on festival pages ("Plan with friends", or "Your crew" rows; signed out opens sign-in; no profile offers to make one; only when profiles are set up). `crew/[id].tsx` (stack push; `useCrewPage`): signed out asks to sign in; ended crew; invite view (faces, "Join the crew", what joining shares, full); member view (festival link, name with Rename for the owner, opens ready to rename when just started via `fresh=1`, Invite friends via `shareMessage` of `https://afterglowmusic.app/crew/<id>`, Sets you share as `ArtistRow`s with the who line, Everyone's picks with your live stars, Remove for the owner, Leave / End this crew with a confirm). Profile lists your crews (the "Coming soon" card is gone when profiles are set up; it still says Friends when they aren't).

- **Liquid Glass, step 1: the floating layer (2026-10-07; the user: "a bit of Liquid Glass in the overall UI of the app across all pages and themes")**: `components/LiquidGlass.tsx` `GlassFill` is the back layer of anything that floats (its parent clips it to its `radius`). On an iPhone with iOS 26 (`nativeGlass()`: `expo-glass-effect`'s `isLiquidGlassAvailable` and `isGlassEffectAPIAvailable`, in Expo Go, no config plugin) it is Apple's `GlassView` (`regular`, dark, tinted with the theme's `glowDeepA(0.12)`, or `glowA(0.42)` when `lit`; `pointerEvents="none"` and not `isInteractive`, so it can never take a press from its button). Elsewhere (older iPhones, Android, the website) a copy: `BlurView` 40 dark, a night tint (`raisedA(tint)`, 0.4 by default), a white sheen from the top and the theme's glow pooling at the bottom (`LinearGradient`), and a rim brighter on top and glow-tinted underneath. With Reduce transparency on (followed live), a solid `raisedA(0.96)` surface. Used by `GlassCircleButton` (so every page's Back/Share and Discover's header buttons; `selected` → `lit`), `NoticeBell`, `QuestButton` and the `GlassTabBar` pill. Test mock `expo-glass-effect` in `test/setup.ts` (`__state.available`); `components/__tests__/liquidGlass.test.tsx`.

Engine: `RATING_WEIGHT` love 1.3, like 0.7, ok 0, meh -0.4, pass -0.8 for spill-over; `RATED_RANGE` keeps the artist itself in [0.8, 1] / [0.7, 1] / [0.4, 0.7] / [0, 0.4] / [0, 0.3] (`RATED` love/pass unchanged); any rating makes the artist informed. Feed: `RATING_NET` love 1, like 0.6, ok 0, meh -0.5, pass -1; meh and pass count as turning down. Bell lineup news and Tune's "Sounds like" treat love and like as favourites and never meh/pass. UI: `RateArtist` (artist pages, map sheet) and Tune both lay out Love them + Like them / They're OK / Not really + Not for me (`ratingA11y`: "Love X's sound" and "X is not for me" labels kept, "Like X", "X is OK", "X is not really for me"; tapping your answer again on RateArtist clears it); Tune slides warm answers right, cold left, OK fades; its finish line says "You love 2 and like 5 artists here." `ArtistRow`: love = glowing heart, like = outline heart, meh adds "Not really for you", pass dims + "Not for you". - **Festivals from EDMTrain (Milestone 63, branch `claude/edmtrain`, awaiting review, the user's EDMTrain key and a first run)**: the user chose EDMTrain, fully automatic. Guide: `docs/events-import.md`. `src/data/import/edmtrain.ts` (pure, tested): `edmtrainCatalogue(events, curated, today, rules)` groups EDMTrain's per-day festival events by folded name + venue, consecutive days into runs and runs within 21 days into one edition (`weekends` for 2+ runs), keeps each act's weekdays (`days`) and single weekend (`weekend`), skips festivals that are over or more than 365 days ahead, with under 5 acts (`IMPORT_RULES`), or matching a curated festival (overlapping dates and the same city, or a similar name), reuses artist ids by folded name (new artists with empty `sound`), ids `<slug>-<year>`, soonest 150. `Festival.source` (`{ name, url }`, catalogue key `source`, https) credits it: "Event info from EDMTrain ↗" at the bottom of the festival page. `scripts/import-edmtrain.ts` (run with `npx --yes tsx@4`, reads `EDMTRAIN_API_KEY`, never prints it) fetches `edmtrain.com/api/events?festivalInd=true&startDate&endDate&client=`, curates against `BUILT_IN.snapshot`, checks the file with `buildCatalog` exactly as the app would, refuses empty or problematic output, shrinks to fit 90% of `MAX_FILE_CHARS`, writes `public/catalogue.json` (committed empty). `website.yml` runs it nightly (`cron 17 9 * * *`) and on pushes to main when the secret exists (not on PRs), falling back to the live copy on failure; `firebase.json` serves `/catalogue.json` with a 5-minute cache and CORS. `CATALOG_URL` = `https://afterglowmusic.web.app/catalogue.json` (tests mock it empty in `test/setup.ts`). EDMTrain's answer format couldn't be checked from the cloud session: the first run is its test.

With many festivals: the Festivals tab shows Going, the shelf, then "More festivals · N" (collapsed, soonest first under month headings via `byMonth`: "November", "March 2027", "Date to come", "Finished"; 20 at a time with "Show more · N left"); the Lineup tab's chips (nothing going) are the shelf; the welcome's "Where are you going?" and My Sound's festival picker get a search field ("Find your festival" / "Find a festival") over the shelf; memory tagging offers festivals around the filming date plus "Another festival…" (a search). Lineup news: more than `NEW_FESTIVALS_GROUP_AT` 3 new festivals with acts you'd love arriving together become one notice ("12 new festivals with artists you'd love", opens Festivals, id `lineup:new:<at>`); `NEWS_MAX` is now 200.
