Pure `data/import/youtube.ts`: `searchUrl` ("<name> live set", embeddable, 10 results), `detailsUrl` (videos.list snippet, contentDetails, status), `searchIds`, `isoSeconds`, `pickLiveSet` (title names the artist (folded) and has set words, not `NOT_A_SET` words, ≥ `MIN_SET_MINUTES` 10 and ≤ 6 h, public, embeddable, not live; score +4 artist's own channel, +3 `KNOWN_CHANNELS`, +1 "full set", +1 30 to 150 min; ties keep search order), `clipTitle` (video title minus the artist's name, capitalised, "Live set" if nothing's left, ≤ 60), `setClip` (catalogue clip `yt-<videoId>`, one minute (`CLIP_SECONDS`) a third of the way in, channel credited), `clipPlan` (artists without a clip, skipping misses younger than `RETRY_MISSES_DAYS` 30, at most `MAX_SEARCHES` 90: search is 100 of the 10,000 daily units). `scripts/youtube-clips.ts [published.json]` (website.yml "YouTube clips" step after photos, non-PR runs with the `YOUTUBE_API_KEY` secret): keeps published `yt-` clips (their festival while it's listed, even once over, else the artist's soonest upcoming, else any), searches curated festivals first then EDMTrain ones, soonest first, billing order; stops on 403 (quota) or 5 failures; writes `clips` and `clipMisses` (artist -> day, ignored by the app) into `public/catalogue.json` only if `buildCatalog` reads it with no problems. The Google API isn't reachable without a key from the cloud session: tested with fixtures and a fake server (two nights: 90 found, then kept and 90 more). Guide and key steps: "Discover clips from YouTube" in `docs/events-import.md`. Tests: `data/import/__tests__/youtube.test.ts`.

Pure `data/import/colors.ts`: `dominantColor(rgba, w, h)` (24 hue bins, skipping dark, light and grey pixels, weighted by saturation and mid lightness; undefined below 4% of the photo's worth) and `smallPhotoUrl` (Deezer 56×56). `scripts/artist-colors.ts [published.json]` (website.yml "Artist colours" step after photos, non-PR; installs `jpeg-js@0.4.4` with `npm install --no-save`, imported by a variable name so it's not an app dependency; keeps last night's colour while the photo is unchanged; up to 600 new a night) writes `colors`. Artist pages with a photo show it blurred (`blurRadius` 45) behind the top of the page under a night gradient that covers the whole page (no edge against the sky), and the hero halo takes their bright tone. - **Crew updates (Milestone 105, branch `claude/crew-updates`, stacked on `claude/artist-page`, awaiting review, a rules deploy and phone testing; the user's idea, decisions on 2026-10-01: videos crew-only and public both count, quick check-ins too, a stories row on Discover, crew updates before the wider social overhaul)**: where your crew is, from videos and check-ins, with honest times. Guide: "Crew updates" in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`.
  - *Times*: `CrewUpdate.at` = when it happened on the phone (filming time, or the tap); `sentAt` = when it reached the crew (server time; the rules require `request.time`). The crew sees `at`; `at` decides the newest (a 4:05 PM video beats a 3:00 PM one that only got through at 5:00 PM); arriving `LATE_AFTER_MS` 10 min or more after says "Sent 3:00 PM, reached the crew 5:02 PM".
  - *Backend*: Firestore `crews/{id}/updates/{updateId}` (uid, kind video/checkin, atMs, sentAt, festivalId (must be the crew's), artistId, stage ≤ 60, note ≤ 80, videoUrl/thumbUrl https, videoPath/thumbPath, sharedId, durationSec, width, height); members read; create as yourself while a member, `atMs` within the last week and not in the future (+5 min), never updated, deleted by author or owner. Storage `crews/{crewId}/{uid}/{file}` (your own folder only; links only in the members-only updates). `SocialService.postCrewUpdate` (idempotent by id: an existing doc is returned), `crewUpdates(crewId, sinceMs, max)`, `deleteCrewUpdate`; leaving or being removed takes your updates; the crew ending removes all. `crewUpdateFromDoc` in `mapping.ts`. `fakeSocial` has `updates` and `network.down`. Emulator: 57 checks pass (the storage emulator couldn't read Firestore for a members-only storage rule, so the verifiable own-folder rule is used). **The user must redeploy rules** (`docs/firebase-setup.md` step 5).
  - *Pure* (`features/crews/updates.ts`): `clock`, `ago`, `byHappened`, `crewStories` (one per crewmate, never you/blocked/left, the last `UPDATES_WINDOW_MS` 36 h, same update in two crews once, fresh first), `updateLine` ("Updated 4:05 PM · Main Stage · SLANDER"), `lateLine`, `lastUpdateLine`, `stageOf` (from set times), `CHECK_INS`, `cleanNote`, outbox (`OutboxItem`, `updateId`, `enqueue` (ordered by `at`, max `OUTBOX_MAX` 30), `delivered` (per crew), `failed`, `retryDelay` 15 s doubling to 2 min, `cleanOutbox` (drops after `OUTBOX_KEEP_MS` 24 h), `outboxLine`), `markSeen`, `liveCrews` (festival not over, the morning after counts; undated counts), `latestPerMember`.
  - *State* (`state/crewUpdates.tsx`, `CrewUpdatesProvider` inside Crews; `useCrewUpdates()` falls back to off): outbox `crewOutbox.v1`, seen `crewSeen.v1`; sends oldest first whenever there's a chance (items change, network back via `expo-network`, a Discover share finishing, foreground, retry timer); a video also being shared to Discover waits for that upload and points at it; reads live crews every `POLL_MS` 60 s while open and on foreground; crewmates' profiles once each. `checkIn(festivalId, { note, stage, artistId })`, `sendVideo(memory)` (at = `capturedAt`), `markSeen`, `remove`, `stories`, `updatesFor`, `crewCount`, `crewFestivals`.
  - *UI*: `features/crews/CrewStories.tsx` (`STORIES_H` 92) under Discover's header whenever you're in a live crew: you first (Check in, a dashed amber ring and "Waiting…" while the outbox has something), then crewmates (gradient ring when new, a green dot for check-ins, first name and the time it happened), or a line saying what will show; `ClipCard` takes `topExtra` so videos stay clear of it. `check-in.tsx` (form sheet): festival chips when several, "Watching <artist> · <stage>" from the set on now, stage chips, quick check-ins (one tap sends), your own words; says "Sent to your crew" or "Saved for when there's signal … still marked 5:30 PM". `crew-story.tsx` (full screen): progress bars, avatar, "Updated … · place", "2 h ago", the late line in amber, video or a check-in card, tap left/right. `memory-tag.tsx` "Your crew · Send to my crew" switch (`send-to-crew`; on for new videos). Crew page "Latest from the crew" (`CrewLatest`: everyone's latest incl. you, late lines, the outbox line, Check in). `components/PersonAvatar`.
  - *Tests*: `features/crews/__tests__/updates.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/crewUpdates.test.tsx` (stories, late video, offline check-in sent an hour later still marked when tapped, crew page, outsiders refused).

- **Replies to crew updates (Milestone 112, branch `claude/crew-replies`, awaiting review, a rules deploy and phone testing; the loose end of option 5, built on the user's overnight go-ahead)**: a reply is a crew update of kind `reply` (`replyTo` = the update it answers, words in `note`), so it goes through the same outbox: it waits for signal and keeps the time you sent it. Rules: kind `reply` needs words, a `replyTo` that exists in the same crew, and no video; `replyTo` only on replies. Emulator: 62 checks pass. Pure in `features/crews/updates.ts`: `isReply`, `QUICK_REPLIES` (On my way / Be there in 10 / Save me a spot / Where exactly? / Can’t make it), `repliesTo` (oldest first, blocked left out), `repliesLine` ("2 replies · Sam: Be there in 10"), `answered`; `crewStories` counts a reply only when it answers one of yours (it joins the replier's story: "Replied 4:12 PM", a card "Replying to your 4:05 PM update, “Meet at our spot”"), and `latestPerMember` ignores replies (they say nothing of where someone is). `useCrewUpdates()` adds `reply(update, words)` and `repliesFor(update)` (sent, then yours still waiting). `features/crews/ReplyBar.tsx` at the bottom of `crew-story.tsx`: the last three replies as bubbles with their times ("· waiting for signal" in amber), quick reply chips and your own words; on a reply to you it answers your own update ("Write back"). The crew page's "Latest from the crew" adds "1 reply · Sam: On my way" under yours. Tests: replies in `features/crews/__tests__/updates.test.ts`, the reply case in `src/app/__tests__/crewUpdates.test.tsx`.
- **Where's the crew (Milestone 113, branch `claude/crew-replies`, awaiting phone testing; option 3 of the user's list)**: Tonight's "Your crew" section starts with a "Where’s the crew" card: everyone in your crews at that festival (you included, once each, never blocked people) grouped by the stage named in their newest video or check-in from the last day and a half (replies don't count), stages in the festival's order then any other names, most recent first; "Somewhere else" for updates with no stage; "No update yet from Jo."; each person a chip with their face and the time it happened ("Maya 9:40 PM"), dimmed after `STALE_AFTER_MS` 90 min ("may have moved" for screen readers), opening their story. It says "From their latest videos and check-ins, not live location." Nothing is tracked: only what people posted. Pure `features/crews/where.ts` (`crewWhere`, `whereLine`); `features/crews/CrewWhereCard.tsx`. Tests: `features/crews/__tests__/where.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/crewWhere.test.tsx`.
- **App Store readiness (Milestone 114, branch `claude/crew-replies`; option 9 of the user's list)**: `docs/app-store.md`, a plain checklist from what the code does: Apple Developer account, a suggested bundle id `app.afterglowmusic` (not set: EAS asks on the first build), a production build to TestFlight (`eas build --profile production`, `eas submit --latest`), the listing (iPad screenshots are required while `supportsTablet` is true), the App Privacy answers (email, profile, shared videos, comments, crew updates and user id collected and linked; location, music library, Look after yourself, analytics and ads not collected; no tracking), the age rating, a reviewer account, what guideline 1.2 / 5.1.1 / 4.8 need and that reports in Firestore must be read, Spotify's 5-user limit, legal and content gaps. `app.json` now declares `ios.config.usesNonExemptEncryption: false` (HTTPS only), so uploads skip the export question.
- **Overnight polish (branch `claude/polish`, stacked on `claude/crew-replies`, awaiting review)**: fixes from a review pass. The crew outbox lets go of an update the server keeps refusing (`REFUSED_TRIES` 3; a reply to a deleted update) and carries on with the rest, instead of holding everything after it for a day; only no signal stops the queue. Your own check-ins still waiting for signal place you on Where's the crew. On your own video's comments you can delete someone's comment and also report or block them. Tonight opens by itself only over one of the five tabs (`TABS` in `TonightLauncher.tsx`), so a night that begins while you're filming or tagging waits. Water reminders before any water tonight keep a steady beat from noon. Tests in `crewUpdates.test.tsx`, `comments.test.tsx`, `tonight.test.tsx`, `careReminders.test.ts`. (PR #79 was merged into `claude/crew-replies` after that branch had gone to main, so these fixes reach main through `claude/polish-2`.)
- **More polish (branch `claude/polish-2`, from main, awaiting review)**: the Friends hub's "Following you" and "Going to your festivals" lists are worked out with who you followed when they were read, so following someone there keeps them listed (showing Following, undoable) for the visit. A check-in only says "Sent to your crew" once it has left the outbox: `checkIn` returns the update's id, and the check-in screen says "Sending…" ("The signal's slow. It keeps trying by itself…") or "Saved for when there's signal" until then. Weekend 2 of a two-weekend festival now counts as on: `isLive(festival, today)` in `festivals/when.ts` (either weekend to the morning after it, not the days between; one-run festivals unchanged) is used by Tonight's `isOn`, the festival page's live cards and `YourFestival`, so Tonight, water reminders and the live view work on ACL weekend 2 even with Every weekend picked. Tests in `friends.test.tsx`, `crewUpdates.test.tsx` and `tonight.test.ts`.
- **Crew updates review fixes (branch `claude/polish-2`)**: from a code review of PR #76. `storage.rules` lets you write over your own crew file (`create, update`), so a video whose upload finished but whose update didn't save can be sent again under the same name (emulator: 62 checks pass; **needs a storage rules deploy**). When reading a video's Discover copy fails, the outbox counts it as a failed try and retries on the usual back-off instead of waiting for the app to reopen. Crew-only videos you upload are remembered per crew (AsyncStorage `crewVideos.v1`, `withSentVideo` / `cleanSentVideos` in `updates.ts`); once a crew you sent them to no longer has you (checked with `service.crew` first, so a failed read deletes nothing), `SocialService.forgetCrewVideos(crewId, ids)` removes your files from storage (each of mp4/mov/m4v/jpg tried). The reply bar on `crew-story.tsx` has its own key (it shared the update card's). Tests in `crewUpdates.test.tsx` and `updates.test.ts`. Refusals are counted apart from no-signal tries (`OutboxItem.refused`, `refusedOnce`), per crew: one crew refusing never stops the others getting it, and only the refusing crews are given up after `REFUSED_TRIES`. `delivered(..., reached)` records `reachedSome`; an update no crew could take is listed in `useCrewUpdates().dropped`, and the check-in screen says "Couldn't reach your crew" instead of "Sent". `forgetCrewVideos` treats only a missing file as removed; any other failure throws, so the list is kept and tried again.
- **Deploy key check (branch `claude/deploy-check`, 2026-10-02)**: every website deploy since at least Oct 1 failed with "Failed to authenticate": the `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` secret held `.env`-style lines ("EXPO_PUBLI…") instead of the service account `.json`, so the nightly catalogue (160 YouTube clips on Oct 2, with the `YOUTUBE_API_KEY` secret working) never reached the live site or the app. The Firebase repository variables were also empty. `website.yml` now has a "Check the Firebase key" step before Deploy that fails with a plain message when the secret isn't a service account key (never printing it); `docs/web-deploy.md` has a troubleshooting row. The user must replace the secret (step 2) and add the six variables (step 3). Since 2026-10-03 the key works, and a "Database and storage rules" step (after Deploy, non-PR runs, `continue-on-error`, `firebase-tools@14 deploy --only firestore:rules,firestore:indexes,storage` with the key as `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`) deploys rules and indexes on every push to main: the live rules were found to predate Milestone 124 (founder reads were refused), so founders and other newer features had been quietly failing.
- **Android menus and the spectrum (branch `claude/polish-2`)**: from a code review of PR #75. Android's alert shows at most three buttons (`ANDROID_ALERT_MAX`), so `showActionSheet` on Android puts longer lists (the artist page's Rate menu, My Sound's ••• menu) in the app's own sheet via `DialogHost`, which now shows whatever is queued on any platform (only the web and long Android lists queue) and closes on Android's back button. `SoundSpectrum` segments shrink to make room for their 2 pt gaps, so the line ends exactly at its track (checked in Chromium). Tests in `src/lib/__tests__/web.test.tsx`.
- **This or that (Milestone 115, branch `claude/this-or-that`, awaiting phone testing; option 1 of the user's list, now that real clips arrive nightly)**: `this-or-that.tsx` (full-screen modal, no swipe-down; `?then=discover` ends in Discover, else My Sound): two live sets in 16:9 cards ("or" between), one playing at a time through the usual `ClipPlayer` (the other shows "▶ Tap to hear"; a set that can't play says so and can still be picked), the artist's face, name and top sound (in its colour), "This one" on each, then Both / Neither; "Round 3 of 8 · Tap the set you'd rather hear"; Skip for now. Each pick is a like on that clip (`setLiked`), so it counts like a like in Discover with spill-over; Both likes both, Neither likes nothing (preferring one set is no verdict on the other, so nothing is skipped). The end: "Your sound is taking shape", "You picked 6 sets…", a `SoundSpectrum` and top three sounds from the live `tasteMix`, See My Sound / Start watching. Too few sets ("Not enough sets yet") offers Pick artists you know. Pure `features/taste/thisOrThat.ts`: `topSound`, `thisOrThatPairs(clips, artist, { prefer, skip, rounds })` (one YouTube clip per artist with sound tags, never an artist you rated or reacted to; your festivals first; queues per top sound, biggest first, each round two different sounds, turning through them; deterministic), `likedBy`, `roundLine`, `pickedLine`, `ROUNDS` 8, `ROUNDS_MIN` 3. Entry points: the welcome's last step leads with "Play this or that" when there are at least `ROUNDS_MIN` pairs (Pick artists you know second), and My Sound's ••• menu ("Play this or that"). Checked in Chromium. Tests: `features/taste/__tests__/thisOrThat.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/thisOrThat.test.tsx`.
- **Find my crew (Milestone 117, branch `claude/find-crew`, awaiting review, a Firestore rules deploy and phone testing; option 4 of the user's list)**: a meeting spot each crew picks ahead of time, and one tap to say you've lost them. Firestore `crews/{id}/meta/meet` (`text` 1 to `MEET_SPOT_MAX` 80, `by` = you, `at` = server time; members read and write, members delete; removed when the last member leaves). `SocialService.meetSpot(crewId)` / `setMeetSpot(crewId, text)` (empty clears; `MeetSpot` in `social/models.ts`, `meetSpotFromDoc` in `mapping.ts`). Crew updates may carry `lost: true` (check-ins only, rules-checked): `useCrewUpdates()` adds `meetSpotFor`, `setMeetSpot` (throws when it can't save) and `callLost(festivalId)`, which queues a check-in "Can’t find you. Heading to <spot>" (or "Can’t find you") through the usual outbox, so it waits for signal and keeps the time you tapped. `features/crews/MeetSpotCard.tsx` (the spot, "Set by Maya · 9:10 PM · Change", or "Pick a meeting spot", inline field) on Tonight (one per crew there) and the crew page; `features/crews/LostButton.tsx` (amber "Can't find my crew", confirmed, then Sending… / Saved for when there's signal / Sent / Couldn't reach your crew) on Tonight and the check-in screen. A lost update reads "Can’t find the crew · 5:30 PM" (`updateLine`), has an amber ring in the stories row and an amber card in the story. Emulator: 64 checks pass. Tests: the meeting spot case in `src/app/__tests__/crewUpdates.test.tsx`.

It ends by itself the morning after the last day (Tonight's window).

Switch / Not now; then "Festival mode is on · Full app" or "Festival mode · Switch on"); Settings → Festival nights → "Test festival mode" (any day, with `testFestival`); the festival page's Info tab has `TryFestivalMode` for any festival not over. In a test with nothing filmed there, the lineup's sets stand in ("Test: lineup sets stand in for videos filmed here").

  - *No signal*: pure `features/friends/adds.ts` (`queueAdd`, `markAdded` (`later` when it wasn't the first try), `markTried` (no-signal tries never count as refusals; `ADD_REFUSED_MAX` 12 refusals give up), `dueAdds`, `addDelay` 15 s doubling to 5 min, `nextDue`, `tidyAdds` (waiting given up after `ADD_KEEP_MS` a day, finished forgotten after 3 days), `cleanAdds`, `addLine`). `state/friendAdds.tsx` (`FriendAddsProvider` inside FestivalMode; AsyncStorage `friendAdds.v1`: scans per account, your code per account and whether it reached Afterglow, the look): your code is made on the phone and saved with `setFriendCode` when there's signal; a scan is kept at once, tried straight away when online (10 s timeout = no signal), else sent by itself on signal back (expo-network), foreground or its wait; going through later sends a phone notification "You and Maya are friends now" (`lib/notifyNow.ts`, only if notifications are already allowed; nothing on web) that opens their profile. `social.noteFollowing(uid)` counts them as followed on the phone.

Rules: the reverse follow needs the friend link in the same write with their current code, both profiles, and not blocked. Account deletion removes your friend code and links. No counts, shortcuts or tabs.

As you hold, the camera turns into a glowing heart (`components/HeartHold.tsx`: `HeartFace` over the button, `HeartHalo` behind the bar, `hold` 0 to 1 after a 120 ms wait so a tap stays a camera; letting go early eases back); on the long press it blooms (`HeartBurst`: a pink wash, a ring and the heart rising and growing, `burst` over 750 ms) and `/care?from=heart` opens at 320 ms, where `features/care/HeartIntro.tsx` lands the heart (with its glow) into its place above the title while the pink fades (`HeartMark`, `BloomWash`). The figures are typical harm-reduction ranges written from general guidance: **have them checked against DanceSafe/TripSit before launch** (`docs/app-store.md`, which also says the app is now 17+).

- **"I'm OK" and asking your crew for help (Milestone 121, branch `claude/crew-care`, awaiting review, a Firestore rules deploy and phone testing; the user said "Yes! Sent only when you tap it, never from how you feel by itself. Pure `features/crews/care.ts`: `CARE_NOTE` ("I’m OK" / "Not feeling great. Can someone come find me?"), `careFor(level)` (okay or better → ok), `careAlerts(updates, crews, { me, blocked, now })` (each crewmate's latest care check-in within `CARE_WINDOW_MS` 3 h, when it's help; a later ok clears it; never you, blocked people or people who left), `myCare`, `helpTitle` ("Maya isn’t feeling great"), `helpBody`, `toNotify` / `withNotified` / `cleanNotified` (`NOTIFIED_MAX` 100), `careFestival(tonight, crewFestivals)`. `updateLine` reads "Not feeling great · 5:30 PM" / "I’m OK · 5:30 PM"; `crewWhere` ignores care check-ins. `useCrewUpdates()` adds `sendCare(festivalId, care)` (through the outbox), `careAlerts`, `myCare` (sent or waiting), and nudges once per call for help with `notifyNow` (opens `/crew-story?uid=…`; AsyncStorage `crewCareNotified.v1`; only if notifications are already allowed). UI: `features/crews/CareButtons.tsx` ("I’m OK" green, "Ask my crew for help" red and confirmed; after asking, "I’m OK now"; Sending… / Saved for when there's signal / Sent / Couldn't reach) on Tonight, the Friends tab at a festival, the check-in screen, and Look after yourself's feeling card ("Tell your crew?" for 30 min after a check-in, with the one that fits; needs a crew at tonight's festival or your only live crew); `CrewHelpAlerts.tsx` (red card: who, "Asked for help 5:30 PM · 12 min ago", the late line, "Last said they were at Main Stage, 9:40 PM." or a hint, See their update, On my way (a reply)) at the top of Tonight's crew section, the Friends tab at a festival and Look after yourself. Stories: a red ring that stays until they're OK (green when fresh for OK); the story card is tinted. The privacy policy says only that one check-in reaches the crew. Docs: "I'm OK and asking for help" in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`. Tests: `features/crews/__tests__/care.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/crewCare.test.tsx`.

Pure `features/secrets/secrets.ts`: `SECRETS` (Light show: shake the phone on Discover; PLUR: search "plur" (or "peace love unity respect"); Sunrise crew: open the app between 5 and 7 AM, once; Light inside the light: tap the glow level ring on Quests seven times; Small print: tap the version line in Settings seven times; Fireworks: pull Festivals to refresh three times within 20 s; Edge of the map: zoom the festival map all the way in; Bass drop: press and hold the My Sound title), `searchSecret`, `isSunriseHour`, `shakeStep` (three jolts of `SHAKE_G` 1.6 g within 900 ms, each 120 ms apart), `tapStep` (seven taps, each within 700 ms), `cleanFound`, `secretsLine`. `state/secrets.ts` (shared store, AsyncStorage `secrets.v1`; `revealSecret` plays the moment every time and marks it found the first time; `onSecret`). `features/secrets/SecretStage.tsx` (root layout; never takes touches but its toast): Light show (dimmed screen, ten laser beams from the bottom corners sweeping in festival colours, white strobes), Heart rain (30 hearts swaying down, "Peace · Love · Unity · Respect"), Fireworks (five bursts of 26 sparks with gravity), Sunrise (a warm sky and a sun rising, "Up for the sunrise"), Bass drop (a build, a flash and four rings rolling out), Sparkle (stars twinkling); each with haptics to match (`haptic.heavy` is new, for the drop); the first find adds a purple "Secret found" toast (opens Secrets); reduced motion: only the toast. `useShake` (expo-sensors `Accelerometer`, no permission needed, only while Discover is live, never on web); `SunriseWatcher` (root layout). `secrets.tsx` (stack push): "3 of 8 secrets found", a bar, found ones (how, when, tap to play again) and the rest as riddles. Settings has a new "Fun" group (Quests, Secrets) and Quests links to Secrets. Festivals now has real pull to refresh (`useCatalogRefresh` in `state/catalog.tsx` reads the hosted catalogue again; at least 700 ms so the spinner reads). `test/setup.ts` mocks `Accelerometer` (`__accel` sends a reading); `test/afterEnv.ts` resets the store. Pure `features/festivals/calendar.ts` (`monthGrid`, `festivalsOn` (either weekend of a two-weekend festival, not between), `monthFestivals`, `calendarMonths`, `monthTitle`, `monthLine`). - **Review fixes for tonight's work (branch `claude/review-fixes`, stacked on `claude/calendar`)**: from a code review of Milestones 135 to 137 and the polish branches. Daily quests now count which things were added since the day began, not how many (`Counters` are id lists: liked/saved/watched clip ids, rated/warm/followed artist ids, stars as "festival:artist"; `added`), so taking an old like or star back and giving it again never counts; `lowerBase` is gone. The day's three are drawn once and kept in the book (`QuestBook.picks`, `picksFor`), so planning a festival mid-day never swaps one. Events counted while the book loads land on it as it loads. "Head to head" is offered only when This or that really has `ROUNDS_MIN` pairs for you. Your level waits for side quests, badge claims and secrets to load, so launching never toasts a false level-up. `PlanProvider` exposes `starKeys` (was `starCount`). Festival map: frames once per `frameKey` (filter or tour), so a place looked up later never yanks it back; the base dots come back while you drag and step aside only once settled; it measures after both directions of a fling come to rest, and after a pinch where it's springing to; Edge of the map plays only when you reach full zoom, not on every move there. Shakes count force beyond gravity (`SHAKE_G` 1.8 beyond 1 g), so running or dancing never sets off the light show; the accelerometer reads every 100 ms. The crash screen's "Go to Discover" resets navigation to the tabs before drawing again (the root boundary replaces everything, so `router.replace` had nothing to talk to).
- **What's new (branch `claude/whats-new`, stacked on `claude/review-fixes`)**: `whats-new.tsx` (modal): "New in Afterglow · Fresh tonight", a card per new thing (festival map, daily quests, secrets with a hint, festival calendar), each with "Try it ›", and "Let’s go". `features/whatsNew/whatsNew.ts` (`WHATS_NEW` with a `key`, bump it and rewrite `items` for the next update; `showWhatsNew`). `WhatsNewLauncher` (root layout) opens it once per key over a tab, only for someone who already used the app (going, reactions or ratings) and never on a festival night (Tonight opens instead); someone new is marked as having seen it. Off in screen tests unless `showWhatsNewInTests(true)`. AsyncStorage `whatsNew.v1`. Tests: `src/app/__tests__/whatsNew.test.tsx`.
- **Saying yes (branch `claude/going-moment`, stacked on `claude/whats-new`, awaiting phone testing)**: tapping "I'm going" on a festival page is a little celebration: a success haptic and a pop of confetti from the pill (`components/ConfettiBurst.tsx`: 24 pieces in the festival colours flying up and out, tumbling and falling away over 1.3 s; a new `key` fires it again; never takes touches; nothing with reduced motion). Undoing stays quiet. The big quest toasts (all three done, the weekly, a new level) burst with it too.
- **More quest pieces (branch `claude/more-quests`, stacked on `claude/going-moment`, awaiting phone testing)**: the festival map has a glass locate button at the top right: "Show me on the map" switches on Near you (it asks for location; refused says how to allow it in settings) and glides to you; once on, "Show where I am" glides back (`FestivalMap` `toHere`). Discover's first-time tips end with "Daily quests →" (a spark; spoken "The ring at the top right holds your daily quests. Tap it to see them"; `QUESTS_HINT` in `discover/hints.ts`, after the care tip, which the website now skips; `state/questTip.ts`, AsyncStorage `questTip.v1`, done the first time Quests opens; not in festival mode). Three new milestones on the Badges tab (`milestones.ts`, medals in `Milestones.tsx`): On a roll (a 7-day quest streak, flame), Afterglow forever (30 days, double flame) and Neon (450 glow, a spark); `MilestoneInput` takes `streak` (`longestStreak` in `quests/daily.ts`, over the kept 60 days) and `glow` (`useProfileBadges` reads `useDailyQuests`). `test/afterEnv.ts` also resets the care and quest tips. Tests: the locate case in `festivalMap.test.tsx`, the tip case in `quests.test.tsx`, `longestStreak` in `daily.test.ts`.
- **Second review fixes (branch `claude/review-fixes-2`, stacked on `claude/more-quests`)**: from a review of this morning's work. Festival map: after a filter change while zoomed in the base dots come back (the reframe resets them); "Show me" glides to you only when you ask (a new `toHere`, answered once), never again on a filter change or a refreshed location; a map framed before any places were known (looked up later) frames again once they arrive. Daily quests: a book saved by the earlier version (counts as numbers) keeps its glow, streak and today's progress and only restarts its snapshots (`needsBase`, filled by `turnOver`); the best streak is kept in the book (`bestStreak`, raised by `settleQuests`; `bestStreakOf`), so the streak badges stay earned after their days are tidied away; events counted on a new day before the book has turned over wait for today's book instead of landing on yesterday's; the tracker updates the book as it is at that moment (`updateQuestBook(update)` replaces `setQuestBook`), so an event counted in between is never overwritten; whether This or that has enough pairs is worked out once for every screen (`enoughSetsFor`, memoised on catalogue, ratings, reactions and festivals); if This or that runs out of sets for you, opening it counts for Head to head, so it never blocks your streak. `useCareTip` and `useQuestTip` are undefined while loading, and Discover shows neither tip until both are known (no flash of the wrong one). Tests: old books, `bestStreakOf` in `daily.test.ts`, the new-day case in `quests.test.tsx`.

Firestore `usage/{uid}` (`throwbacks`, `memory`): sharing a throwback writes the memory and the count + 1 in one batch; unsharing deletes it and writes the count − 1; rules: a memory with `pastName` needs the count moving on by one in the same write (`getAfter`) and at most 3 unless `hasPlus` (an active `plus/{uid}`); the count only moves with a throwback created or removed in the same write, is read only by you, and can be deleted only once your profile is gone (account deletion does it after the profile batch, quietly). A pasted catalogue JSON (`looksLikeCatalogFile`) is summed up (`fileSummary`, `fileSummaryLine`). **Check before publishing** (checked with `buildCatalog` against the live catalogue) → **Publish to everyone** (confirmed) → Firestore `published/{id}` (`LivePatch`: title, festivalId, file ≤ `PATCH_MAX_CHARS` 800,000, by, server `publishedAt`, optional dropId; read by anyone, written/deleted only by the support account, never updated); "Copy as a catalogue file". **Pictures and PDFs** (`expo-document-picker`, added with `EXPO_OFFLINE=1 npx expo install`, in Expo Go; `test/setup.ts` mocks it with `__state.next`) → **Save to the inbox**: Firestore `drops/{id}` (`Drop`: by, festivalId or festivalName, note ≤ 500, text ≤ 50,000, files ≤ `DROP_FILES_MAX` 10, status waiting / sent / done) and Storage `drops/{id}/<n>-<name>` (support account only, images, PDFs or text under 25 MB). **Copy for Claude** shares `claudeBrief` (festival, note, each file's download link, text, the drop id) and marks it "With Claude"; Use the text, Done, Delete. **Live on Afterglow** lists published updates with **Take down**. `LivePatchSync` (root layout) reads `livePatches()` on open and foreground into `state/livePatches.ts` (AsyncStorage `livePatches.v1`); `CatalogProvider` keeps its base `Built` and applies them with `applyPatches` (`data/merge.ts`, oldest first, unusable ones skipped). - **Crew chat, and crews by invite only (Milestone 141, branch `claude/crew-chat`, awaiting review, a Firestore rules deploy and phone testing; the user asked on 2026-10-03 for chat in crews, agreed to by joining, with crews joinable only by invite)**: guide: "Invite only, and the chat rules" and "Crew chat" in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`.
  - *Invite only*: `SocialService.joinCrew(id, code?)` needs an in-app invite or the invite link's current code, else `not-invited` ("Crews are invite only…"). `crewInviteCode(crewId)` (any member; made the first time, `crews/{id}/meta/invite`, members-only read) and `newCrewInviteCode` (owner; the old link stops). Joining (and starting a crew) writes `crews/{id}/joins/{uid}` (`agreed: true`, server `at`, the link's `code`) in the same transaction; rules `joinedFairly(id)` check it. Links are `https://afterglowmusic.app/crew/<id>?code=<code>` (`crewPath(id, code)`); `useCrews()` adds `inviteLink`, `newInviteLink`, `join(id, code?)`. The crew page (`code` param) shows "This crew is invite only" without one, else the chat rules ("By joining, you agree to the crew chat rules", `CHAT_RULES`), a guidelines link and "Agree and join". The invite screen's link sharing makes the coded link; owners get "Make a new invite link". Start a crew's copy says crews are invite only. Leave/remove copy says messages go too and a new invite is needed.
  - *Chat*: Firestore `crews/{id}/chat/{messageId}` (`uid`, `text` 1 to `CHAT_MAX` 500, `atMs`, server `sentAt`; members read and write as themselves, never edited, removed by writer or owner, the support account reads and removes reported ones). `SocialService.sendCrewMessage` (idempotent by id), `watchCrewChat(crewId, max, listener, onError)` (Firestore `onSnapshot`), `deleteCrewMessage`, `reportedMessage`; `takeDown('chat', 'crewId/messageId')`; reports `kind: 'chat'`; `crewMessageFromDoc` in `mapping.ts`. Leaving or being removed takes your messages; ending a crew removes chat, joins and the invite code. `fakeSocial` has `chat`, `crewCodes`, `agreed`, live watchers and `codeOf(crewId)` for tests. Emulator: 88 checks pass. **The user must redeploy the rules.**
  - *Pure* `features/crews/chat.ts`: `CHAT_RULES`, `QUICK_MESSAGES`, `messageId`, `cleanMessage`, outbox (`queueMessage`, `messageSent`, `messageFailed` 15 s doubling to 2 min, `dueMessages`, `gaveUp` after `CHAT_REFUSED_TRIES` 3 refusals or `CHAT_KEEP_MS` a day, `cleanChatOutbox`), `cleanChatRead`, `chatLines` (sent plus yours waiting, in the order written, blocked left out), `unreadCount`, `arrivedLate` (`CHAT_LATE_MS` 10 min), `chatRows` (day headings, runs within `RUN_GAP_MS` 5 min), `chatPreview`, `unreadLine`.
  - *State* `state/crewChat.tsx` (`CrewChatProvider` inside CrewUpdates; `useCrewChat()` falls back to off): each of your crews' newest `CHAT_WATCH` 150 messages followed live; outbox `crewChatOutbox.v1` sent oldest first on signal, foreground and a back-off timer; last read `crewChatRead.v1`; `linesFor`, `latestFor`, `unreadFor`, `unreadTotal`, `send`, `remove`, `retry`, `markRead`, `loaded`.
  - *UI*: `crew-chat/[id].tsx` (stack push): header (crew, people, festival), the rules card, day headings, bubbles (yours in glow, theirs glass with name and face), "Waiting for signal" / "Not sent. Tap to try again" in amber, "Reached the crew 8:20 PM" when late, quick messages when the box is empty, hold (or the VoiceOver action) to delete, report or block; non-members are told only people in the crew can read it. `features/crews/CrewChatCard.tsx` ("Crew chat", the latest message, "2 new messages") on the crew page and, at a festival, the Friends tab under Check in. The Friends tab's crew rows say "N new messages", and the tab's glow dot includes unread chat. HQ's Reports read and remove chat messages. The Community Guidelines have a "Crew chats" section; the privacy policy says who sees messages and that joining records agreement and how you were invited.
  - *Like check-ins, built for bad signal (the user on 2026-10-03)*: every message carries the time it was written (`atMs`), which the crew sees and which orders the chat; the server's `sentAt` is only used to say when it got through. Sending is `SocialService.sendCrewMessages(crewId, inputs)`: all of a crew's waiting messages in one small batch write (no read first), given up after `CHAT_SEND_TIMEOUT_MS` 12 s as no signal (Firestore keeps trying in the background; a later retry that finds one already there treats it as sent, never doubled; resolves with the ids now in the chat). Delays are said plainly (`features/crews/chat.ts`: `heldFor`, `waitingLine`, `deliveryLine`, `lateNote`, `justArrivedLate`, `arrivedLateLine`, `DELAY_NOTICE_MS` 1 min): in the chat, an amber "Waiting for signal. Your message sends by itself as soon as there’s a bar, still marked 8:02 PM." with Try now (`sendNow`); once yours get through after a minute or more, "Your 2 messages reached the crew, held up 18 min by signal. The crew sees the time you wrote them." (`deliveryFor`, dismissable), plus a phone notification when held 10 min or more and you're not in that chat (`notifyNow`, only if allowed; `setOpenChat`); a crewmate's late message reads "Sent 7:30 PM · reached the crew 8:00 PM" under it, and the chat points it out ("Maya’s message from 7:30 PM just got through, held up 30 min by signal. It’s up the chat at 7:30 PM."). The crew chat card shows messages waiting for signal in amber. Emulator: 89 checks pass.
  - *Seen by*: Firestore `crews/{id}/reads/{uid}` (`at` = server time; members read, you write only your own, removed with you or the crew; emulator: 90 checks pass). `SocialService.markCrewChatRead`, `watchCrewReads` (live). `useCrewChat()` sends your receipt whenever you read a chat (`markRead`), again once there's signal if it didn't get through, and exposes `readsFor`. Under your latest sent message: "8:00 PM · Delivered", "Seen by Maya", "Seen by Maya and Sam", "Seen by everyone" (pure `seenBy`, `seenLine` in `chat.ts`; both times are the server's). `fakeSocial` has `reads` and `readAs(crewId, uid)`.
  - *Crew call*: a daily quest in the plan lane ("Send a message in your crew chat", 15 glow, goes to Friends), offered only when you're in a crew (`QuestAbility.crew` from `useCrewChat().canChat`); sending counts the `chat` quest event. Typing PLUR in a crew chat sets off the PLUR secret too.
  - *Tests*: `features/crews/__tests__/chat.test.ts`, the invite-only case in `features/social/__tests__/crews.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/crewChat.test.tsx` (unread card, rules, quick and typed messages, offline then sent with the written time, report and block, leaving), `crews.test.tsx` (invite only, agree and join). Other crew tests join with `mockSocial.codeOf(crew.id)`.

- **Sprouts (Milestone 143, branch `claude/sprouts`, stacked on `claude/crew-chat`, awaiting review, a Firestore rules and indexes deploy, and phone testing; the user's idea on 2026-10-03, a rave tradition: they chose "code now, tap later", "your journey grows them" and "a few seeds a day")**: guide: "Sprouts" in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`. Pure `features/sprouts/sprouts.ts`: `SPROUT_KINDS` (seedling, plur, wanderer, bass, melody, groove, spotlight, sunrise with body colours and an accent), `sproutLink` / `readSproutLink` / `sproutCardFrom` (`https://afterglowmusic.app/sprout/<id>?u&h&k&n`, or `afterglow://sprout/…`), `sproutIdFrom` (20 characters), `giftTitle`, `seedsLeft` / `seedsLine` (`SEEDS_PER_DAY` 5, `SEEDS_FESTIVAL_DAY` 10, by `questDay`), growth (`GROWTH` festival day 8, give 4, film 3, watch 1; `GROW_AT` 0/6/18/36; `growthOf` with `leaning` by the top track (sunrise for a video filmed 5 to 8 AM; bass/groove/melody by the watched sets' sounds), `festivalDaysOf`, `garden`, `newlyBloomed`, `givableKinds`, `collectedKinds`, `gardenLine`, `cleanBloomed`, `STARTER_ID`). Pure `features/sprouts/outbox.ts` (gifts and scans kept for signal: `retryDelay` 15 s doubling to 5 min, `CLAIM_KEEP_MS` 3 days, `queueClaim`, `tidy`, `claimLine`). `Sprout.tsx` draws a little bean with a face, leaves by stage and the kind's mark in bloom (heart, star, sound wave, flower, spiral, sun, sunrise), swaying (still with reduced motion; `locked` grey). `state/sprouts.tsx` (`SproutsProvider` inside FriendAdds; AsyncStorage `sprouts.v1`: gifts, scans, picked-up sprouts per account, blooms, `starterAt`): `give`, `takeBack`, `claim` (kept when signed out, picked up after sign-in), `checkGift`, `refresh` (who picked up your sprouts, notification when it happens with the app in the background), garden from `seen`, memories, gifts and festival days. Firestore `sprouts/{id}` (rules in `firestore.rules`; emulator: 92 checks pass) and two indexes; `SocialService` `giveSprout`, `claimSprout` (`sprout-taken`), `sprout`, `sproutsGiven`, `sproutsReceived`, `takeBackSprout`; account deletion removes them; `sproutFromDoc`; `fakeSocial.sprouts`. Screens: `give-sprout.tsx` (modal; kind chips, note, seeds, the code: `FriendQR` now takes `dots` and `centre`), `sprout/[id].tsx` (full-screen, works on the website; `APP_STORE_URL` in `config/app.ts`, empty for now), `garden.tsx`. The scanner reads sprout codes too and opens without an account (friend codes still need one). Entry points: a Sprout button in the Friends tab's quick actions (now 52 pt circles), a hideable "Your garden" row on Profile (`GardenShelf`, shelf `garden`), Settings → Your garden, a daily quest "Pass it on" (plan lane, signed in), and What's new. Tests: `features/sprouts/__tests__/sprouts.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/sprouts.test.tsx`. The bell says when someone picks up a sprout you gave (`sproutNotices` in `outbox.ts`, kind `sprout`, for `SPROUT_NEWS_DAYS` 14; `SproutsProvider` now sits just inside Social, around Notices). Three milestones on the Badges tab: Green thumb (a sprout in bloom), Kind soul (10 sprouts given and picked up), Gardener (all 7 kinds) (`bloomed`, `given`, `kinds` in `MilestoneInput`). Later: tapping phones (NFC) with the development build.
- **Sprout fixes and side quests (branch `claude/sprouts`, in PR #126)**: two side quests (now 12): Sprout swap (give a sprout and get one on the same night of a festival you're going to) and Spread the love (5 sprouts given and picked up at one festival) (`QuestInput.sprouts`, fed by `QuestTracker` from `useSprouts`). From a review: claims and gifts in progress are tracked (`inFlight`), so a scan is never picked up twice; a sprout scanned while signed out that turns out to be your own is dropped, never put in your garden; switching accounts stops a send round; taking back a gift that may already be on Afterglow marks it `takenBack` (seed back at once) and removes it there once there's signal (`giftsToTakeBack`; `takeBackSprout` treats a missing sprout as done and says `sprout-taken` when someone has it, which keeps it given); a sprout older than 30 days or from someone who blocked you says so at once (`sprout-gone`, "This sprout can’t be picked up. Ask them for a new code.") instead of retrying for days; who picked up your sprout is read on launch, signal back and coming back to the app (not after every send); `give` reads seeds from the latest state, so two quick taps never spend an extra seed. The website's sprout page asks people to create their account there first.
- **Sprout blooms and link previews (branch `claude/sprouts`, in PR #126)**: the bell says when one of your sprouts blooms ("Your sprout bloomed into a Wanderer sprout" / "You can give Wanderer sprouts now.", `bloomNotices` in `outbox.ts`, from `bloomedAt` kept in `sprouts.v1`, `useSprouts().blooms`). Sprout links get their own link preview: `scripts/page-previews.ts` writes `dist/sprout.html` (`sproutPreview` in `features/share/previews.ts`: "A sprout for you · Afterglow" with `public/sprout-og.png`, 1200 × 630, drawn from the real `Sprout` in Chromium; a `wide` preview keeps the big card), and `firebase.json` sends `/sprout/**` to it (the app inside is the same).
- **Review fixes for crew chat, socials and the artist page (branch `claude/sprouts`, in PR #126)**: changing your photo or cover with a social username set was refused by the rules (the profile was written with a nested `socials`): every profile write now goes through `profileFields` in `firebase/mapping.ts` (usernames as their own fields; tested). The owner removing someone from a crew now makes a new invite link (and deletes their join record), so the old link can't let them straight back in. A chat message still in your outbox shows as waiting even when the phone's own copy of the chat already has it (Firestore shows writes at once), so "Delivered" means Afterglow has it. Waiting messages carry their account (`ChatOutboxItem.uid`) and read times are kept per account (`uid/crewId`), so switching accounts never sends one account's words as another; marking a chat read never stamps a time before its newest message (a slow phone clock left messages unread). A retry that mixes messages already there with new ones sends the new ones on their own instead of counting them as refused. A refused chat listener stops loading; anything written while the outbox loaded is kept. The artist page's crowd row treats only a missing index as "none" (no signal is tried again). A pasted link to an Instagram post, reel or story gives no username. The garden celebrates a sprout that bloomed since you last looked (`BadgeCelebration` `unlock.kind 'sprout'`, `useSprouts().newBlooms` / `bloomShown`, `bloomsShown` in `sprouts.v1`). Tonight has a Sprout button. A sprout picked up in the background (after signing in from a sprout link, or once there was signal) is marked `late` and the bell says "Your PLUR sprout from Maya is in your garden" (`arrivalNotices` in `outbox.ts`).
- **Quest fixes from a review (branch `claude/sprouts`, in PR #126)**: daily quests are offered only when they can be done that day, so a streak never breaks on an impossible one: Knock knock only while a festival week door is unlocked and not yet opened (`useAllOpenedDoors` in `state/festivalWeek.ts`); In the crowd only when there are at least 2 crowd videos (`useCommunityClips('mix')`) and it counts each crowd video once (its shared page marks it seen, `markSeen(community:<id>)`, never your own); Pass it on needs a profile (`QuestAbility.sprout`). The day's three are drawn once sign-in, your festivals and your crews have loaded (`QuestAbility.settled`; `DailyQuestTracker` waits up to `SETTLE_WAIT_MS` 8 s), so quests needing them aren't left out after a cold start. `openDoor` waits for the store to load and counts the quest itself, once per door. Festival week only counts down to a festival's first run (`nextRun(...).index > 0` gives none; pick weekend 2 to count down to it). Sprout swap goes by when you scanned a sprout (`GardenSprout.scannedAt`), not when it got through. True colours counts only colours you tap (plus the one showing). `questDay` turns over at 4 AM by the wall clock, also on clock-change days. The artist page's next show follows your weekend.
- **Wild sprouts (branch `claude/sprouts`, in PR #126)**: on each night of a festival you're going to that's on (`useTonight`), a wild sprout appears in your garden: `wild:<festival>:<night>` (`tonightKey`), already grown, its kind from `wildKind(id)` (a hash over the seven kinds, the same for everyone there that night), `foundAt` the festival's name; kept per phone in `sprouts.v1` `wild`. The garden celebrates it ("A wild sprout appeared", `BadgeCelebration` `unlock.foundAt`), tiles read "Found at Cyclops Cove 4", and the bell says "A wild Bass sprout appeared at Cyclops Cove 4" (`wildNotices`). Wild sprouts count for kinds collected (Gardener) but aren't yours to give until you grow that kind. Tests in `sprouts.test.ts` and `sprouts.test.tsx`.
- **Last sprout fixes (branch `claude/sprouts`, in PR #126)**: from a final review: reads inside the send round and take-back have a time limit (a hung read on weak signal can't freeze sending); scans in flight don't drive the retry timer; a scan saved for one account is never taken over or reported as picked up by another account on the same phone (`queueClaim` keys by sprout and account); a storage read that fails (not a broken file) never leads to saving empty data over your garden; giving waits until saved sprouts have loaded (`problem: 'loading'`); shown celebrations are kept up to `SHOWN_MAX` 2000 so old ones never come back. `BadgeCelebration` takes an `id` (else a key from the unlock), so one celebration straight after another gets its own moment, and ignores taps for its first `ARM_MS` 400 ms so a double tap never dismisses the next unseen.
- **Wild sprouts need a plan (branch `claude/sprouts`, in PR #126)**: `sprouts.v1` keeps `goingSince` (festival -> when it was first marked going on this phone; festivals already going the first time this runs get 0). A wild sprout, double seeds and festival-day growth (`festivalDaysOf(..., goingSince)`) count only when the festival was marked going before that night began (`nightStart`), so ticking "I'm going" on a festival that's on gives nothing extra that night.
- **Your festival** (Milestone 5) on each festival page. Before set times: lineup ranked into Must see / Strong match / Explore from the live match. After set times: day-by-day timeline, clashes grouped with a suggestion (clear winner, close call, or your starred pick, plus how much of the other set you can still catch), explore picks as discoveries when you're free or as a quiet alternative. A star marks a personal must-see at that festival only (festivalId + artistId); it's a planning preference stored apart from reactions and never changes the match. Artist pages opened from a festival get a `festival` route param and star for that festival; opened elsewhere they show no star.

Do not start the next milestone unless asked.
- **A fresh welcome (branch `claude/notes-welcome`, awaiting review and phone testing; the user chose it on 2026-10-03)**: `welcome.tsx` now has four steps (`STEPS`: hello, going, waiting, taste). Hello: `features/welcome/HeroOrb.tsx` (a breathing sphere in the icon's colours with four sound-coloured bubbles turning round a faint orbit, 28 s a turn; hidden from screen readers; still with reduced motion; 170 pt below 700 pt tall, else 220), "Find your sets, see your sound, go with your people." and three lines with coloured dots (`PROMISES`) rising in one after another. Going is unchanged. Waiting ("Here’s what’s waiting", with your first festival and its countdown, or "A few things to find once you’re in."): `features/welcome/Waiting.tsx`, four glass rows rising in (Your first sprout with the real `Sprout`, Three quests a day with a ring glyph, Plan with your crew, Look after yourself, worded for the website without the camera hold), then Continue. Taste is unchanged. Tests: the Welcome cases in `screens.test.tsx`, `thisOrThat.test.tsx`. Checked in Chromium at phone and iPhone SE sizes.

Faces: seedling smile, PLUR joy (happy closed eyes, open smile), Wanderer wink, Bass cool grin, Melody bliss (closed eyes, singing "o"), Groove sunglasses, Spotlight sparkle eyes, Sunrise sleepy. Leaf shapes: round, heart (PLUR), pointed (Wanderer, Sunrise), jagged like a sound wave (Bass), petal (Melody), wide (Groove). In bloom each wears something (`Wear`: PLUR a kandi bracelet, Wanderer a bandana, Bass headphones, Melody floating notes, Groove a gold chain) and a bigger mark on top (`Bloom`: heart, star, speaker cone with sound rings, flower crown, spinning record, light burst, rising sun), with a soft glow in the kind's colour, Spotlight's faint beam, and four twinkling sparkles (one shared value, off with reduced motion or `still`). Stage 0 is a seed peeking out of a mound of soil. Bodies have an outline, gloss and a rim of light; leaves have a shine. Locked kinds are grey silhouettes of their own leaves and what they wear (`toner`). `public/sprout-og.png` redrawn with PLUR, Bass and Melody in bloom. Checked in Chromium at every stage and size (26 to 290).
- **Website sound and swiping (branch `claude/web-sound`, awaiting the user's check in Safari; from the user's phone test on 2026-10-03: "feels a little laggy, sometimes the sound is started in the off state")**: `YouTubePlayer.web.tsx` used to mute any clip that wasn't playing 1.5 s after it was asked, so a clip that was only slow to load started muted. Pure `features/discover/webSound.ts`: `autoplayVerdict(lastState, waitedMs)` (playing or ended = started; buffering = keep loading with sound, checked every `AUTOPLAY_CHECK_MS` 400 ms up to `BUFFER_PATIENCE_MS` 10 s, then left alone; never started, cued or paused = the browser refused), checked from `AUTOPLAY_GRACE_MS` 1.2 s; `YT_STATE`. A refusal is remembered for the page (`SoundMemory`, module level): later clips start muted at once (no silent pause first) until you tap or press a key anywhere, then the next clip tries with sound again. `ClipPlayer` waits `NEAR_LOAD_DELAY_MS` 900 ms before loading the YouTube player of a clip next to the one on screen in a browser (the clip on screen always loads at once), so a swipe isn't slowed by the next player starting. Phones are unchanged. Tests: `features/discover/__tests__/webSound.test.ts`.

Drawing: `ArtistParts.tsx` (`ArtistBack`, `ArtistTop`, `ArtistWear` under/over the face, `ArtistFace` for cyclops, hollow, glasses, alien, stitched, fang), `Sprout` props `artist` and `tier` (`TIER_COLOR`), `SproutOf` (a kind or an artist's, looked up in the catalogue). State: `useSprouts()` adds `artists`, `newArtists`, `artistShown`; `give(kind, note, artist?)` ('not-yours' unless filmed). Codes: `SproutCard.artist`, link params `a`/`an`, `cleanSproutArtist`, `sproutName`, `aSprout`; `Gift.artist`, `GardenSprout.artist`; Firestore `artistId`/`artistName`. - **Back buttons that didn't work (branch `claude/back-buttons`; reported by the user on 2026-10-04)**: two causes, found by clicking Back/Close on every page in Chromium. (1) `PageActions` was drawn before the page's ScrollView on Settings, Your garden, Contact support, Look after yourself and Friends, so the scroll view sat on top and took the taps: `PageActions` (and the new `SheetClose`) now have `zIndex`/`elevation` 20. (2) 42 screens called `router.back()`, which does nothing with no history (a page opened from a link, or the website after a refresh): all now use `goBack()` (`lib/nav.ts`: back, or Discover). Sheets and pop-ups that only closed by swiping down (Sign in, Start a crew, Check in, Show my sound for, Switch account, Request a festival, Your sound, What to discover, a missing memory) get `components/SheetClose` (a glass X at the top right); a tag screen with nothing to tag closes itself. Tests: `src/app/__tests__/backButtons.test.tsx`.
- **Bug hunt over 2026-10-03/04's work (branch `claude/bug-hunt`)**: three reviews of PRs #127 to #131. Website sound: a clip that had been on screen was thrown away and reloaded when you swiped on (its `mayLoad` latch never set while active: now set during render once active); unmuting with YouTube's own button now counts as a tap (the player page's `time` message carries `muted`); a play with no answer yet, or still unstarted, waits `UNSTARTED_PATIENCE_MS` 3 s before it counts as refused. Tagging: `memory-tag` waits for memories to load before leaving with nothing to tag. Sprouts: a gift link edited to add `a`/`an` can no longer turn a plain sprout into an artist's (the server's copy wins whenever it was read); artist ids follow the catalogue's own shape (`^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,79}$` in `cleanSproutArtist` and the rules; emulator 93 checks) and `give` cleans the artist before saving; the first-run "already seen" mark covers every filmed artist, known to this catalogue or not, so a hosted catalogue arriving later brings no burst of celebrations; received artist sprouts no longer count as kinds or in Profile's garden line; locked artist silhouettes keep their face. Welcome: the first step scrolls and shrinks with larger text or a short screen; its gradient ids are unique (`welcome-halo`).

The overview (`features/sound-map/SoundOverview.tsx`) is your sound's summary card, then "Your sounds" (your top three; "Where you might start" before you've shaped it), "Sounds to try next" (neighbours, "Close to dubstep"), "Find your sound faster" (three tips for where you are) and "Every sound" (the rest, opening in place), and an "Open your bubble map" button. Each sound card: what it feels like, your share, your best matches in it (to their pages), Watch (Discover on that sound, when it has clips), and "How it sounds" (what to listen for, how it differs from its neighbour; open on your top sound). The words live in `features/sound-map/guide.ts` (pure: `SOUND_GUIDE`, `nextSounds`, `closeTo`, `otherSounds`, `artistsIn`, `soundTips`; tests in `__tests__/guide.test.ts`; screen test `src/app/__tests__/soundOverview.test.tsx`). Map tests now open `/sound?view=map`.
- **Collectibles speed pass and a bug hunt** (2026-10-04): collectibles and sprouts smaller than `MOVES_FROM` (100, in `Collectible.tsx`) are still unless given `still={false}` (the welcome screen's sprout), so the garden, shelves, hunts and finds scroll smoothly; the big ones (detail sheets, the celebration, the find page) still move. A still collectible is drawn as one picture instead of up to seven layers; a stopped one rests (never frozen mid-blink). Sprouts have their own gradient names per drawing (`useId`), so two the same on one web page keep their colours. Stickers: a send with no answer in time counts as no signal and is tried again (the phone waits 15s, longer than the backend's 12s), and only "ended" or "no such sticker" stop retries (anything else is tried again, e.g. rules not deployed yet); the backend says "ended" only when it really is (found after its end date, or sent more than `FIND_GRACE_DAYS` after it); scanning signed out keeps your own find even if another account on the phone has that sticker; a check that finishes after you sign in still lands; a hunt that couldn't be read is tried again each time signal comes back. My Sound: the overview always shows your sound across everyone (not the festival the map was showing; "Your sounds" is hidden when empty); `view=map` opens the map even when the tab is open (the Stargazer quest and your profile's orbit use it); switching views resets the map's "back to top matches" button and closes search.
- **Follow-ups to that bug hunt** (2026-10-04): taking a sticker down in HQ marks its place in its hunt `gone` (one transaction with the delete; Firestore rules let HQ rewrite a hunt's items at the same length), so the hunt can still be finished: `huntProgress` counts a gone place only if you found it first (`counts`, `HuntProgress.total`), `HuntRow` leaves out gone ones you never found, HQ counts only live stickers. End dates (`parseEndDate`) now last until that day is over everywhere (the end of the day at UTC−12), whatever time zone HQ's phone is in; `untilLine`/`findLine` show the day it was set for (UTC, 12 hours before), and older ones read the same. On My Sound each orb's atmosphere is drawn in the same picture as its glass (`OrbGlass`; `OrbHalo` is gone), one drawing fewer per bubble. Mirrored creature parts (`mirror`/`both` in `creatures.tsx`) are lit from the top left like the rest: `relight` swaps their shade, highlight and rim fills to mirrored twins (`<uid>_vsm`, `_vhm`, `_vrm` in `Collectible.tsx`; gradient names now end `_`).
- **Hunt pages and the "Hunt complete" card** (2026-10-04, the sticker launch kit): `/hunt/[id]` (modal; from the garden's hunt rows and "See the hunt" / "Share your hunt" on a sticker's page) shows `HuntCard` (`features/finds/HuntCard.tsx`): the hunt's stickers in a grid (found ones in colour, gold-ringed once done; the rest grey, "Still out there"; taken-down ones you never found left out), "2 of 6 found" or gold "All 6 found", and `huntDateLine` ("Latest find …" / "Finished …", from `HuntProgress.last`). Share image on phones (`sharePoster`), share text everywhere (`huntShareText`). It reads the hunt again when it opens. `docs/stickers.md` has a checklist for running a hunt at an event. Tests: `stickers.test.tsx` ("finishing a hunt opens a card to share"), `finds.test.ts`.
- **Discover: smoother swipes and sound that starts on (iPhone)** (2026-10-04, the phone test: "feels a little laggy, sometimes the sound is started in the off state"; the earlier fix was the website's only). Sound: `lib/audioSession.ts` `allowClipSound()` (expo-audio `setAudioModeAsync({ playsInSilentMode: true, interruptionMode: 'doNotMix' })`, once, when Discover first shows), so YouTube clips have sound with the silent switch on, as crowd videos always did (expo-video set the same session, which is why it was "sometimes"); the player page's `restart(play, sound)` unmutes before playing a clip that comes on screen (YouTube remembers a mute across every player): always on phones, on the website when the sound memory allows (and that clip's own `muted` is cleared). Lag: taste is worked out once for the app (`TasteProvider` in `state/taste.tsx`, reactions deferred; `useTaste()` reads it, or works it out itself outside the provider) instead of by every clip on screen; `useRankedFeed` no longer re-ranks just because you moved (where you are only decides what stays put) and takes `seen`/`reactions` deferred, so a swipe never waits for a re-rank; `ClipCard` takes `index` with stable `onSkip`/`onNext`, so its `memo` holds; the clip next to the one on screen loads its player after `NEAR_LOAD_DELAY_MS` on phones too (450 ms; 900 on the web). Tests: `autoplay.test.tsx` ("sound"), `youtube.test.ts`; the WebView mock keeps injected commands in `__injected`.

(`LIKE_FAVOURITE` 0.45 similarity to one of `favouritesOf`: artists you love or like or whose sets you liked or saved, best match first, up to 12), "Right in your sound: melodic bass.", or "A first guess. Rate them to sharpen it."). `usePickReasons()` feeds it your live signals. Shown under every must-see and strong pick (For you's ranked list and By day, before set times) and under each stop of the Full day route (after set times), and read out with them. - **Set times from a photo** (2026-10-04, the user: HQ uploads "able to be text or photo"; they chose free reading in the browser): HQ's Data tab has **Read a photo of set times** on the website (`features/admin/readPhoto.web.ts`: Tesseract.js 5.1.1 from jsdelivr, loaded on first use, English, words with their boxes; `readPhoto.ts` in the app says to use the website). Pure `features/admin/photoText.ts`: `linesFromWords` splits a recognised line where words are more than `COLUMN_GAP` (2.5) line heights apart (recognition reads across a grid's columns), `inColumns` rebuilds columns from the narrowest lines up (a line wider than `BANNER` half the page, or reaching across two columns, is a heading such as the day) and reads each column top to bottom, band by band; `tidyPhotoText` drops stray marks, mends times ("1l:OO" → "11:00", "10.30" → "10:30"), turns "FRIDAY SET TIMES" into "FRIDAY" and joins a time on its own line with its act (the line above, else below; never a day or stage heading). The text lands in the box to check, then **Read the text** as before. `STAGE_WORDS` (setlist.ts, now exported) also knows meadow, pod and grounds. Checked end to end in Chromium with real recognition on a drawn three-stage grid (all five sets right); that recognition is a test fixture (`__tests__/fixtures/timetable-grid-words.json`). - **Festival news by email** (2026-10-04, the user: "auto check websites for lineup updates to give me an automatic heads up"; they chose an email through GitHub): `scripts/page-watch.ts` runs in `.github/workflows/website.yml` after the link previews (not on pull requests; the workflow now has `issues: write`): every upcoming festival with `links.website` (catalogue, or published from HQ: the published updates are read through Firestore's REST API with the website key, `published` is public) is fetched and compared with last night's `/page-watch.json` on the live site (kept for a page that can't be read tonight); tonight's goes into `dist/page-watch.json`; news becomes a GitHub issue "Festival news: <date>" (`gh issue create`), which GitHub emails. Pure `src/data/import/pageWatch.ts`: `pageText`, `namesOn` (catalogue artists named on the page as whole words, folded, at least 4 letters, not on the lineup), `hasSetTimes` (says set times / timetable / schedule / running order and lists at least `TIMES_FOR_SET_TIMES` 6 times), `pageFacts` (plus the share picture), `watchNotes` (new names, up to `NAMES_SHOWN` 15; set times appearing; a new share picture; nothing on a first look or a new address), `watchReport`. HQ → Data: **Official page** under the chosen festival → **Watch this page** checks and publishes the festival with its `website` (`websiteUpdate` in `draft.ts`, https only). User guide: "Festival news by email" in `docs/events-import.md`. Tests: `pageWatch.test.ts`, `hqData.test.tsx` ("sets a festival's official page").

Send them to us" while a festival has no set times (before it, or while it's on) → `/send-set-times/[id]` (modal): paste text (`TIP_TEXT_MAX` 20,000) and/or a screenshot (`features/setTimes/shrinkPhoto.ts`: expo-image-manipulator to 1600/1200/900 px JPEG, base64 under `TIP_PHOTO_MAX` 900,000), signed in only; thanks once sent; "already sent" while one is waiting. - **Close friends, step 1: the list** (2026-10-04, the user: a separation between close friends and other friends, for easier chats; they chose 1-to-1 messages, built in two steps): star anyone you follow as a close friend from the star beside Follow on their profile (`features/friends/CloseFriendToggle.tsx`, a switch, "Only you can see your close friends"). The list is kept only on the phone, per account (`state/closeFriends.ts`, `closeFriends.v1`, newest first, up to `CLOSE_FRIENDS_MAX` 30): nobody else sees it and nobody is told. The Friends tab shows **Close friends** first, under the quick actions (also in festival mode): `CloseFriendsRow` (their faces in a glow ring, first name, and the next festival they're going to when they show it, `nextFestivalOf`; only people you still follow and haven't blocked; before you've starred anyone, a one-line hint). - **Close friends, step 2: 1-to-1 messages** (2026-10-04, the user chose 1-to-1 messages for easier chats with close friends): a private chat between two people who follow each other, built on the crew chat (same outbox, retries, "held up by signal" notes, read receipts, reports and take-downs). A chat is a room with the id `dm_<uidA>_<uidB>` (sorted; `directRoom`, `isDirectRoom`, `otherInRoom` in `features/social/models.ts`); the crew chat functions take either kind of room, and the Firebase adapter maps it to `crews/<id>` or `dms/<a>_<b>` (`room()`), with `chat` and `reads` under it. New service calls `openDirectChat(uid)` (makes the chat the first time) and `watchDirectChats`. Rules (`firestore.rules`, `match /dms/{id}`): only the two members read it; made only by one of them, only when they follow each other both ways and neither has blocked the other; a message only from a member while still friends both ways and not blocked by the other; never edited; your own message (or HQ) can be deleted; emulator test "1-to-1 chats". Ways in: **Message** on the profile of someone you follow (`features/friends/openChat.ts`: "You can’t message {name} yet" when they don't follow you back, "No signal" offline), tapping a close friend on the Friends tab (hold for their profile; a dot when they've written), and the **Messages** list on Friends (`MessagesList.tsx`, up to 6, latest first, with the last message and a count of new ones; nobody you've blocked). The chat screen (`src/app/crew-chat/[id].tsx`) shows their face and handle at the top ("just the two of you"), the chat rules for two (`DM_RULES`), "Seen" or "Delivered" under your messages, and "Message {name}". Like crew chats, messages stay when an account is deleted until HQ takes them down. Needs `firebase deploy --only firestore,storage`. Tests: `directMessages.test.tsx`, `friends.test.tsx` (close friends now open the chat).
- **Send into a chat** (2026-10-05, the user chose "Share into messages" to make the new 1-to-1 messages useful): the share button on a festival page, an artist page, a Discover clip (YouTube) and a profile now opens **Send to** (`src/app/send.tsx`, a modal) when you have a profile; signed out it still goes straight to the system share sheet (`features/messages/useSendOrShare.ts`). Share your plan's **Must-sees** gains **Send to a friend** (sends the festival with your top picks). Send to shows the card being sent, an optional line ("Add a message", up to 200 characters) and who to send it to (`features/messages/recipients.ts`, pure `orderRecipients`: the chats you've been talking in, latest first, then close friends, then other friends (people you follow who follow you back, from `followers`), then your other crews; nobody twice or blocked; up to `RECIPIENTS_MAX` 40), each with **Send** → **Sent**; a friend without a chat yet gets one made (`directRoomWith` in `features/friends/openChat.ts`, which also says when they don't follow you back); **More ways to share** opens the system sheet with the page's usual words. Sending counts for the "share" daily quest. Nothing new on the server: a share travels as an ordinary chat message holding the website link (`features/messages/shares.ts`, pure: `shareLink`, `parseShare` (only `afterglowmusic.app` links of the known shapes: `/festival/<id>` with optional `?must=a,b,c` (up to 5), `/artist/<id>`, `/?clip=<id>`, `/u/<handle>`), `shareMessageText` keeps it within `CHAT_MAX` with the link whole, `shareNoun`, `shareRoute`, `shareParams`/`shareFromParams`), so it works offline through the chat outbox, in crew chats and 1-to-1 chats alike, and is a plain link anywhere else. In a chat the message shows as a card (`features/messages/ShareCard.tsx`: kicker, name, one line and picture: festival poster or a glow with its initial, artist photo and top two sounds, the clip's YouTube still, a profile's face; must-sees show overlapping faces and the names; something no longer in the catalogue says "This isn’t on Afterglow any more"), your words above it; tap to open it in the app. Chat previews (Messages list, crew card) read "Sent a festival" / "Sent must-sees" instead of the link. Tests: `features/messages/__tests__/shares.test.ts`, `sendToChat.test.tsx`.
- **Chats in one place** (2026-10-05, the user: an easier way into crew chats): the Friends tab's Messages list is now **Chats** (`features/friends/MessagesList.tsx`): your 1-to-1 chats and every crew's chat together, latest message first, crews you haven't chatted in after; crews show a glow badge with their first letter, the festival beside the name, the last message ("Maya: …", or "Say hi to your crew…") and a count of new ones; 5 shown, then "Show all N chats". In festival mode the crews already shown as chat cards there are left out (`skipCrews`). The Friends tab's glow dot already lights for any unread chat. - **Overnight fixes** (2026-10-05, the user: keep polishing overnight; from three reviews of the last two days' work):
  - *Set times from fans*: HQ marks a fan's tip live only when what it publishes is set times for that same festival (`fromTip` now holds the tip's festival; `Review.setTimes`); a tip whose festival is gone says so instead of being read into the festival already chosen; a screenshot still being read never lands in a tip opened since. Sending with no signal gives up after 20 s (`SET_TIMES_SEND_TIMEOUT_MS`) and says "No signal" instead of sitting on "Sending…". A fan whose last tip went live can send again for that festival (the live record is cleared first; the fake mirrors it). The thank-you isn't repeated on a new phone or after a reinstall (`setScouted`: the first look on a phone notes what's live silently), and a fan is noted when they send so their first thanks still comes. The nightly page watch opens its "Festival news" issue only after tonight's look is live (a new step after the deploy), so a cancelled or failed deploy doesn't email the same news twice; it reads every page of published updates (`nextPageToken`).
  - *Messages*: deleting your account removes your 1-to-1 messages and read marks (the other person keeps theirs), and a chat with a deleted account leaves Chats; nobody but the two named in a chat's id can ask whether it exists (`dms` get rule; emulator check added); the close-friend star waits until your saved list is read, so a quick tap can't write over it; unread counts take the later of this phone's read time and your read mark on the server (no old messages lighting up on the website or a second phone); "Send to" leaves out past chats with people who aren't friends both ways any more; reported 1-to-1 messages say so in HQ; the test double refuses a chat with someone you blocked, like the rules.
  - *Map*: the card strip keeps the chosen festival in view (or goes back to the first) whenever the cards change; Show all, gliding to a festival and "Show me" no longer count as you moving the map, so Show all really shows every card again (the pill sits outside the map's touch area); on the website the lights only redraw when they move, zooming is instant so they never lag behind the map, and swiping the cards chooses a festival there too. Crewmates' stars stop showing as soon as you leave their crew.
  - *Festivals tab*: the Calendar link no longer runs off the right edge.

- **Overnight fixes, round 2** (2026-10-05, from three more reviews: Discover and taste; festivals, plans and reminders; profile, memories and care):
  - *Your data*: Edit profile waits until your profile has been read (`profileStatus` 'loading' / 'ready' / 'failed' and `reloadProfile` in `state/social.tsx`; "Loading your profile…", or "couldn’t be loaded" with Try again), and fills the form when it arrives, so saving can no longer replace your real profile with a blank one (right after launch, after switching account, after a failed read). Look after yourself applies changes made before it finished reading on top of what was saved, and never saves after a read that failed; daily quests, side quests and secrets don't save after a failed read either (and side quests wait for the read before saving). Badge claims are one shared store (`state/badgeClaims.ts`, `claimBadge`, `forgetBadgeClaims`), so a claim reaches your glow level at once. The start-up tidy-up of Memories never removes the files of a memory being kept.
  - *Look after yourself*: with the Face ID lock on, the card on a festival page and on Tonight says "Private. Tap to open" instead of what's running; leaving the app locks it again; a phone without Face ID or a passcode set up (`isEnrolledAsync`) just opens rather than locking you out.
  - *Reminders*: set reminders are timed by the clock on that day (a clock change during a festival no longer moves them an hour), every reminder's id carries its time (a new time zone reschedules them), and set reminders stop when you stop going.
  - *Dates*: a festival with dates but no listed days is on for all of them (`isLive` uses its dates); the last night's sets after midnight count as happening now, not over, until 6 AM (`NIGHT_ENDS_HOUR`, `stillLastNight` in `festivals/when.ts`, used by `nextRun` and `whenRank`).
  - *Discover*: the first clip plays when festival mode switching on or off changes the feed; a festival's feed keeps your place when the catalogue refreshes (`useRankedFeed` compares the focus by id); passive watch signal never lifts an artist you skipped or passed on above where that put them (`engine.ts`: spill from what you said and spill with watching worked out apart; the lower wins for an artist with a negative explicit signal).
  - *Also*: Tonight between festivals shows your next one (with its countdown) or "Find a festival"; crewmates who share a first name are told apart ("Sam K.", "Sam R.": `distinctNames` in `crews/together.ts`).
  - Tests: `when.test.ts` (after midnight, no listed days), `engine.test.ts` (watching never outweighs a skip), `together.test.ts` (`distinctNames`), `tonight.test.tsx` (between festivals).
- **Overnight fixes, round 3** (2026-10-05, from reviews of crews and social, and of HQ, sprouts and My Sound; plus the rest of round 2):
  - *Crews*: the crew-updates outbox only sends once your crews have really been read (`confirmed` from `useCrews`: a list still loading, or emptied by a failed read, no longer throws queued check-ins away); outbox items carry the account that wrote them (`OutboxItem.uid`) and only that account sends or sees them (older items without one are held until they expire); crew-only videos you sent are kept per account (`crewVideos.v1` keys are now `<uid>:<crewId>`; older keys are left alone); "Latest from the crew" leaves out people you've blocked; friend adds waiting for signal stop when you switch account mid-send.
  - *HQ updates*: a published update only ever adds (`asPatch` in `data/merge.ts`: "replace" becomes "add"; HQ's check refuses such a file with a plain line), artists already in the catalogue keep their own record (photo, colour, sounds), and a festival already there takes only its lineup, set times, days, stages, artist days and links from an update, so later fixes to its dates or poster still reach everyone; phones read the newest 200 updates (applied oldest first), so a new one always arrives.
  - *Plans*: an artist who plays twice has both sets in the timetable, clashes and Now (`buildSchedule` makes a timed pick per set); the festival clock's night now runs 7 AM to 7 AM (`DAY_BREAK`; was noon), so a daytime festival's 11:30 set is at the start of its day, not the end of the night; `NIGHT_ENDS_HOUR` is 7 to match.
  - *Also*: sprouts check the giver on the server (an edited link can't put your own gift in your garden); My Sound's artist sheet has a close button (`SheetClose`) and its dimmed backdrop is a labelled button; a video's comments that couldn't be read for want of signal say so with a Try again (not "isn't available any more"); labels for "Browse festivals" and "Seen the set times?"; a little space under a festival's official links.

Notifications that arrive while the bell is open (its refresh, a new follower) get the "New" glow too before they're marked read.
