Profile (`/u/<handle>`) and video (`/shared/<id>`) links ask a signed-out visitor to sign in, with a button. A shared video starts muted in a browser (browsers refuse to start sound by themselves) with "Tap for sound", and a tap pauses and plays. Unknown links show "Page not found" (`+not-found.tsx`) rather than the developer page. Text fields show a soft focus ring for keyboard users. Dialogs move keyboard focus to their first button, and their backdrop is no longer a button wrapped around the buttons. A must-sees link (`/festival/<id>?must=a,b`) shows "Their must-sees" at the top of the festival, and so does tapping a must-sees card in a chat.

- **Works with no signal (2026-10-05, branch `claude/no-signal`, awaiting phone testing)**: an audit of everything used at a festival, for a cold start in airplane mode and for losing signal mid-use, then fixes for the gaps.
  - *Audit*: already fine with no signal, all on the phone: festival mode and its Lineup tab and camera, your plan, full-day route, timetable and Now (catalogue kept in `catalogFile.v1` with a timeout, plus the bundled file), Look after yourself (`care.v1`, never sent), memories and camera, quests, finds and stickers (`finds.v1` outbox), sprouts (`sprouts.v1` outbox), friend adds, and sending check-ins, care check-ins and chat (outboxes). Gaps: on a cold start with no signal your profile couldn't be read, so check-ins, "I'm OK" and the crew chat said you weren't ready; your crews, crewmates' names, their stars, latest check-ins, meeting spots and the chat were empty or "Couldn't open this crew" (Firestore keeps nothing between launches on phones); a crewmate whose profile read failed stayed "Crewmate" all session; Firestore answers a query with no signal from its empty memory, which looked like "no crews" (and would have let the outbox drop waiting check-ins once signal returned) and like "no HQ updates" (wiping kept live patches, set times among them); reads had no timeout; the festival map's streets (Apple/Google tiles) don't load without signal.
  - *What changed*: pure `features/offline/lastKnown.ts` (`cleanLastKnown`, `keep` merging per crew, `forgetAccount`, `keptAt`, `whenRead`, `lastUpdatedLine`; `LAST_KNOWN_KEEP_MS` 30 days, `KEPT_MESSAGES_MAX` 150, `KEPT_UPDATES_MAX` 100, `KEPT_ACCOUNTS_MAX` 5) and the shared store `state/lastKnown.ts` (AsyncStorage `lastKnown.v1`, per account, `writable` flag, saves batched once per tick; `rememberLastKnown`, `lastKnownFor`, `useLastKnown`, `forgetLastKnownAccount` on deleting the account). Kept from every successful read: your profile, blocks and follows (`state/social.tsx`; a kept profile is never published over the real one, and everything is read again when signal returns), your crews (`state/crews.tsx`: `keptAt`, unconfirmed until read, read again on signal back so the outbox only sends against real crews), crew stars and crewmates' profiles (also `features/crews/useCrewPage.ts`, which opens a crew you're in from the kept copy and reloads on signal), crew updates and meeting spots (`state/crewUpdates.tsx`: `readAt`/`readLive`, read straight away when signal returns), and each chat's newest messages (`state/crewChat.tsx`: `readFor`; `loaded` is true with no signal so nothing spins forever). The Firebase adapter: reads give up after `READ_TIMEOUT_MS` 12 s as no signal (`readDoc`), queries that come back from Firestore's memory count as no signal (`serverDocs`: crews, invites, stars, updates, follows, live patches), and empty snapshots from memory are skipped (`nothingYet`: chats, read receipts, 1-to-1 chat list).
  - *What you see*: `features/offline/LastUpdated.tsx`, a quiet line with a small dot (dim with no signal, the purple when only stale): "No signal, showing what you had · Last updated 9:41 PM", "Last updated 9:41 PM" when signal is there but the read didn't get through, or "No signal. This fills in once you’re back online." when nothing was kept yet. On the crew page, Where's the crew (Tonight and Friends), the crew chat (instead of "No messages yet"), and Friends in festival mode (instead of "Start a crew" when your crews couldn't be read). The festival map says "No signal · the streets may not load, the festivals still show" on phones. The app-wide pill now reads "No signal · your plan, crew and lineups still work".
  - *Not covered*: 1-to-1 chats list on a cold start (the chats open from a crewmate's profile once read), Discover clips (YouTube needs signal), sharing to Discover and comments (they say so already).
  - *Tests*: `features/offline/__tests__/lastKnown.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/noSignal.test.tsx` (used with signal then opened in airplane mode: crew, check-in, meeting spot and chat with the line; "I'm OK" waits and reaches the crew on signal back; a read that fails with signal keeps what you had; nothing kept doesn't claim an empty chat; the map's line). `fakeSocial` has `network.noReads` (reads fail too, like a cold start in airplane mode); `test/afterEnv.ts` resets the store.
- **Faster start and smoother scrolling** (2026-10-05): nothing looks different; the same answers come out faster.
  - *How it was measured*: the website build (`npx expo export --platform web`) in Chromium with the processor slowed 4x and a phone-sized window, the hosted catalogue swapped for one the size the nightly imports make (2,500 artists, 150 festivals, 2,000 clips, 4,000 sounds-alike pairs, 1.3 MB), median of 5 runs; time until Discover's header shows, and the main thread's long tasks (`PerformanceObserver`); a Chrome CPU profile mapped back to the source files. "Returning" also has the saved catalogue, 200 reactions, 400 clips seen and 3 festivals going.
  - *What was slow*: (1) the taste engine's spill-over asked every artist about every other artist (2,500 x 2,500, millions of steps) each time taste was worked out; (2) taste was worked out four or five times at start, because Social, Notices (through `useLoves` and the recaps) sat above `TasteProvider` and each worked out their own; (3) the feed's last page (`FeedEnd`) counted every festival's clips with `lineup.includes` for each clip (12 million steps) on every swipe; (4) every festival row and clip card that asked for the taste built its own lookup of the 4,000 sounds-alike pairs.
  - *Changes*: `features/taste/engine.ts` spills only from artists with a signal (same order, so identical numbers); `TasteProvider` moved up in `app/_layout.tsx` to just inside `RatingsProvider` (everything it reads is above it), so the whole app shares one taste; `useTaste` builds the pair lookup only where the taste is worked out (`state/taste.tsx`); `similarityWith` keeps pairs in a map per artist (no new text per lookup) and `soundSimilarity` adds the same tags in the same order without building a set (`features/taste/similarity.ts`); `lineupClips` uses a set and `defaultFocus`/`otherFestivalsWithClips` count clips per artist once (`features/festivals/links.ts`); `FeedEnd` remembers its suggestions between swipes; `useFestivalSummary` remembers each festival's clip count. A check against the old code on the big catalogue with 277 reactions and 40 ratings gave exactly the same taste, similarities and festival picks (the engine alone: about 800 ms down to 210 ms in Node).
  - *Before → after*: new user, Discover shown 13.9 s → 3.5 s, main thread blocked 13.9 s (longest 10.9 s) → 2.3 s (longest 0.8 s); returning, 30.3 s → 3.8 s, blocked 29.5 s (longest 19.5 s) → 2.6 s (longest 0.7 s); opening Festivals 1.2 s → 0.6 s (returning 1.3 s → 0.9 s); back to Discover 1.6–1.7 s → 0.3–0.4 s; My Sound unchanged (about 0.65 s, mostly drawing). Bundles unchanged: iOS 7.6 MB Hermes bytecode (5.9 MB of JavaScript, of which Reanimated 0.74 MB, React Native 0.56 MB, Expo Router 0.45 MB, Firebase 0.6 MB), web 6 MB.
  - *Considered, not done*: turning on the React Compiler (`experiments.reactCompiler`; the code already follows its lint rules, but the tests don't run through it, so it would change every screen untested); Expo Router's lazy routes (`asyncRoutes`, still experimental, and a brief blank on each first visit); loading Firebase, maps or the camera later (in Expo Go every route file is loaded at start anyway, to check it, so only a store build would gain).
  - Tests: `links.test.ts` (a doubled lineup counts once), `similarity.test.ts` (a source's pairs both ways round, last one wins; sound tags alike either way round).
- **Launch decisions (2026-10-05, the user's choices)**: *Demo festivals hidden*: the invented Solstice 2026 and Horizon 2027 never reach the app, the website or the nightly scripts (`DEMO_FESTIVALS` and `shippedDemo()` in `data/bundled.ts`); the demo artists stay, and the demo clips (all filmed at those two) drop out with them, so the bundled catalogue is the real festivals only and Discover's clips come from the hosted file each night. `buildCatalog` takes `{ needClips }` (default true, so a broken hosted file still can't empty Discover); the bundled build passes false. Tests (Jest sets `JEST_WORKER_ID`) keep the demo festivals, which the golden and screen tests are built on. *18+*: the timing guide stays, so the app is rated 18+; sign-up says "I'm 18 or older", the Terms and Privacy Policy say 18 (`LEGAL_UPDATED` October 5, 2026); `docs/app-store.md` and `docs/app-store/listing.md` record the decision. Tests: `data/__tests__/shipped.test.ts`.
- **Play fixes: glow, stickers, quests, sprouts (2026-10-05)**, from a review. *Glow level* (`quests/LevelSync.tsx`): the level is worked out from what's on the phone, so it's now only sent when it's higher than what the account already shows (read fresh with `standingOf`); a new phone, reinstall or the website (level 1) never lowers or removes it, only the "Show my glow level" switch removes it, and it's never sent to the support account (`isSupportAccount`). *Throwback glow*: a claimed throwback badge (`past:` key) counts its 75 glow only while you still have that throwback (`badgeGlowCount` in `quests/daily.ts`, fed by `useThrowbacks` in `useDailyQuests`, which now waits for memories to load), so re-tagging a video's past festival can't earn glow again and again; other claims count as before. `useBadges` shares one read per person between screens. *Quest day*: `turnOver` keeps the book as it is when the clock or time zone goes back a day (today's done quests can't be earned twice). *Stickers*: `SocialService.myFinds(max)` (Firebase: `finds` where `uid` == you, already allowed by the rules) brings finds kept on your account back to this phone once a session when signed in with signal (`withAccountFinds` in `finds/finds.ts`: found and sent, earliest scan, each sticker read for its name, creature and hunt); `adopt` keeps the account's own find or the better state (found > waiting > ended > gone) and the earliest scan, so a signed-out re-scan never replaces a confirmed find. Taking a sticker down in HQ now marks it (`takenDown`, `takenDownAt`, server time) instead of deleting it: scans made before still count when sent up to 3 days later, later ones read "isn't an Afterglow sticker any more" (`checked`), HQ's list and print sheet leave it out (rules: HQ may mark it once; `findOpen` in `firestore.rules`; emulator: 96 checks pass). *Sprouts*: a gift refused by Afterglow (not just no signal, via `socialErrorKind` or a timeout) is given up after `GIFT_REFUSED_MAX` 5 refusals (`giftTried` in `sprouts/outbox.ts`), its seed given back (`seedGifts`), and the garden and give page say "Couldn’t be sent, so your seed is back"; taking back a gift stops after the same refusals or `not-found`; tries wait 15 s (the backend's own write wait is 12 s). Tests: `finds.test.ts`, `daily.test.ts`, `sprouts.test.ts`, `stickers.test.tsx`, `glowLevel.test.tsx`. **Needs a Firestore rules deploy.**
- **HQ fixes (2026-10-05)** (from a review of Afterglow HQ; needs a Firestore rules and indexes deploy):
  - *Accounts*: a person's privileges panel opens only once the "With privileges" list has been read (before, it could start blank and saving Plus wiped an existing flair, or the other way round), and saving writes only the privilege that changed; a list that couldn't be read says so with Try again instead of "No one yet".
  - *Updates carry only what they change*: "Watch this page" publishes just the festival's id, name, dates and `website`; a set list for a festival already on Afterglow publishes only additions (`"add": { lineup, days, stages, sets }`: new acts and acts given days, new days and stages, the sets read, each replacing that act's set that day), or with Replace the lineup, the whole lineup and its `sets`. `parseCatalogFile` notes `festivalParts` (which parts each festival wrote, and its additions) when a festival lacks its lineup, days or stages or has `add`; `asPatch` then changes only those parts and joins the additions (`withAdditions` in `data/merge.ts`), links change one by one (a website never removes the tickets link), and a partial update for a festival no longer on Afterglow is left out. Older, whole updates read as before. So later catalogue fixes show through, and Take down undoes only that update. HQ's check reads a draft through `asPatch` like the app, the summary counts additions, and "Copy as a catalogue file" gives the festival whole as it will look.
  - *Support*: the rules take a message's `email` only if it's your sign-in email and its `handle` only if it's your profile's; every reply is kept (`replies`, at most `SUPPORT_REPLIES_MAX` 20; `reply`/`repliedAt` stay the latest; older messages show their one reply; `repliesOf`), shown in HQ and in Contact support; HQ's email link is built with `mailtoLink` (only an address that looks like one, encoded); your support messages are deleted with your account (the rules let you delete your own).
  - *Reports*: filed with `status: 'open'` at the server's time (the rules require both); HQ reads every open report plus the newest of all (index status + createdAt), so old or flooded reports can't hide an open one; a reported comment has Read it (`reportedComment`), a reported profile Look at it (opens `/u/<handle>`).
  - *Set times from fans*: the rules take only a festival id like the app's (`^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,64}$`); HQ lists them newest first (index status + createdAt desc) with Show older; a fan is thanked (and an upload marked done) only while the box still holds their text: choosing another festival, Use the text, reading a photo or emptying the box forgets where it came from.
  - *Privacy*: the policy (Oct 5) says Afterglow support can read crew chats and 1-to-1 messages and does so only for a report or a safety issue, mentions 1-to-1 messages, and that support messages and 1-to-1 messages go with your account; the rule comments match (read access unchanged).
  - Not done: Plus end dates in the rules, a subresource-integrity check for Tesseract, a redesign of the 200-update cap.
  - Tests: `file.test.ts` (updates written in part), `setlist.test.ts` (only what changes, later fixes show, take-down undoes only one), `admin.test.tsx` (comment and profile reports, open reports never hidden, every reply kept, `mailtoLink`, deleted with the account), `hqData.test.tsx` (waits for the list, saves only what changed), `fanSetTimes.test.tsx` (not thanked for another source); emulator: 97 checks pass.
- **Memories and camera fixes (2026-10-05, branch `claude/fix-memories`, awaiting review, a Firestore rules deploy and phone testing)**: fixes from a review of Memories, the camera and sharing to Discover.
  - *Never losing a video*: `state/memories.tsx` only writes `memories.v1` after it has been read back (a `writable` ref, as in the other stores); a failed read is tried again (2 s doubling to a minute, and when the app comes back) and `hydrated` stays false until then. A list that can't be read whole is first copied untouched to `memoriesDamaged.v1`, and while that copy exists nothing in the folder is ever tidied. The launch tidy-up (`orphanFiles` in `features/memories/store.ts`, with `memoryIdTime`) only takes files no memory points to, never one named after a memory's id, and only once its name says it's more than `ORPHAN_MIN_AGE_MS` (a day) old; names that don't say when are kept. The crew outbox waits for your memories to be read before taking a queued video for deleted (`crewUpdates.tsx`), and waits for a Discover share still waiting for signal.
  - *A recording is kept at once*: the camera keeps a finished clip straight away as an untagged memory (`keepNow`) and opens `memory-tag?id=…&fresh=1`, which tags that memory ("New memory"; Cancel asks "Discard this video?" and deletes it). If the file can't be copied it falls back to the old draft. While the clip is being kept the camera says "Keeping your video…" and the record and Photos buttons wait; closing meanwhile keeps it in Memories with a Tag badge and opens nothing. Finding where you are gives up after `PLACE_TIMEOUT_MS` 6 s (`device.ts`).
  - *Share to Discover outbox*: `features/social/shareOutbox.ts` (pure: `queueShare`, `unqueueShare`, `shareTried`, `sharesOf`, `shareRetryDelay` 15 s doubling to 2 min, `cleanShareOutbox`, `SHARE_OUTBOX_MAX` 50) and `state/social.tsx` (AsyncStorage `shareOutbox.v1`, memory ids per account, written only after it's read): `share(memory)` keeps it first, then uploads; with no signal it returns `'waiting'` (no alert) and goes by itself, oldest first, when signal is back, the app returns or the retry timer fires, with the memory's latest tags. A refusal lets that one go with "Couldn’t share it to Discover" and the plain reason. `useSocial().waiting`; Memories tiles show "Waiting" (or the upload %), the memory page "Waiting for signal", and the tagging sheet says it's on its way.
  - *Uploads and removals*: the throwback limit is checked before the upload starts (and again just before the batch; the rules stay the guard); `useThrowbackShares` no longer reads an unknown count as 0; Share to profile checks the limit before asking. Delete on the memory page is off while it uploads, and an upload that finishes after its memory was deleted is taken down again (`wasRemoved`). `unshareMemory` removes the files first (only "already gone" counts as removed) and the doc last, so a file is never left without its doc.
  - *Rules*: a shared memory's `videoUrl` and `thumbUrl` must be Firebase Storage download links (`https://firebasestorage.googleapis.com/v0/b/<bucket>/o/memories%2F<uid>%2F<id>.<ext>?alt=media&token=…`) for this owner's own files of this memory. Emulator: 97 checks pass. **Needs a Firestore rules deploy.**
  - *Small*: Save on the tagging sheet is off while saving; retagging a shared memory says the Discover copy keeps the tags it was shared with; a shared video that couldn't be loaded says "Couldn’t load this video" with Try again instead of calling it removed. `fakeSocial`'s share, unshare and throwback count fail with `network.down`, and `sharedMemory` with `network.noReads`.
  - Tests: `features/memories/__tests__/store.test.ts`, `features/social/__tests__/shareOutbox.test.ts`, `state/__tests__/memories.test.tsx`, and in `camera`, `shareToDiscover`, `pastFestivals`, `likes` and `crewUpdates` screen tests.
- **Friends, chats and crews redesign (2026-10-05, the user asked for "a lot more thought put into the visual design of friends, chats, and crews", taking cues from the profile orbit)**: *Your friends* is now an orbit like the profile's (`features/friends/FriendsOrbit.tsx`, `FriendsCircle.tsx`): you in the middle with a glow, your close friends on the inner ring (56 pt, purple ring, named, a dot when they've written; tap to message, hold for their profile), everyone else you follow on a wider, fainter outer ring (tap for their profile); the whole orbit turns once every 140 s as one, so faces never cross names (still with reduced motion). It replaces the separate Close friends row and the Your friends faces row (which showed the same people twice); a line under it says "4 close friends · 10 friends", and before you've starred anyone a hint says how to bring people into the inner ring. One hook reads your friends for both the orbit and Recent activity (`useFriendsCircle.ts`: close friends first, then others, up to 30). *Chats* is one glass card like a messages app (`MessagesList.tsx`): crew chats show the crew's picture, the time of the last message on the right ("4m", "3h", "Tue"; `chatTime`), the festival under a crew's name, a glow ring and badge when there's something new. *A crew's picture* (`CrewOrb.tsx`): up to four of its people's faces round a small ring of light, "+N" for the rest, the crew's first letter before faces load; used in Chats, on Your crews cards (with the festival's countdown and new messages as chips), in the crew chat's header and at the top of the crew page. Section headings are moonlight headlines with their links on the right (`SectionHeader.tsx`). Pure layout in `features/friends/orbit.ts` (`splitOrbit`, `ringPlaces`, `crewOrbPlaces`, `chatTime`, `nextFestivalOf`), tested in `features/friends/__tests__/orbit.test.ts`; `CloseFriendsRow.tsx` is gone.
- **My Sound map: clearer groups, more room, no shine (2026-10-05, the user: the map "feels a little tight" and "grouping of styles isn't very visually available"; drop the lean card from the map and the shine on bubbles)**: `computeLayout` (`sound-map/layout.ts`) now groups artists by their main sound: each sound is a cluster round you in ring order, in a wedge sized by how many it holds, its strongest artists at its heart and the rest on a sunflower spiral (`GOLDEN`); the relaxation keeps `GAP` 22 (was 16) between bubbles and a wider `GROUP_SPACE` 30 between different sounds, so channels show between groups; each sound's name sits just beyond its own cluster rather than round the whole map. Bubble size still shows the match. `OrbGlass` (`Orb.tsx`) keeps only the atmosphere ring in the sound's colour (no glint, sparkle, shading or glass edge). The Map view no longer shows the "Leaning …" card (it's on Overview). The golden test's map coordinates were updated on purpose for the new layout (taste, feed and plans unchanged).
- **Discover clips lost and kept safe (2026-10-05, the user: "EDC currently has no clips but had a ton before")**: the 16:16 UTC run's YouTube step found a video another artist's clip already used (a b2b set), so the app's check refused the file ("Two clips share the id"); the step only warned, the site went out with no YouTube clips, and every later run carried forward from that empty file (346 clips → 2). Now `scripts/youtube-clips.ts` uses each video once (carried clips, catalogue clips and new finds; `pickLiveSet` skips used videos), takes several published files (later ones only fill artists the first has no clip for), and `CLIP_SEARCHES=0` searches none. The workflow searches only on the nightly run and "Run workflow" (merges keep the day's YouTube allowance), reruns the step without searches if it fails, and stops before the deploy if that fails too. A "Run workflow" input, `restore_clips_from`, takes an earlier Hosting release's version id, clones it to a short-lived `clips-restore` channel and carries its clips forward (`docs/events-import.md`, "Getting lost clips back"). Also: the shipped demo clips filed under the hidden demo festivals move to a real festival whose lineup has the artist (`shippedDemo`, `bundled.ts`), and the EDMTrain import checks its file with `needClips: false` (clips come in the YouTube step), since after hiding the demo festivals it was refused for having no clips.
- **Discover opens on everything; crew row only at the festival (2026-10-05, the user: "Discover should default to explore all", and the check-in row isn't useful outside festival mode)**: `startFocus(stored)` (`discover/focus.ts`) now opens on Your mix every time; a festival you pick lasts while the app is open (and a festival page's "Discover the lineup" still opens it), but no longer comes back next launch, and neither the festival you're going to nor the one with most clips is chosen for you. Following, a sound or Tonight, picked last, still come back. The crew stories row with "Check in" (`CrewStories`) shows on Discover only in festival mode; checking in is still on the crew page and the Friends tab. Tests: `discover/__tests__/focus.test.ts`, Discover in `screens.test.tsx`; `crewUpdates` and `crewCare` screen tests now run in a festival mode preview.
- **What to discover, redesigned (2026-10-05, the user: "this could have a better ui overall")**: `src/app/discover-focus.tsx` now leads with big tiles (Tonight full width on a festival night; Your mix, with three faces from the clips, and Following side by side; a check badge and glow on the one playing), sound chips with each sound's colour dot (`SOUND_COLOR`) tinting the chosen one, festivals with clips as cards over their headliners' faces (`FestivalCover`, `coverPhotos`, 4 faces) with a Going pill (going ones first), festivals you're going to with no clips yet in a dashed "Clips on the way" list instead of greyed-out rows, and the crowd-videos and next-clip switches in one grouped card under "Playing" (moonlight switch thumbs). Accessibility labels unchanged.
- **Friends tab reordered; a crew is its chat (2026-10-05, the user: orbit "slightly more condensed", then the buttons, then crews, "auto open crew group to chat. From chat we can open more crew settings")**: outside festival mode the tab is now the orbit (300 pt tall, was 340; inner ring 66 pt from the middle, was 82; your face 66 pt), then the round buttons (Find, My code, Scan on phones, Sprout, Invite), then crew invites, then **Crews**: each crew's card shows its last chat message (or the latest check-in) and new messages, and opens the crew's chat (`/crew-chat/[id]`); Start a crew below. The crew chat's header has a ••• "Crew settings" button (as well as tapping the crew's name) for the crew page. `MessagesList directOnly` then lists only 1-to-1 chats, titled Messages, so crews aren't listed twice; then friend adds, activity, people you may know. Festival mode's Friends tab is unchanged. Tests: `crewChat.test.tsx`, `directMessages.test.tsx`.
- **My Sound map: easier to take in and move around (2026-10-05, the user: the map "is even harder to move around and understand than before")**: the sound groups had spread the map to about four screens across, and it opened zoomed in on a few bubbles. Now `computeLayout` packs tighter (`MIN_R` 26 / `MAX_R` 64, `GAP` 12, `GROUP_SPACE` 16, groups nearer you: `DIST_K` 0.7, `RHO_K` 0.95), the map shows at most 26 artists (`MAP_MAX`, was 40), and it opens on the whole map (`frameMap`: centred on everyone, zoom 0.38 to 1.1). Zoomed out, each sound's strongest artist and your top 3 keep their names (`leaders`, `TOP_NAMED`), grown back against the zoom so they stay readable; everyone else's name shows from `DETAIL_MIN` 0.72 (or 1.4 × the framing) in. Each sound's name now sits on top of its own group (moved up, else below, until it's clear of every bubble, with room for being drawn up to 2.2 × bigger zoomed out) and is bolder. Double-tap zooms in on that spot when zoomed out (every name shows), and back to the whole map when zoomed in; pinch can go a little past the framing. The golden test's map numbers (positions, sizes, leaders, sound names) were updated on purpose; taste, feed and plans are unchanged. Tests: `sound-map/__tests__/fit.test.ts`.
- **App themes, profile backgrounds and favourite artists, brought in (2026-10-05; the user couldn't find the themes: they were built on 2026-10-03 on `claude/wizardly-fermi-n849a2` and never merged)**: merged into today's app with every clash resolved (the work itself is described in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`, "Profile backgrounds" and "App themes": Settings → Look → App theme, and Edit profile → Background). Screens built since then had hard-coded purple and night shades; they now go through `glowA`/`glowDeepA`/`nightA`/`raisedA` so they follow the theme (Friends, What to discover, My Sound, Tonight, festival and artist pages, the route, crew pictures; the multi-colour gradients stay). Profiles are written by one function, `profileToDoc` (socials as their own fields; `profileFields` is an alias), and the socials join the "newer fields" left out when the live database is still on older rules (`withOlderRules`), so saving a profile never fails for want of a rules deploy. When that happens Edit profile says "Saved, without your socials" instead of dropping them quietly. The CI rules deploy has failed on every run since it was added (the key lacks Service Usage Consumer), so the rules for socials (and much since) only go live when the user deploys them or adds the roles (`docs/web-deploy.md`). Rules emulator: 101 checks pass.

Now, when opening a chat is refused although you follow each other (checked on the phone and with `following(them)`), `openDirect` (`state/crewChat.tsx`) throws `MessagesOffError` (`features/friends/openChat.ts`) and the alert says "Messages aren’t ready yet … its database needs an update first". The fix itself is deploying the rules (`docs/web-deploy.md`). Test: `directMessages.test.tsx`.

Now merged: **`claude/cool-carson-gdah6q`**: festival invites link to the festival; crew, friend-link and profile pages wait for sign-in to be read before asking for it; a profile opened signed out says "@handle is on Afterglow" with Sign in or join and Look around first; Share image works on the website too (`lib/sharePicture.web.ts` with `html-to-image`: the browser's share sheet on phones, a download on computers; `sharePicture.ts` on phones), so the plan card's picture (including must-sees) is offered on the website; the crew route shares as a picture card. **`claude/partner-links`**: places to stay, partner tickets and Get ready shop links with commission notices, all hidden until real links are added (`config/partners.ts`, `features/partners/partners.ts`, catalogue fields; Privacy Policy line only when partners exist; see `docs/money.md`).

The step now deploys `firestore:rules,firestore:indexes` first and `storage` separately, says which failed, and fails if either does. Storage needs the key to have **Cloud Storage for Firebase Admin** as well (`docs/web-deploy.md`). The same run restored the 346 lost Discover clips from release `aaeb73690b23c50f`.

Your profile background (animated backdrop, else cover photo) glows behind the Friends orbit (`OrbitBackdrop` in `components/ProfileCover.tsx`, full screen width, 380 pt, faded into the night top and bottom). Open as the chat opens: × (fold), "I’m here" (one tap, glowing), "I’m OK" / "I’m OK now", "Can’t find you" and "Need help" (both asked first, the same alerts as the Friends tab), "More…" (`/check-in` for stage, photo, own words), then the quick messages after a hairline. Everything goes through the crew updates outbox (`checkIn`, `sendCare`, `callLost`), marked with the time you tapped; a caption above the row says "Sent “I’m here” · 9:41 PM" (gone after `SENT_NOTE_MS` = 6 s once through), "Sending…", saved for signal (amber) or refused (red). `CheckInPanel.tsx` now shares `useCareActions` and `useCheckInStatus` with it (no change to the panel). - **My Sound list (2026-10-05, the user: "another tab for list view of all artists ranked with the ability to search and filter similar to the map … list view can even be the second tab")**: My Sound now switches between Overview, List and Map (in that order; `view=list` opens it, like `view=map`; screen readers hear "Ranked list of artists"). The list (`features/sound-map/SoundList.tsx`, a virtualized FlatList with memoized rows) ranks everyone the map draws from for the chosen scope (all artists, or a festival's lineup and day: the same festival chips and state as the map, so switching views keeps the festival), not just the map's 26: artists the app knows something about (`informed`) are numbered best match first (live taste, ties A to Z; the top 3 numbers glow), with photo, name, your love/like heart, their top two sounds (a sound's share ≥ 0.5, or their strongest one) with the first sound's colour dot, "Following", "Not for you" dimmed like `ArtistRow`, and match % in tabular figures. The rest wait behind "Show N more you haven’t reacted to yet" and are then marked New (no number). Search works like the map's (name or sound, "dub" finds dubstep, reusing `mapSearch`) and reaches everyone; sound chips (All sounds plus each sound the listed artists carry, coloured dots and counts; `FilterChips` gained an optional `dot`) and a Best match / A–Z toggle; rows open `/artist/[id]` with the festival when scoped. Choosing "Cover photo" still shows the photo alone; removing the photo shows the animation alone. No change to what's saved. Tests: `backdrops.test.tsx`.
- **Shows on artist pages (2026-10-05, branch `claude/artist-shows`, awaiting review and the first nightly run; the user: "I'd also like to see individual shows under the artists profile", from EDMTrain)**: `scripts/import-edmtrain.ts` now also fetches `festivalInd=false` events for the next `SHOW_RULES.daysAhead` 183 days in one request (failure only skips shows, with a `::warning::`; festivals still publish) and adds them as the catalogue key `shows` (`[{ id: "edm-<eventId>", date, name?, venue, location?, place?, link, artists: [ids] }]`), shrinking the show cap until they take at most `SHOWS_MAX_CHARS` 400,000 characters and the file stays under 90% of `MAX_FILE_CHARS`. Pure `data/import/edmtrainShows.ts` `edmtrainShows(events, artists, today, SHOW_RULES)`: only artists on Afterglow (curated plus the festival import's), matched by `fold`, b2b acts split with `b2bParts` when not on Afterglow themselves; skips festivals, livestreams, past or far-off events and ones without id, https link, venue or day; name dropped when it's just the venue; place rounded to 0.001; soonest first, kept while any artist on it has fewer than `perArtist` 6, `maxArtists` 8 per show, `maxShows` 1500. Model `Show` (`artistIds`), `CatalogSnapshot.shows`; `file.ts` `parseShow` (https link, real day, venue, at least one artist id; `MAX_SHOWS` 3000, `MAX_SHOW_ARTISTS` 12; one plain problem for the bad ones); `mergeSnapshots` joins by id (file wins); `validateSnapshot` quietly drops unknown artists (and shows left with none), sorts by date; `Catalog.shows` and `showsOf(artistId, from?)`. Later nightly steps keep the key (they spread the file). Artist page: "Upcoming shows" after Playing (date block month/day/weekday, event or venue, place, "with X and Y" in glow for other Afterglow artists, ↗; opens the EDMTrain event page; first `SHOWS_SHOWN` 5, "See all N" / "Show fewer"; credit "Show info from EDMTrain ↗"; hidden when none), words in pure `features/artists/shows.ts`. EDMTrain's answer for `festivalInd=false` couldn't be checked from the cloud session. The top of a profile no longer fades to solid night (which left a visible band against the night sky's purple haze) but to transparent, with `components/FadeMask` (`@react-native-masked-view/masked-view` on phones, a CSS `mask-image` on the website; stops from pure `components/fadeStops.ts`), so it melts into the page. Pure `features/plan/hours.ts`: `DayHours` (`arrive?`, `leave?`, `breaks: { from, to, label? }[]`, minutes on the festival-day clock like `toMinutes`: before 7 AM runs past 24:00, so 1:00 AM is 1500), `cleanHours` (arrive before leave, breaks clipped inside, sorted, overlaps merged, at most `MAX_BREAKS` 4, labels trimmed to `BREAK_LABEL_MAX` 24; undefined = all day), `hoursLine` ("Arriving 4:00 PM · Leaving 2:00 AM · Dinner 7–8 PM", three or more breaks counted), `hoursSaid` (in full, for screen readers), `timeRange`, `clockLabel`, `dayRange` (times from `BEFORE_FIRST` 120 min before the day's first set to `AFTER_LAST` 60 min after its last), `stepTime` (`HOURS_STEP` 15), `newBreak` (`NEW_BREAK` 60 min, halfway through your time there, labels `BREAK_LABELS` Dinner / Rest / Meet up), `freeMinutes`, `missedBy`, `missedLine` ("SUBTRONICS plays at 12:15 AM, after you leave. Stay later?", "…before you arrive. Come earlier?", "…during dinner. Move it?"), `fixLabel` / `makeRoom` (stay until the set's end, arrive at its start, or remove the breaks in its way). `RouteInput.hours` (by day): `dayRoute` adds cuts at your arrive, leave and break edges, plans only between arrive and leave, and during your break the only choice is a break (a set across one is cut around it, so it reads "First 30 min" then "Last 30 min"); those stops are `{ kind: 'break', yours: true, label }`, never merged with a plain break; a starred set with no minutes left in your hours is reported in `DayRoute.missed` (time order) instead of vanishing. No hours for a day = exactly the old route (all the old route tests pass unchanged; a test compares). Used by `useFestivalPlan` (Full day, Tonight, Live now, plan card and shared route text) and `ReminderSync` (set reminders follow the route in your hours). Not used by the crew route (it's about the group, not one person's evening) or the memory tagger's artist guess (a video proves you were there). UI (`features/plan/YourHours.tsx`, `DayHoursBar` under the Full day summary via `RouteList`'s `underSummary`): a slim glass row "Your hours  all day" / "Your hours  Arriving 4:00 PM · …", tap to open the editor (a bottom sheet, `Modal` with `SheetClose`, a backdrop that closes, slide or none with reduced motion): Arrive and Leave steppers (− / + 15 min, "From the start" / "Until the end" until set), Breaks (label chips, From / To steppers, Remove), Add a break, Reset to all day, Save. A quiet amber note (new `color.amber` token) for each missed must-see with a one-tap fix ("Stay until 1:30 AM"). Your break stops read "Dinner · 10:30–11:30 PM"; Live now says "Dinner until 11:30 PM." during one (`LiveRoute.pause`). With nothing on in your hours the day says "Nothing on for you in your hours." Data: `Artist.soundcloud` (catalogue artist field, wins; plain problem when it isn't a soundcloud.com link), `SoundCloudProfile`, `CatalogSnapshot.soundcloud` (file key, bad entries left out with one plain problem; `mergeSnapshots` joins, second wins; `validateSnapshot` quietly drops unknown artists), `Catalog.soundcloud` / `soundcloudOf(id)`, `soundcloudCardId` ("soundcloud:<artist id>"); `withoutClips` keeps the pages and drops one whose card you flagged (even a link set on the artist). Discover: `features/discover/soundcloud.ts` (`soundcloudClip`/`soundcloudClips`: one card per artist with a page, a `Clip` with `source: { provider: 'soundcloud', url, name?, image? }`, title "On SoundCloud", filed under their first festival; `reactedCards`; `embedUrl` (`w.soundcloud.com/player/?url=…&color=<accent>&auto_play=…&visual=true&show_comments=false&hide_related=true&show_reposts=false&show_teaser=false`); `soundcloudPlayerHtml`: the embed plus SoundCloud's Widget API (`w.soundcloud.com/player/api.js`) for play/pause/progress, ready after 1.5 s even without the API; `parseSoundCloudMessage`). `media.ts` `SoundCloudMedia` (poster: the artist's photo, else the page's picture). `SoundCloudPlayer.tsx` (WebView, like YouTube's) and `.web.tsx` (same-origin srcdoc iframe): plays while live and not paused, pauses otherwise, your own taps on its buttons become the card's pause/play (ignored within 1.2 s of our command), progress line from `relativePosition`, falls back to the stage on error or 15 s without loading. `ClipPlayer` loads a SoundCloud player only while its card is on screen, so two never play at once. `ClipCard`: square-ish player (up to the card's width, leaving `SOUNDCLOUD_ROOM` 250 pt), the blurred breathing `AmbientBackdrop` from the poster (or the artist's colours), credit "soundcloud.com/<page> ↗" opening the page, share (sends the artist), ••• `flagMenu` ("What's wrong with this SoundCloud page?": Wrong artist / Doesn't play / Something else; "Page hidden"; the flag is kept in `clipFlags.v1` so the card and the Listen link stay hidden on this phone), fallback "SoundCloud isn't playing here. Listen on SoundCloud". Ranking (`feed.ts`): cards are scored like clips but placed by `withPages` after the videos are laid out: only artists with a match ≥ `SOUNDCLOUD_MIN_MATCH` 0.6 or more likes/saves/loves than skips (never a turned-down artist; a skipped card cools the artist), fresh by score then seen lately then skipped; one after every five other cards at most (`SOUNDCLOUD_EVERY` 6, counting what's kept on screen), never beside the same artist; ones that don't fit wait. The videos' own order is unchanged. `useRankedFeed` adds the cards for the mix (all), a festival (its lineup), a sound (artists carrying it) and Following (artists you follow); not Tonight or festival mode. Likes, saves and skips on a card feed taste like a clip's (`TasteProvider` adds `reactedCards`); cards you liked or saved are in `useClipPool` (Your clips shows the artist's photo and "Their uploads"; Profile tiles too). Artist page Listen: `listenLinks(name, soundcloud?)` puts SoundCloud first. HQ isn't changed: its updates never change artists already on Afterglow, so a wrong page is fixed with the artist's `soundcloud` field in `catalogue/afterglow.json`. soundcloud.com isn't reachable from cloud sessions: tested with a fake `fetch` (two nights: found, kept, misses waiting; SoundCloud down keeps last night's) and fixtures; the oEmbed answer's shape for a profile page (`author_name`, `author_url`, `thumbnail_url`) and the Widget API inside the phone's web view are unchecked until the first nightly run and a phone test. - **Glow level that changed on every sign-in (branch `claude/level-fix`, 2026-10-06; reported by the user from phone testing on the website)**: two causes. (1) While Profile loaded, the pill showed the level your account shows to others (sent earlier, maybe from another phone), then swapped to the level counted on this phone or browser, which could be lower: two different numbers depending on which arrived first. (2) Quests, side quests, badges and secrets are counted per phone, not per account, so after switching accounts the other account showed (and published) the same level. Fix: pure `features/quests/accountGlow.ts` (`creditGlow`, `glowAtLevel`, `cleanLedger`, `LEDGER_ACCOUNTS_MAX` 20): a ledger of `phone` (the most glow this phone has given out) and each account's glow; only glow above `phone` is new and goes to the account signed in (glow earned signed out goes to the next account that signs in; the first account signed in after this update gets the phone's glow so far); a drop is never taken away and earning it back isn't counted twice; the level the account already shows is a floor (`max`, never added on top). `state/accountGlow.ts` (shared store, AsyncStorage `accountGlow.v1`, never saved after a failed read). `useDailyQuests` now returns your account's glow and level when signed in (the phone's when signed out) and `owner`; `ready` also waits for sign-in, the ledger and your published level (`useStandingRead` in `standing/useStanding.ts`: `settled` once read or failed). Profile passes `level={null}` while loading so `ProfileHeader` shows no pill (never the published stand-in); the Quests hero, `GlowLevelCard` and Edit profile's row wait too. `QuestToast` doesn't toast a "new level" on switching accounts. `LevelSync` publishes the account level, still only upwards. Tests: `features/quests/__tests__/accountGlow.test.ts`, "your level on Profile" in `src/app/__tests__/glowLevel.test.tsx` (stand-in while loading; sign out and in, switch accounts, restart; no leak).
- **Map fixes: a keyless website map, and shows on an artist's tour** (2026-10-06, branch `claude/map-fixes`, awaiting phone testing; the user on the website: the festival map and an artist's tour said "API key required", and "we should also see upcoming shows"). **Tiles**: CARTO's basemaps now answer with an "API key required" picture, so `FestivalMap.web.tsx` draws OpenStreetMap's own standard tiles (`TILES` `https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png`, no key, no subdomains; `TILES_MAX_ZOOM` 19, the map itself still zooms 2 to 18; `detectRetina` off, since OSM has no high-resolution tiles and fetching the next zoom down would load four times as many and shrink every name), credited "© OpenStreetMap contributors" in the quiet credit corner (now in the system font; OSM's tile policy: visible credit, normal Referer, no bulk downloads, move to a paid provider if traffic grows; `docs/app-store.md`). `leaflet.css` turns the light tiles into a night map with one filter on `.leaflet-tile-pane` (`invert(1) saturate(0.3) sepia(0.4) hue-rotate(225deg) brightness(0.88) contrast(0.92)`: land about #171717, streets #0a0a0a, water slate violet #383642, names soft lavender #c4b9d0); lines and lights over the map keep their colours. No page or CSP allowlist needed changing. **Shows on the tour**: `festival-map?artist=<id>` now also shows the artist's upcoming club shows with a place (`catalog.showsOf(id, today)`), as small hollow lights in the accent (`SHOW_COLOR`, 8 px ring, a ring round it when chosen; under the festivals' lights, not clustered), joined into the same date-ordered tour line, and as cards among the festival cards (date block, the show's name or venue, venue and town, "Show · Sat"; first tap chooses it and glides there, a second opens its EDMTrain page with `Linking.openURL`, as the artist page does). Festivals behave as before. Pure `features/festival-map/tour.ts`: `showPins(shows, today)` (from today, with a place, soonest first), `tourStops(festivalMarkers, pins)` (one date order; a festival before a show on the same day; undated festivals last), `stopDate`, `tourLine(festivals, shows)` ("1 festival · 3 shows", "2 shows, in date order", "No festivals or shows with a place yet"; the subtitle is one line), `showPinLabel`. Both maps take a `shows` prop; selection is by festival or show id. The artist page's "See their tour on the map" (label "See X's festivals and shows on the map") shows when any festival or upcoming show has a place, under Upcoming shows when they play no festival. Tests: `features/festival-map/__tests__/tour.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/tourShows.test.tsx`, the tour case in `festivalMap.test.tsx`.
- **More Discover clips per artist (2026-10-06, branch `claude/more-clips`, awaiting review and the next nightly run; the user: "it can keep pulling more videos for the feed, not just one per artist")**: the nightly YouTube step now keeps up to `MAX_CLIPS_PER_ARTIST` 4 different sets per artist. Pure `data/import/youtube.ts`: `searchUrl(name, key, have)` asks for `SEARCH_RESULTS` 15 (was 10; same 100 units) and rotates `CLIP_QUERIES` ("live set", "full set", "festival set") by clips already had; `liveSets` (every candidate, best first; `pickLiveSet` is its first); `setWords`/`sameSet` (title words without the artist's name and filler; same set when one's words contain the other's or Jaccard ≥ 0.6; two empty = same); `pickLiveSets(name, details, { blocked, taken, max })` (up to `NEW_PER_SEARCH` 2 per search, never like an existing title or each other, +1 for a year not yet had); `clipPlan(wanted, clipCount, misses, today, max, { moreAsked, later, cap })` returns `{ id, name, have }`: first no-clip artists on upcoming festivals (as before, misses wait `RETRY_MISSES_DAYS` 30), then artists below the cap, upcoming before `later` (others with clips), fewest first, then given order, skipping `moreAsked` younger than `RETRY_MORE_DAYS` 21; `cleanAsked` (valid days, not future, within the window); `clipBudget(chars, limit)` (`fits(clip, share)`, `add`). `scripts/youtube-clips.ts`: carries earlier clips per artist up to the cap (each artist's first clip before anyone's extras; a restore file tops artists up rather than only filling empty ones), counts catalogue clips towards the cap, writes `clipMoreAsked` beside `clipMisses` (both cleaned each night; ignored by the app), stops new extra sets at `CLIP_BUDGET_SHARE` 0.85 of `MAX_FILE_CHARS` (checked before each search, so no quota is spent once full) while first clips and carried clips may reach `FIRST_CLIP_SHARE` 0.95; log: clips in all, new tonight (first clips, more sets), searches, on upcoming lineups artists with none and below 4, the file's share of the limit, and a line when the budget stopped it. Quota: 90 searches × 100 + ≤ 90 details + ~1 per 50 clips for shapes ≈ 9,150 of 10,000 units: 70 for sets (about 50 to 110 new a night) and `SHORT_SEARCHES` 20 for Shorts (up to 40). **Shorts** (the user, same day: vertical clips "to see how real posts will work"): `shortsUrl` (search `videoDuration=short`, "<name> live #shorts"), `pickShorts(name, details, { blocked, taken, max })` (upright by `uprightOf`, `MIN_SHORT_SECONDS` 15 to `MAX_SHORT_SECONDS` 180, public, embeddable, not live now, title names the artist and has `LIVE_WORDS` (live, set, festival, drop, crowd, stage, rave…), none of `NOT_LIVE` (interview, tutorial, reaction, remix, edit, mashup, lyrics, type beat…), hashtags stripped by `withoutTags`, never a title the artist has; own channel +4, known channel +3, then search order; up to `NEW_SHORTS_PER_SEARCH` 2), `shortClip` (`/shorts/<id>` link, from 0:00 to its length, `vertical: true`, `short: true`), `shortsPlan` (upcoming lineups before the rest, fewest Shorts first, below `MAX_SHORTS_PER_ARTIST` 2, a separate cap so Shorts never crowd sets out; `clipShortsAsked` waits `RETRY_SHORTS_DAYS` 21). Sets are planned first with 70 of the searches, Shorts get the rest; Shorts count as extras for the file budget. The catalogue file's `video.short: true` sets `YouTubeClipSource.short` (and `vertical`); `docs/catalogue.md` says so. Feed: `isShort(clip)`; Shorts wait while one of the last `SHORT_GAP` 2 cards was a Short and get a turn of their own once `SHORT_EVERY` 4 cards have passed without one (only when Shorts are left, so the no-Shorts order and speed are unchanged); the same-artist rules apply as to any clip. Autoplay-next is unchanged: a Short loops like any clip unless autoplay moves on. Feed: same-artist spacing (`ARTIST_GAP`, `REPEAT`, cooled artists last) already handled several clips; `interleave` now sorts each lane once and stops scanning early (and only looks for an "urgent" artist when one could be), giving the identical order ~14× faster at 2,400 clips (checked against the old code on 40 random feeds). Artist page "Live clips" lists every clip and "Fans also watch" picks each artist's unwatched one, unchanged. Tests: `data/import/__tests__/youtube.test.ts` (query rotation, set words, multi-pick, plan order and windows, cleaning, budget, a four-clip file the app reads; Shorts search, picking, the clip and how the app reads it, the Shorts plan), `recommend/__tests__/feed.test.ts` (four sets each: never back to back, a liked artist's other sets come forward, a skipped artist's wait; Shorts first at card 4, at least one every 4 cards, never two within 2 while sets remain). Checked with a fake YouTube over five nights (140 → 738 clips, 40 Shorts a night) and with a nearly full file (extras stop at 85%, first clips continue, searches drop to 19).
- **How you feel, redesigned (branch `claude/care-feelings`, 2026-10-06, awaiting phone testing; the user said the feeling check-in "works, but visually could be more appealing" and approved a sketch)**: same data and behaviour, new look for Look after yourself's feeling card (`features/care/FeelingCard.tsx`; the card now asks its own question, so the page's "How are you feeling?" section title is gone). *Picking*: "How are you right now?", Great / Good / Okay as three glassy glowing orbs (`features/care/MoodOrb.tsx`: react-native-svg radial gradients for the halo, the ball lit from the top left and a highlight; colours from `LEVEL_COLOR`), then "Something’s off" (Tired, Anxious, Too hot, Sick) and "Need a hand" (Overwhelmed, Not great, soft coral outline) as pills with a dot. The groups come from `MOOD_GROUPS` / `HAND_MOODS` in `care.ts` (sick still brings Get help to the top, as every `HELP_MOODS` one does). Labels unchanged ("I'm feeling too hot"). *After a check-in* (until it's 45 min old): a big orb in the feeling's colour with two faint rings, growing in with one ripple when you've just picked it and then breathing slowly (still with Reduce Motion), the feeling in large type, "Checked in just now" / "Checked in 12 min ago", the tip (`MOOD_TIP`, in a coral box for help feelings), "Tell my crew" (only when there's a crew festival and within 30 min, as before; it opens `CareButtons` with the one that fits) and "Change" (the picker again, "How are you now?", with Cancel; picking adds a check-in as before). Stale: "It’s been a while. How are you now?" with "Earlier: Too hot · 1 h 10 min ago" above the picker. *Tonight* (2+ check-ins): `features/care/TonightGraph.tsx`, a smooth line (`features/care/curve.ts` `smoothPath`, a monotone cubic that never swings past a check-in) from the first check-in to now, its stroke a gradient through each check-in's colour with a soft glow, a faint accent area under it, dots with the latest bigger and lit, the lowest moment(s) named under them (`lowestPoints`), times under it (`graphTimes`: first, two between to the quarter hour when the night is long enough, "Now"; `clockShort`), "Tonight" and "N check-ins", spoken as `nightSummary` ("Tonight, 3 check-ins: 9 PM good, 10:30 PM too hot, 11:45 PM great."). *Felt better*: an amber note when the latest low point (level 2 or lower) isn't the latest check-in (`recoveryFrom`, `recoveryLine`: "You felt too hot at 10:30 PM, and you’ve been feeling better since."; only what you said, never why). A smaller orb and type on narrow phones (iPhone SE). Stopwatch chips (`FeelingChips` compact) and the stopwatch graph unchanged. New tokens: `color.coral`, `tintA`. Checked in Chromium at 390 and 320 wide. Tests: `features/care/__tests__/feelings.test.ts` (groups, recovery, lowest, times, smooth line), the orb check-in case in `src/app/__tests__/careGuide.test.tsx`, Tell my crew in `crewCare.test.tsx`. In Spanish (`src/i18n/es/care.ts`, new lines listed in `docs/care-spanish-review.md`) the graph and the "felt better since" note use the 24-hour clock.
- **Comments on every clip in Discover (2026-10-06, branch `claude/clip-comments`, awaiting review and phone testing; the user: "I don't see a comments button or a way to add comments on anything in feed as of yet")**: until now only crowd videos people shared had comments (a small bubble beside the credit). Now every card's action rail has a speech bubble (`CommentIcon`) between Like and Save, the same size as the others, with the count under it (`commentCaption`: "Comment" when there are none, then "12", "1.2K"; VoiceOver "Comments, 12"); crowd videos use it for their existing comments (the old bubble in the credit row is gone, so there's one button). `RAIL` in `videoLayout.ts` now makes room for four buttons. *Data*: Firestore `clipComments/{id}` (`clipId`, `uid`, `handle`, `text` 1 to 300, `createdAt` server time); `ClipComment`, `CLIP_COMMENTS_PAGE` 50, `validClipId` (`^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$`) in `social/models.ts`; `clipCommentFromDoc` in `mapping.ts`. *Rules*: read signed in; create as yourself with a profile and your profile's handle, clip id of that shape, text 1 to 300, `createdAt == request.time`, no other fields; never updated; deleted by the writer or the support account; reports accept `kind: 'clipComment'`. Index (clipId, createdAt asc). Emulator: 103 checks pass (2 new groups). The rules and index deploy automatically from `main` (the "Database and storage rules" step), else `firebase deploy --only firestore`. *Service*: `clipComment`, `clipComments(clipId, max, after?)` (oldest first, the next page starts after the last one's document), `clipCommentCount`, `deleteClipComment`, `reportedClipComment`; `takeDown('clipComment', id)`; account deletion removes your clip comments (softly, so old rules never block it). `fakeSocial` has `clipComments`. Online only, matching comments on shared videos: no signal shows "Couldn't post your comment" with the no-signal words, nothing is queued. *Counts*: `features/social/commentCounts.ts` (plain store, reset by `test/afterEnv.ts`) and `useCommentCount.ts`: one count query per card on screen or next to it, kept 5 minutes, set exactly when the sheet has read every comment and changed at once when you post or delete. *Sheet*: `comments/[id].tsx` now takes `?kind=clip`: the artist and clip title at the top, oldest first, Show more comments (50 at a time), Post or "Create a profile to comment", hold or VoiceOver's action to delete your own or report / block someone, people you blocked left out, a `SheetClose` (instead of Done) on both kinds. Opening comments excuses the watch (never a quick skip); comments never change taste. No bell for clip comments (nobody owns the clip). HQ Reports: "A comment on a clip in Discover", Read it, Take it down. Artist page clips and Your clips open the same card, so the button comes along. Docs: "Comments" in `docs/memories-and-profiles.md`. Tests: `features/social/__tests__/clipComments.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/clipComments.test.tsx`, the clip comment case in `admin.test.tsx`.
- **Search, stations and a Following button in Discover (2026-10-06, branch `claude/discover-stations`, awaiting review and phone testing; the user: "a search bar to be able to search for styles as well as festivals … 'sounds like: subtronics' for those single day shows … 'start discovery based on' for pretty much any search", and "a little toggle … to easily toggle the friend feed into discovery")**: *Search on What to discover*: a `SearchField` (new optional `onSubmit`: Return picks the first result) under the subtitle filters live, reusing `matchesName`/`searchFestivals`/`searchCatalog`: matching sounds as chips ("dub" → Dubstep; sounds with at least one clip), festivals by name or place (cards, and "Clips on the way"), and a new "Artists" section whose rows read "Sounds like SUBTRONICS · 18 clips from them and artists like them" and start a station; "Nothing matches “…”" otherwise; an empty field shows the page exactly as before. *Stations*: a new focus kind `like:<artistId>` (`likeFocus`, `likeOf` in `features/discover/focus.ts`; wide, remembered on start like a sound, kept when asked for, a clip link from it goes to the mix). Pure `features/recommend/station.ts`: `stationArtists(seed, artists, clipCount, similarity)` (the seed when they have clips at closeness 1, then up to `STATION_ARTISTS` 30 with clips at least `STATION_MIN` 0.35 alike by the taste engine's similarity (lineup/listening data, then sound tags), closest first; under `STATION_CLIPS` 12 clips it widens to everyone carrying the seed's sounds until `STATION_WIDE_CLIPS` 24), and `rankStation` (score = `CLOSE_WEIGHT` 0.6 × closeness + `TASTE_WEIGHT` 0.4 × live match, + `SEED_LIFT` 0.3 for the seed, − 0.1 for clips you liked or saved; the same fresh / turned-down / seen lately / skipped blocks and kept clips as the main feed; no artist twice in a row, the seed at most once in `SEED_GAP` 4, each repeat −0.25; SoundCloud cards one in `SOUNDCLOUD_EVERY` for artists ≥ 0.6 close or a decent match; reasons: the seed none, others "Close to SUBTRONICS"). `useRankedFeed` builds the station's clips (catalogue, crowd, SoundCloud) and ranks with it; `feed.ts` is unchanged. Discover's header: "Sounds like SUBTRONICS" / "Similar sounds, every lineup"; `FeedEnd` takes `stationLabel` ("That’s the station"); an artist no longer on Afterglow plays your mix. *Recent stations*: the last `RECENT_STATIONS` 3 (`state/recentStations.ts`, `recentStations.v1`, `addStation`/`cleanStations`) as avatar chips on What to discover. *Start discovery from anything*: Discover's search (`search.tsx`) gains a Sounds section (rows "Discover Dubstep, 8 clips from every lineup") and a small glass button beside each artist ("Sounds like <Name>", sound icon) and each festival with clips ("Discover <Name>", play icon); the artist page has a "More like this" pill after Listen ("Discover more like <Name>"). *Following button*: in Discover's header beside the quest ring, camera and search, a `GlassCircleButton` (new optional `selected`: glow ring and fill, `accessibilityState.selected`) with the people icon, shown when signed in and not in festival mode; it switches to Following and back to what you were on (hint "Tap to go back to Dubstep"; your mix by default); following no one shows the usual Following empty page. Tests: `station.test.ts`, `focus.test.ts`, `stations.test.tsx`. In Spanish since the merge with Languages: 32 lines in `src/i18n/es/discover.ts` and 3 in `es/artist.ts` (reusing `Sounds like {artist}`); the Following button's hint is one whole sentence per focus (`useBackHint`), and `FeedEnd` takes the station's artist name (`station`) rather than an English label.
- **Shows for you (2026-10-06, branch `claude/shows-for-you`, awaiting review and phone testing; the user: "I want single day shows to also be recommended and easy to be seen or accessed. Some text may need to be changed to 'Festivals / Shows'")**: the nightly EDMTrain club shows are now recommended and reachable everywhere. Pure `features/festivals/showsForYou.ts`: `rankShow` (the bill best for you first; the best artist you haven't turned down carries the show, so a disliked artist never does; known = informed, followed or rated warmly), `rankShows` (carried first, then within `NEAR_KM` of you when location is on, then known before first guesses, then match with `FOLLOW_BOOST` 0.15 for artists you follow (ordering only, the match shown stays the live one), then distance, then date), `showsForYou` (next `SHOWS_DAYS` 90, one per night and venue via `dedupeShows`, known and at least `SHOW_MIN` 0.5 or followed, up to `SHOWS_FOR_YOU_MAX` 12; else the soonest, near first, as "Shows coming up"), `filterShows` (near / this week = 7 days / this month = 31 days / all), `searchShows` (artist names, then name or venue, then town), `showsInReach` and `showsByDay` (calendar), `addDays`, `upcomingShows`, `matchLabel`. UI in `features/festivals/ShowCards.tsx` (`useShowTaste`, `ShowDateBlock`, `ShowRow`, `ShowCard`). **Show page** `src/app/show/[id].tsx`: big date block, "Show" tag and the day, title (name or venue), venue and town, distance, "92% match with X", "Get tickets on EDMTrain ↗" (the event link), Add to calendar (`CalendarPill` now also takes `{ event, name }`; `showEvent` in `features/calendar/events.ts`: one all-day event at the venue, who's on, the ticket link and `afterglowmusic.app/show/<id>`; an .ics file on the web), share (`lib/share`, the website link), "On the bill" (`ArtistRow`s, best first, with the artist page's why line or "You follow them"), the EDMTrain credit, and "Show not found" for old links. **Festivals tab**: titled "Festivals & shows" (on its own line under the map/view/bell buttons, which no longer fit beside it), search "Artist, festival or show" with a Shows section (up to 8, opens the show page; "No festival, show or artist matches …"), and a "Shows for you" shelf of `ShowCard`s (date, match, name, town and distance, faces) after Up next and Also going, with See all → `src/app/shows.tsx` ("Shows", chips Near you · This week · This month · All with counts, search by artist, venue or town, 30 at a time; Near you without location shows `NearYouPrompt` titled "Shows near you"). The shelf is hidden when there are no shows (offline, bundled catalogue). **Calendar** (`festival-calendar.tsx`, browser title "Festivals & shows calendar"): shows for you within the chosen reach get a small glow ring on their day, a "Shows for you" legend and list under the festivals (a day lists all of its own, a month the first 8 with See all shows). **Artist pages**: "Upcoming shows" rows open the show page (chevron; tickets inside) instead of EDMTrain directly. Tab bar label unchanged ("Festivals"). Checked in Chromium at 390×844. Tests: `features/festivals/__tests__/showsForYou.test.ts`, the show case in `features/calendar/__tests__/events.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/showsForYou.test.tsx` (shelf → show page → tickets, share link, See all filters and search, tab search, artist page row, not found), updated `artistShows.test.tsx`, `screens.test.tsx`, `manyFestivals.test.tsx`.
- **Posting videos from the website (2026-10-06, branch `claude/web-posts`, awaiting review and a test in Safari on an iPhone; the user tests mostly on afterglowmusic.app and wants to see how real posts work)**: the website can now post a vertical video as a crowd clip, through the same tagging sheet and the same `shareMemory` as the phone. *Choosing*: on the web `/camera` is a "Post a video" page (`ChooseOnWeb` in `camera.tsx`) with Choose a video (filming with Afterglow's own camera stays in the app); `useAddMemory` (Memories' "Post a video", a hold on Discover) and Profile → Moments' New tile / empty-state button ("Post a video") open it on the web, because Safari only opens a file chooser from a real tap. `device.web.ts` `pickVideo` makes and clicks a hidden `<input type=file accept="video/*">` within that tap (iPhone: Photos, Take Video or Files), then pure `pickWebVideo` (`features/memories/webVideo.ts`) checks it: MP4/MOV/M4V only (`webVideoFormat`, by type or by name when the browser gives none; WebM etc. → "That isn't a video Afterglow can share"), under `WEB_MAX_VIDEO_BYTES` (= `MAX_SHARE_BYTES` 150 MB, strictly under like storage.rules → "That video is too big"), and length and size from an off-screen `<video>`'s metadata (`checkWebMeta`: up to `WEB_MAX_CLIP_SECONDS` = `MAX_CLIP_SECONDS` 60 plus a second's grace → "That video is too long"; unreadable → "Couldn't open that video"); when it was made from the file's `lastModified` (now for a fresh one). The draft is a `blob:` link with `keptBySharing: true`. *Keeping*: the web `deviceMemoryFiles` is `sessionOnly` (a map of names to `blob:` links for as long as the page is open; https names are their own link); `MemoriesProvider` neither reads nor writes `memories.v1` when files are session-only (`hydrated` at once, `useMemories().sessionOnly`). `keep` draws a poster frame (about 1 s in, at most 720 px, JPEG 0.75) on a canvas from an off-screen muted inline video (played and paused at once so an iPhone loads a frame), else no still. *Tagging* (`memory-tag.tsx`, `webPost`): the bar button says Share ("Share this video"), Share to Discover is always on and can't be switched off, with "On the website, videos are kept by sharing them. Nothing is uploaded until you tap Share."; Share without what's needed says why in "Share it to keep it" (pick the festival and artist, Sign in / Make a profile with a button, the throwback limit); no Send to my crew. Sharing keeps the video for the page, starts `social.share` (the same outbox, progress and errors; a failure says it stays in Memories until the page closes) and replaces the sheet with Memories, which shows the upload %. *Upload*: `shareMemory` now reads the file first and names it by `videoType(uri, file.type)` (a name in the link wins; a `blob:` link has none, so an iPhone's .mov is `video/quicktime` → `memories/<uid>/<id>.mov`, else `.mp4`), with the same width/height/duration/still as the phone, so it fills Discover edge to edge for other people (your own videos stay out of your own Discover, as on the phone). Storage rules unchanged (they already take `video/.*` in .mp4/.mov/.m4v under 150 MB); the emulator test adds the website's .mov/.mp4/.jpg uploads and a refused .webm: 102 checks pass. *Your moments on the website*: `useMoments()` (`features/memories/useMoments.ts`) is the phone's memories in the app and, on the website, your shared videos read with `memoriesOf` on focus and after each upload, plus any still uploading from the page (`webMoments`, `sharedAsMemory`, `onServer` in `webVideo.ts`); Memories, Profile → Moments and the festival-mode profile use it, and a server tile opens `/shared/<id>` (Remove from profile is there). Empty copy: "Videos you share from the website show here once shared." Tests: `features/memories/__tests__/webVideo.test.ts`, the website memories case in `lib/__tests__/web.test.tsx`, `videoType` in `social.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/webPosts.test.tsx` (the web memories module, choose → tag → Share → server copy, Memories and Profile show it; signed out offers Sign in). In Spanish too (merged with the Languages work): the Post a video page, the website Memories and Videos lines, the tagging sheet's web note and Share-to-keep alert, and the new picker messages (too big, wrong kind of file) are in `src/i18n/es/memories.ts` (the Videos tab line in `es/profile.ts`).
- **Where they play (branch `claude/where-they-play`, 2026-10-06; the user: Profile's "Where they play" link didn't make it easy to see where they play, and single-night shows weren't there)**: `your-artists.tsx` rebuilt around a schedule. Title "Where they play", "N artists you follow and love": the same faces as Profile's Your artists row (`favouriteArtists`, in its order) plus everyone else you follow, each once. Filter chips All · Near you · Festivals · Shows (· Going when you're going to one), with counts (Near you's only once your location is on) and an empty line for each (Near you off: "Use my location" turns it on). Then, by month ("October", "January 2027" when not this year, "Date to come" last for festivals without dates), every upcoming date: a glass date block (month, day, weekday; glowing when you're going), the artist's face or up to 3 overlapping faces with "+N" when several of yours play the same event (each face opens the artist, in the festival's context), the artists' names, the festival or show (name or venue), the place and distance, and tags Festival / Show, Weekend N, Going and Near you (within `NEAR_KM`) in glow. A festival opens its page; a show opens its EDMTrain page (credited "Show info from EDMTrain ↗"). Below: "Your artists" (each with "3 dates · next Sat 18 Oct" or "No dates yet", and Follow), then "You might follow" (suggestions now leave out everyone already on the page). Profile's Your artists row shows "Next: SUBTRONICS · Sat 24 Oct · Denver" (a festival's name instead of the town) under its title, tapping through to the page. Pure `features/artists/where.ts`: `whereTheyPlay` (festival runs still to come, one per weekend the artist plays, honouring `artistWeekends`; the artist's own day from set times or `artistDays` when the festival lists one day label per day of the run; club shows from `showsOf(id, today)`; one entry per event with all your artists; soonest first, going first on a day, festivals before shows; undated last), `whereFilter`, `whereCounts`, `byMonth`, `whereArtists`, `datesOf`, `artistNames`, `shortDate`, `placeWord`, `nextLine`, `datesCount`; `features/artists/useWhereTheyPlay.ts` (also used by Profile; show distance from the show's `place`). `followedShows`/`moreLine` in `follow.ts` are no longer used by the page. Tests: `features/artists/__tests__/where.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/whereTheyPlay.test.tsx`, `src/app/__tests__/followArtists.test.tsx` updated. In Spanish too: every line goes through `t()` / `tn()` / `tc()` (Spanish in `src/i18n/es/sound.ts`); month headings use `monthTitle`, dates `showDateBlock` / `showDateSpoken`, and the Profile "Next:" line `shortDate` in the language.
- **The living garden (branch `claude/living-garden`, the user's idea on 2026-10-06: "a real livable viewable garden", pick them up and move them, little feet that wiggle)**: the top of Your garden is a night meadow (`features/sprouts/GardenScene.tsx`) instead of a grid of cards: a rolling horizon, far stars, a soft green pool (gold for artists) where each resident stands, grass, seven fireflies. Every plain sprout and every artist collectible (earned or given) stands at its own spot (64 to 94 pt: `STAGE_SIZE` by stage, `TIER_SIZE` by level; back rows 10% smaller), its name under it and a small bar (growth to the next stage in sprout green; videos to the next level in that level's colour), tap for the same sheet as before ("from X" lives there; `ArtistSproutSheet` is now exported from `ArtistSproutsSection.tsx`, whose section is a summary line plus the teasers). Pure `features/sprouts/gardenLayout.ts`: `meadow(count, width)` (columns `colsFor` 2 to `MAX_COLS` 5 of `SPOT_MIN_WIDTH` 108, `ROW_HEIGHT` 124, `HORIZON` 54, always one spare spot, each spot a little off its grid line), `spotOrder` (each row from the middle out), `placeResidents` (saved spots kept, newcomers take the first free one, the departed free theirs), `moveTo` (swaps with the occupant), `nearestSpot` (a worklet), `cleanLayouts`. `state/gardenLayout.ts` keeps `{ [uid | 'guest']: { [residentId]: spot } }` in `gardenLayout.v1` (`forgetGardenLayout` in `test/afterEnv.ts`). Motion on one clock (`features/sprouts/motion.ts`: `CLOCK_MS` 24 s loop, `paceOf(id)` whole cycles per loop for bob, sway, blink and twinkle plus a phase, `sine`, `blinkOf`, `glanceOf`): the scene runs one `withRepeat` while the screen is focused and motion isn't reduced, and every creature derives its bob, sway, blink, feet and sparkle from it with its own pace, so 30 residents cost one animation plus their style worklets, and the loop joins up with no jump. `Sprout` and `Collectible` take `driven` (clock, pace, running, lift, glance) and `scene` (no tile or ground shadow of their own); `Plant` is rebuilt from parts into one picture when still (lighter for the Kinds row and shelves) or layers when moving, with two new stubby feet in the kind's deep colour that swing a quarter turn behind the bob (more when carried), an eyes layer that blinks and glances at its nearest neighbour in the row, and its own clock when on its own (the sheet, the celebration). `Collectible` gets generic feet (`FEET`) on lion, pumpkin, sheep, twin, alien, sloth, beast, bot and dragon (round bodies and boxes); the rest keep their own legs, sit on a base, float or are things. Each creature has a ground shadow (two soft pills) that shrinks and fades as it floats and lifts; the fresh ones (a bloom or arrival not yet celebrated, or arrived within `FRESH_MS` an hour) glow. Pick up: hold `LIFT_MS` 260 ms (`Gesture.Pan().activateAfterLongPress`, so a scroll that starts on a creature still scrolls), haptic, it lifts (scale 1.15, feet dangle more, a tilt from the drag speed), follows the finger, and on release springs to the nearest spot with a little squash (reduced motion: straight there), swapping with whoever stood there; the page doesn't scroll while something's carried (`scrollEnabled`, and a non-passive `touchmove` on the website). The Pressable's `onLongPress` guard means letting go of a carried one never opens it. Accessibility: the ground is an image "A night meadow with N sprouts and M artist collectibles. Hold and drag one to move it."; each creature a button with the card's old label and hint "Hold and drag to move". In Spanish (merged after Languages): names under creatures are `KindSpec.short`, collectible timing comes from creature and colours (`paceOf` on the same key as main's `pace`), and the scene's labels, hint and summary lines are in `src/i18n/es/sprouts.ts`.
- **Cheaper checks (2026-10-06; the account ran out of its 2,000 free GitHub Actions minutes for the month)**: `.github/workflows/website.yml` no longer builds or deploys a preview for pull requests (they only type-check and test), the nightly run and "Run workflow" skip the type-check and tests (main was checked on push), and Jest runs with `--maxWorkers=2` and a transform cache kept between runs (`actions/cache`, `.jest-cache`). Before, a pull request took about 10 minutes, the nightly run about 15. Sessions should also batch their pushes: merging `main` into several open branches costs one full run each.

Community clips no longer need a festival (`communityClip`: festivalId `''` for neither, `show:<key>` for a show, title "From the crowd · <show>", translated by `shownClipTitle`).

The suggestion is the loudest `CLIP_SUGGESTED` (30) seconds, from the sound decoded in the page (`loudness.web.ts`, videos up to 4 minutes; otherwise the start). Drag the bright window, 15/30/45/60 s, ±1 s. The whole file uploads with `clip { start, end }` (`clipStart`/`clipEnd` in Firestore, at most 61 s apart) and `durationSec` = the part's length; every player plays only the part (`useClipLoop`, `NativeVideoPlayer`, `VideoPreview`; `seek.ts` keeps player writes out of components for the compiler lint). The still comes from the part.
  - *Preview*: `VideoPreview.tsx` (phone: expo-video with its own controls, sound on) and `VideoPreview.web.tsx` (the browser's `<video controls playsinline>`, `#t=` in the link so Safari draws a frame instead of black).
