- **Back to back understood app-wide (2026-10-06; the user: a LAYZ clip titled "B2B KOMPANY …" should count for and show both artists, and the app should know in general that b2b is two artists playing together)**: `src/data/b2b.ts` (pure): `b2bIndex(artists)` (who's in each b2b act, both ways, by folded name), `partnersInTitle(title, mainId, index)` (the artist named right before or after a "b2b"/"b3b" word, longest name up to 5 words, only artists on Afterglow, never the clip's own artist, at most 3), `withPartners(clips)` (fills `Clip.withArtistIds` from titles unless the file gave `"with"`), `partnersOf(clip)` (own `withArtistIds`, else a crowd video's), `playersOf(clip, membersOf)` (everyone playing, main first, a b2b act's artists included), `together(players)` (one Artist named "LAYZ b2b KOMPANY" with every face as `members`, first artist's id), `titleWithoutPartners`. `createCatalog` runs it: `clipsOf(id)` adds b2bs they play in (after their own), `festivalsOf(id)` adds festivals where a b2b act they're in plays, new `membersOf`, `actsOf`, `playersOf`. Taste (`computeTaste`'s new last argument): every like, save, skip and watch counts for each player; a b2b act nobody reacted to or rated matches like its best-liked artist (`informed`). Community videos remembered after leaving the feed keep their partners (`artistOf` stores `"main|other"`, `PLAYERS_JOIN`). Feed (`scoreClips`): a b2b clip is lane'd and scored by the player you like best. Discover card: a split avatar, "LAYZ b2b KOMPANY" (each name opens that artist), match % of the best-liked, and the title without the repeated "B2B KOMPANY". Artist page: on a lineup only through a b2b act, the Playing row shows that act's set with "As SUBTRONICS B2B CRANKDAT". File format: optional `"with": ["kompany"]` on a clip (`docs/catalogue.md`); `validate` drops ids not in the file. Tests: `src/data/__tests__/b2b.test.ts`.
- **Set times publish by themselves from official pages (2026-10-06; the user, after asking how other apps get set times: "publish it by itself")**: `scripts/set-times.ts` runs each night after the site pictures (workflow step "Set times from official pages"). For every upcoming festival with a `website` and no set times, it reads the page (`pageLines`: one line per block, table cells joined with " | "), reads it with HQ's `readSetList`, matches names to artists on Afterglow only (`readPageSetTimes`), and `decide`s: at least `MIN_SETS` 8 sets, `MIN_KNOWN_SHARE` 60% of acts known, `MIN_COVERAGE` 40% of the lineup, no strange days, at most `MAX_GUESSED_SHARE` 30% without AM/PM and `MAX_NO_STAGE_SHARE` 10% without a stage. Clear readings are written to `public/catalogue.json` as `lineups` entries with `"source": "page"` (new `FestivalLineup.source`, read back by `parseLineup` in `data/file.ts`); `applyLineup` never lets a page lineup replace hand-entered set times and only adds acts. A page unreadable tonight keeps last night's (`/tmp/published.json`). The report (`setTimesReport`) goes at the top of the nightly "Festival news" issue: what was published (new or updated) and what was held, with reasons. `scripts/livePatches.ts` now shared by the page watch and this. Image-only or script-built timetables (Insomniac's posters) still need HQ → read a photo. Tests: `src/features/admin/__tests__/pageSetTimes.test.ts`. Docs: `docs/events-import.md` "Set times from official pages".

- **Crowd videos show whole unless filling the screen crops very little (2026-10-06; the user: shared videos looked "super zoomed in" though filmed upright)**: `features/memories/videoFit.ts` `fitFor(videoSize, screen)`: fill (`cover`) only when that cuts off at most `MAX_CROP` 12% of the picture, else the whole video (`contain`); unknown shape shows whole. The shape comes from the video itself (`useVideoSize`: on phones the player's `sourceLoad` track size; on the website `useVideoSize.web.ts` reads the file's metadata, which the browser turns upright), the saved `width`/`height` until then. Test: `features/memories/__tests__/videoFit.test.ts`.

The artist gets the id the catalogue would give them (`newArtistId`, edmtrain `slug`: "rezz"), with the typed name kept as `artistName` on the memory, the shared copy (`memories` rules: `artistName` up to 60, only with `artistId`; emulator test) and retags. `src/data/extraArtists.ts` remembers these names (from your memories and every shared video read, via `memoryFromDoc`), and `CatalogProvider` makes `useCatalog().artist(id)` fall back to them, so the name shows everywhere a video's artist does; they never join `catalog.artists`, lineups, Discover's ranking or taste. Once HQ adds the artist with that id, the catalogue's own record wins and the videos are theirs. `features/memories/hqRequests.ts` + `useHqRequests` (called on every save in `memory-tag`): a typed-in past festival (not on Afterglow under another year, not in `KNOWN_FESTIVALS`) sends a support message (topic "A festival") asking for a unique badge; a new artist sends one (topic "Something else") asking to add them with that id. - **Crowd videos fit their frame on the website (2026-10-06; the user: uploaded videos still looked zoomed in and focused on the wrong area, upright and sideways)**: the real cause was expo-video's web `VideoView`, which passes its style straight to a `<video>` tag; `StyleSheet.absoluteFill` (left/right/top/bottom 0) doesn't stretch a video tag, so it drew at its own pixel size from the top-left corner. Every `VideoView` (Discover's `NativeVideoPlayer`, `shared/[id]`, `memory/[id]`, `crew-story`) now has an explicit `width/height: '100%'` style. Discover's player also uses the video's real shape (`useVideoSize` + `fitFor` with its measured frame) instead of always filling for an upright flag, so it shows the whole picture unless filling crops at most 12%. The posting preview (`VideoPreview`, both versions) shows the whole video instead of cropping. Test `nativeVideoPlayer.test.tsx`; checked in a browser with test videos marked in each corner.

The garden's buttons gain "Make kandi" (buttons wrap two to a row), opening `/kandi-make` with nobody chosen: it lists the people you follow (`useFriendsCircle`, with photos) to pick from (then the usual kandi maker), or offers Find people when you follow nobody. Test in `visitGarden.test.tsx`.

Test in `gardenBackup.test.tsx`.

Now `state/sprouts.tsx` keeps a private copy in Firestore `gardenBackups/{uid}` (`saveGardenBackup`/`gardenBackup` on `SocialService`; text JSON from `features/sprouts/backup.ts`, at most 900 000 characters; only the owner reads, writes or removes it; removed with the account): your sprouts, sent gifts, blooms, celebrations shown, wild finds and first sprout. Once per account (signed in, with signal) the copy is read and merged in (`mergeStored`: nothing is ever taken away, the earliest first sprout wins); only after that read succeeds is it rewritten, 5 s after changes settle, so an empty phone never writes over a full copy (no signal: retried; refused: left alone). The phone's own file load uses the same merge. Rules test in `scripts/firebase-rules.test.mjs`; app test `gardenBackup.test.tsx`.

- **Long and big videos on the website (2026-10-07; the user couldn't upload videos over a minute to trim them)**: the cause was size, not length: the website refused any file of 150 MB or more when it was chosen (`checkWebFile`), and a minute of iPhone 4K is more. Now only the part that plays is uploaded. *Cutting* (pure `features/memories/cutMp4.ts`, no dependency): reads the file's top-level box headers and its `moov` (never the `mdat`), keeps the video and sound tracks (H.264 or HEVC, MP4 or MOV; others, `udta` and the phone's location notes are dropped), takes the video from the keyframe at or before the part's start to a second past its end and the sound over the same stretch, and writes `ftyp`, a new `moov` (stts/ctts/stss/stsz/stsc and one `co64` chunk per track, an `elst` keeping the original start offset) and a 64-bit `mdat`; the samples are ranges of the original, so `cutPart.web.ts` joins them as `Blob` slices without reading the video into memory, and moves the window (`from`: new time 0). The Firebase share (`shareMemory`) cuts before uploading when there's a `clip` and falls back to the whole file if it can't (`cutPart.ts` does nothing in the app, whose picker trims). *Limits* (`webVideo.ts`): files up to `WEB_MAX_VIDEO_BYTES` 4 GB are taken ("Choose one under 4 GB."), and `PickedVideo.bytes` carries the size; `trim.ts` `clipMaxFor(duration, bytes)` is the longest part that fits 90% of the upload cap with `CUT_MARGIN_SEC` 3 to spare (a minute for ordinary videos, about 44 s of 4K30, 17 s of 4K60), `needsTrim(duration, bytes)` asks for a part when the whole is too big, and the window functions take that `max`; `TrimBar` hides lengths over it and says "This video is a big file, so up to {n} seconds of it can be shared." Loudness isn't read for files over `LOUDNESS_MAX_BYTES` 300 MB. Checked with ffmpeg on H.264 (with and without B-frames) and HEVC, MP4 and MOV: the cut files decode cleanly and their frames match the original's exactly. Tests: `cutMp4.test.ts` (a small MOV fixture with a subtitle track, `__tests__/fixtures/eight-seconds.mov`), `trim.test.ts`, `webVideo.test.ts`.

No signal and no crews kept yet still shows the Last updated line; no crew there still offers Start a crew. The full-app Friends tab is unchanged. Spanish in `es/festivalMode.ts`. - **Grace's gnome garden: a theme, a profile background, a sticker and sprouts only Grace grows (2026-10-08, branch `claude/dreamy-ride-i3iz6j`; the user: account `8EEXIUNCQghOnejeAOygGMSl6Bp1`, Grace, the same person as the panda garden, needs a gnome-themed profile, stickers, and sprouts to grow, give away and show off)**: *Theme and background*: palette `gnome` in `theme/palettes.ts` (mossy night, toadstool red glow, lantern gold), theme **Gnome garden** in `features/themes/themes.ts` (`only: GNOME_FRIENDS`, `backdrop: 'gnome'`), background `gnome` in `profile/backdrops.ts` (`GNOME_FRIENDS` = that uid) and the `GnomeGarden` scene in `AnimatedBackdrop.tsx` (two rocking gnomes, one winking, toadstools breathing, a swinging lantern, fireflies rising; 18 s loop). Someone with two made-for themes gets the newest by default (`madeFor`/`madeTheme` search from the end), so Grace's profile now shows the gnome garden unless the panda garden or a cover photo is picked. `themeGift.v1` now keeps `uid:theme` for themes after the first three (`offerKey`; panda, frog and toes stay keyed by the account alone), so Grace is offered the gnome theme once despite having been offered the panda one. *Sticker*: a **Garden gnome** festival-place creature (`gnome` in `PLACES`, `artistLook.ts`; drawing in `creatures.tsx`), so HQ → Stickers can make and print gnome stickers like any other; it joins everyone's Stickers set in the collection. *Sprouts*: a new kind, **Gnome sprout** (`gnome`, with `keepers: GNOME_FRIENDS` and `plantedAt` 2026-10-08 in `SPROUT_KINDS`; a red pointed hat with a gold star on top and a white beard in `Sprout.tsx`). Kept kinds (pure, `sprouts.ts`): `keptKinds`, `keptSprouts` (the keeper's garden gets its own sprout, `kept-gnome`, a plain sprout with `grows: 'gnome'`, growing from the planting date like any sprout and always blooming into a gnome; backed up with the garden), `givableKinds(view, bloomed, uid)` (the keeper can give gnome sprouts from the start; nobody else ever can, even with one given to them), `kindListed` (the kind shows in the garden's Kinds and the collection only for someone who has one, or keeps them). Given ones arrive grown and show on the receiver's shown garden like any kind. Rules: `sprouts` take kind `gnome` only from that uid, and a profile shows the `gnome` background only for it (emulator: 122 checks pass). Tests: `sprouts.test.ts` ("gnome sprouts belong to their keeper"), `themes.test.ts`, `backdrops.test.ts`. **Needs a Firestore rules deploy** (until then gnome sprouts and the gnome background are refused when saved). To give someone else a kept kind: add a `SPROUT_KINDS` entry with `keepers`, its look in `Sprout.tsx`, and the uid check in `firestore.rules`.
- **Gnome hats in place of the gnome sprout (2026-10-08, same branch; the user didn't like a gnome that arrives grown: a hat that grows into a gnome hopping round the garden, Grace with an endless supply, anyone passing theirs on, and passing moves it: the giver loses it)**: the `gnome` kind is now **Gnome hat** (`seed: true`, `stages` Gnome hat / Peeking out / Little gnome / Gnome, `grown` line, still `keepers: GNOME_FRIENDS`). A hat arrives at stage 0 and grows from when it arrived like a plain sprout (`garden()`; remembered in `bloomed` once grown, with the garden's celebration and the bell: "Your gnome hat is fully grown"); the keeper's own (`kept-gnome`) grows from the planting date. Drawn by `features/sprouts/Gnome.tsx` (`GnomeHat`: a hat in the soil, a face peeking out, a little gnome, a grown gnome with a long beard, coat, belt and a gold star; hat sways, eyes blink; grey when not collected); `Sprout` hands `kind="gnome"` to it. A grown gnome hops in the garden (`GardenScene`: bigger, slower steps while it wanders). *Giving* (pure in `sprouts.ts`): `passable(view)` is the grown hats someone gave you; `givableKinds` offers the hat to its keepers always and to anyone else only with one to pass on; `giftStage` (a hat shows as a hat on the give and pick-up screens). The keeper's gifts use no seeds and have no `via`; anyone else's carry `via` (the hat in their garden), use no seed either, and need it grown ("Grow your gnome all the way first, then you can pass it on."). *Moving it*: `Gift.via` (outbox, kept by `cleanGifts`; `tidy` keeps an unclaimed one so it can still be taken back), `passingOn(gifts)` hides those hats from the garden at once; `GardenSprout.passed` marks it gone for good once picked up (`noteClaimed`) or as Afterglow says (`refresh` reads `passedOn` on what you received, so another phone or a reinstall agrees); taking it back, or a gift refused too often, brings it back. Backend: `SproutInput.via`, `Sprout.via`/`passedOn`; Firebase writes the new sprout and `passedOn` on yours in one batch, and a take-back deletes it and clears `passedOn` in one batch (`takeBackSprout(id, via)`); `fakeSocial` checks the same. Rules: a `gnome` sprout comes from the keeper's uid, or with `via` naming a gnome sprout given to you, not passed on yet, marked `passedOn` as this one in the same write; `via` only on gnomes; you mark your own once (`passedOn` pointing at a sprout made from it) and clear it only once that sprout is gone (emulator: 123 checks pass, "gnome hats: …"). Tests: `sprouts.test.ts` (growth, passable, givable, outbox), `src/app/__tests__/gnomeHats.test.tsx` (keeper gives with no seeds; a growing hat can't be passed; a grown one passed leaves the garden, comes back when taken back, goes again when passed). **Needs a Firestore rules deploy.**
- **Giving moves it (2026-10-09, same branch; the user: "if someone passes any collectible, they lose it and it transfers to the other user", for sprouts and artist collectibles; artist collectibles can be earned again by filming more; kandi stays per person and can't be passed on)**: new plain sprouts still come from your daily seeds (nothing leaves your garden); everything else you give comes out of your garden. Pure (`sprouts.ts`): `passable(view)` is every grown sprout of a kind (grown, given to you, wild, your bloomed first sprout), every fully grown hat and every artist collectible you were given, the ones given to you first (Afterglow can check those), never a keeper's own hat; `toPass(view, kind, artistId?)` picks the one to give; `isReceived`; `givableKinds(view, uid)` (no more `bloomed` argument) is a new sprout, the keeper's kinds and the kinds you have one of. Outbox: `Gift.via` (the sprout in your garden) and `Gift.local` (your first sprout, a wild one: Afterglow isn't told), `passingOn` hides them at once, `seedGifts` counts only new plain sprouts, `artistsGivenAt` (your own collectibles given, per artist). State (`state/sprouts.tsx`): `give` picks what leaves (your own artist collectible if you have it, else one you were given; any other kind from `toPass`); `Stored.gone` (`account:id`, once picked up) and `Stored.artistGone` (`account:artistId` → when) keep it gone for good, in the backup too; taking it back or a gift refused too often brings it back; `refresh` reads `passedOn` on everything you received. Your own artist collectible counts only videos filmed after you last gave it (`sinceGiven` in `artistSprouts.ts`, and back-to-back artists in videos you posted before then don't bring it back), so filming them again earns it back. Screens: the give screen says "Giving it moves it: it leaves your garden for theirs." and lists artist collectibles you were given ("given to you"); an artist you were given has "Give one" in its sheet; bloom lines say "You can give it away now: it moves to their garden." Rules: `via` on any sprout, checked the same way as hats (given to you, same kind or a plain one that grew into it, same artist, not given on yet, `passedOn` set in the same write; emulator: 124 checks pass). Tests: `sprouts.test.ts`, `artistSprouts.test.ts` ("giving your own collectible away"), `src/app/__tests__/givingMoves.test.tsx`. **Needs a Firestore rules deploy.** Note: `screens.test.tsx` "shows your sound as chips…" fails on `main` too from 2026-10-09 (it reads the real date, and Austin City Limits weekend 2 is on).

- **Website feed jumping to the top or the end (2026-10-09; the user: the Discover feed sometimes glitches and scrolls all the way to the top or bottom)**: in a browser, `pagingEnabled` becomes CSS scroll snapping on every child of the list, and the list's children include the blank stand-ins for clips not drawn yet (a block many pages tall with one snap point at its edge), so a quick flick could land on that edge: the very top, or the end page. `lib/paging.ts` (`PAGED`, `pagedListStyle`, `pageSnapStyle`): on the website each clip (and the end page) is its own snap point with `scroll-snap-stop: always`, so a flick stops at the next clip; phones keep `pagingEnabled`. Used by Discover and `components/VerticalPager` (a profile's videos). Checked in Chromium: before, the snap points were clip 1, clip 2, one 4-page block and the end; after, one per clip.

It now keeps `pin:`, `ms:` and `past:` keys of up to 160 characters (no spaces; organizer festival ids `<org>--<event>` fit). Claims are still per account on each phone or browser (the account scope puts them away and brings them back on sign-out and sign-in). Test: `festivalFriends.test.tsx` ("keeps your friends a tap away").
- **Care timing guide cross-checked against TripSit (2026-10-10; the user wants the guide in the App Store build and asked whether it could pass without a qualified review)**: `src/features/care/guide.ts` compared with TripSit's published onset and duration data (github.com/TripSit/drugs, `drugs.json`). Changed to stay at or beyond TripSit's ranges: LSD worn off by 9–14 h (was 8–12), ketamine onset 5–20 min, strongest to 20–45, worn off by 1–2 h (was 5–15 / 15–30 / 45–90 min), cocaine worn off by 60–90 min (was 30–60), GHB onset 15–60 min and strongest 60–90 (was 15–45 / 45–90; the "too soon" warning now lasts the full hour). The rest already matched or were more cautious (MDMA's onset runs to 90 min against TripSit's 70, so the wait-before-more warning is longer). Not a qualified review.

No lawyer's review (the user's choice). The App Store name is Afterglow: Festival Sets ("Afterglow" was taken).

- **Instant updates and the care guide switch (2026-10-12, both for the App Store build before EDC Orlando; the user said "yes to both")**: *Instant updates*: `expo-updates` (~57.0.23, `EXPO_OFFLINE=1 npx expo install`; plugin added to `app.json` by hand), `updates.url` `https://u.expo.dev/<projectId>`, `checkAutomatically` ON_LOAD, `fallbackToCacheTimeout` 0 (downloads in the background, used on the next start), `runtimeVersion` policy `appVersion`; `eas.json` channels: production and preview builds on `production` (so one update reaches TestFlight, the App Store and the Android link), development on `development`; `npm run update` = `eas update --channel production --environment production --auto`. Guide: `docs/testflight.md` "Instant updates". Builds before build 4 have no updater. *Care guide switch*: Firestore `appSettings/app` (`careGuide`, `by`, `at`; anyone reads, only the support account writes; rules test added), `AppSettings` / `appSettingsFromDoc` in `social/models.ts` (anything odd keeps the guide shown), `SocialService.appSettings` / `setAppSettings`, shared store `state/appSettings.ts` (AsyncStorage `appSettings.v1`, kept for no signal), read by `LivePatchSync` on launch and on coming back to the front. HQ → Data → "Care timing guide" switch (`features/admin/AdminSwitches.tsx`, asks first). Off: Look after yourself keeps stopwatches (own words only: `StartStopwatch` `guided`), feelings, water, body and Get help; no substance list, phases, mixes or Timing guide section (`StopwatchCard` `guided`, `care.tsx`). Test: `careGuide.test.tsx` ("with the guide switched off from HQ"). Also pinned "today" in date-dependent screen tests that broke once EDC Colombia (Oct 10–11) and ACL had passed (`festivalMap`, `compare`, two in `screens`).
- **Camera and map crashes in the TestFlight build (2026-10-13; the user: opening the camera, and opening the map, closes the app in TestFlight build 3; fixed without crash reports, at the user's request)**: no JavaScript or native package changed between build 1 (where the camera checks passed) and build 3, and the app's error boundaries would catch an error in its own code, so the causes are native. *Map*: `safeRegion` (`features/festival-map/map.ts`) keeps every region Apple's map is given valid (centre a number and at least 5° from a pole, longitude wrapped, height that stays inside the poles, width at most 350°): `regionOf(boxAround(...))` pads the festivals' spread by 40% with no upper limit, so festivals far apart (or a far-off place) asked MapKit for more than the world, which stops the app ("Invalid Region"). Used for `initialRegion` and every `animateToRegion`. Reanimated pulses inside map pins (your festivals, where you are) are now still rings: animated views inside `react-native-maps` markers are a known crash in real builds, and they only appear once you're going to a festival or near-you is on. *Camera and map*: `OverLiveView` (`components/LiquidGlass.tsx`) makes `GlassFill` draw a plain tinted surface (no BlurView, no Apple glass sampling the live layer) on the camera and map screens. The camera's `CameraView` mounts once the screen's opening slide ends (`transitionEnd`, or `SETTLE_MS` 350 ms) and is `active` only while focused. Tests: `safeRegion` cases in `map.test.ts`; `memories.test.tsx` waits for the camera to start.
- **Yulian's raccoons (2026-10-13; the user: @nyxara (Yulian) should have a special raccoon sprout, "a trash can that then has raccoon hands popping out and then a head and then the whole raccoon is out", super cute and high quality, and the user's own account too)**: a new kept kind, **Raccoon** (`raccoon` in `SPROUT_KINDS`, `seed: true`, stages Trash can / Little paws / Peeking out / Raccoon, `plantedAt` 2026-10-13), working like the gnome hat and the caterpillar: arrives as a trash can, grows from when it arrived, can be passed on once the raccoon is out (it leaves your garden and starts again as a trash can in theirs); keepers give as many as they like with no seeds and grow one of their own (`kept-raccoon`). Keepers: `KEEPS_EVERY_KIND` (the user's account) and, new, **by handle**: `KindSpec.keeperHandles` (`RACCOON_FRIENDS = ['nyxara']`, case-insensitive); `keptKinds`, `keptSprouts`, `kindListed` and `givableKinds` take the account's handle too (`state/sprouts.tsx`, `give-sprout.tsx`, `garden.tsx` pass `social.profile.handle`), and the database looks the same handle up (`handles/nyxara` → uid), so Yulian's account id isn't needed; if Yulian changes handle, change `RACCOON_FRIENDS` and the rule together. Drawn by `features/sprouts/Raccoon.tsx` (`RaccoonSprout`, vinyl-toy style: a shiny ridged bin with a fish bone poking out and two eyes glinting under the lifted lid; the lid open with two little paws gripping the rim; the lid up behind and a masked face peeking over the rim; the whole raccoon sitting by the tipped bin with a slice of pizza, an apple core rolled out, a fluffy striped tail; the bin rattles now and then, paws and face bob, the tail swishes, eyes blink; grey when not collected); checked in a browser screenshot of every stage. A grown raccoon wanders the garden like the gnome hops (`GardenScene`). Rules: `sprouts` take kind `raccoon`, from the user's account, whoever holds `handles/nyxara`, or given on with `via` (emulator: 127 checks pass, "raccoons: …"). Tests: `sprouts.test.ts` ("raccoons: …"). Spanish in `i18n/es/sprouts.ts`. **Needs a Firestore rules deploy** (automatic from `main` when the website key has the roles).
- **The app ships by itself (2026-10-13; the user: "can you set it up so I give you information and it auto publishes to TestFlight")**: `.github/workflows/app.yml` (name "App") runs on `workflow_run` of "Website" when a push to `main` passed its checks: `eas update --channel production --environment production` with the commit's subject as the message (every build from build 4 on picks it up), and, when `expo.version` in `app.json` differs from the previous commit's, `eas build --platform ios --profile production --auto-submit --non-interactive --no-wait` (built on Expo, sent to TestFlight). "Run workflow" builds the iPhone app by hand, with an "Android too" box for the preview APK (Android never automatic: its first build asks set-up questions). Does nothing until the `EXPO_TOKEN` secret is added (steps in `docs/testflight.md`, "Shipping by itself"). Rule in `CLAUDE.md`: raise `version` exactly when a change needs a new build.
- **Sending builds to TestFlight from GitHub (2026-10-13)**: the first "Run workflow" built build 4 on Expo but couldn't submit it non-interactively ("Set ascAppId in the submit profile"): `eas.json` `submit.production.ios.ascAppId` is now the App Store Connect app id 6821236569, and "Run workflow" has "Only send the latest finished iPhone build to TestFlight" (`eas submit --latest`), so a build that finished without reaching TestFlight is sent without a new one.
- **Your festivals at the top of Festivals, and going kept on a new install (2026-10-15; the user: going to EDC Orlando and Cyclops Cove but they no longer showed at the top "with a nice countdown and useful information"; asked for a countdown, top artists, friends going and anything else useful)**: *Why they vanished*: going (`going.v1`) lives only on the phone, so a fresh install (Expo Go → TestFlight) starts empty, and three seconds later `state/social.tsx` published that empty list over the profile's `festivals`. Now `GoingProvider` reports `fresh` (nothing ever saved for this account and nothing picked) and has `restore(ids)`; when fresh and your profile shows festivals, they're brought back into going, and publishing waits until going has loaded and any restore is done. (Only profiles with "Going to" switched on keep the list; one already wiped can't come back, so tap Going again once.) *The cards*: every upcoming festival you're going to (not the set-times one, which keeps `LineupReadyCard`) gets a `NextUpCard`; two or more sit side by side in a snapping row (`UP_PEEK` 28 px of the next showing, at most `MAX_UP_W` 520 wide), the first "Up next", the rest "Also going". Each adds to the big countdown: the last fortnight as a row of 14 lights, one a night, the nights behind you lit (`countdownLights`, `LIGHT_NIGHTS`, first weekend only), **Your top artists here** (`topForYou`: the three best matches with a match above 0, 56 px faces ringed in the festival's light, name and "% match", each opens the artist), who's going (`useGoers` + `goersLine`, up to four faces), and chips: the facts line, "★ n must-sees" (`mustSeesAt`), "Get ready done/total" with a little bar (`usePrep`, hidden while it's on). Pure parts in `features/festivals/upNext.ts` (tested); screen tests in `src/app/__tests__/going.test.tsx` (both cards with a friend going; a new install restoring from the profile, which fails without the fix). Spanish in `src/i18n/es/festivals.ts`.
- **Push notifications, Apple Music and Spotify on TestFlight (2026-10-18, version 1.0.1; the user: "Spotify, Apple Music, and notifications still aren't built"; for pushes they chose "phone to phone" over a Firebase server)**: *Pushes* (`docs/push-notifications.md`): `PushRegistration` (root layout) registers this phone's Expo push token through the new `SocialService.registerPush(token | null, lang)` into `crews/{id}/push/{uid}`, `dms/{id}/push/{uid}` and `pushTokens/{uid}` (rules: `pushShape`, members / the two / friends both ways and support read; up to `PUSH_TOKENS_MAX` 5 tokens), again when your rooms change and once a day (`pushRegistered.v1`); asks permission once, the first time you're in a crew or chat (`pushAsked.v1`). The Firebase adapter, after a write, posts to Expo (`PUSH_ENDPOINT`) without waiting: crew/1-to-1 chat (the last message, "+n more", only the ones newly through on a retry), crew updates (help/ok/lost/check-in/video/photo/reply via `updatePushKind`), meeting spot, crew invite (`/together`), support reply (`/support`); words in each recipient's language (`pushText`, `tIn` in `src/i18n`), batches of `PUSH_BATCH` 100. Leaving, removal, the crew ending and account deletion delete push records; signing out first takes this phone's token out (3 s at most) and forgets `pushRegistered.v1`. `expo-notifications` plugin added to `app.json` (aps-environment). Privacy policy updated (October 18, 2026). Android pushes still need FCM. *Apple Music*: local Expo module `modules/afterglow-musickit` (Swift: `developerToken` via `DefaultMusicTokenProvider`, `authorize` via `MusicAuthorization` + `MusicUserTokenProvider`), wrapped by `features/music/apple/nativeMusicKit.ts` (`readAuthorization`); the adapter uses MusicKit's own developer token when no `EXPO_PUBLIC_APPLE_MUSIC_TOKEN_URL` is set, so no token service; `NSAppleMusicUsageDescription` in `app.json`; the user ticks MusicKit for `app.afterglowmusic` in Identifiers. *Spotify*: no code change; the user creates the Spotify app and adds `EXPO_PUBLIC_SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID` to EAS production (`docs/spotify-setup.md` "On TestFlight"); 5-account development-mode limit. Version 1.0.0 → 1.0.1 (native changes): the first 1.0.1 build needs `npm run build:ios` from the user's PC to sign in to Apple and set up the push key. Tests: `features/notifications/__tests__/push.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/push.test.tsx`, `features/music/__tests__/apple.test.ts` (MusicKit's own token).
- **Track ID with Shazam, and Apple Music saying why it didn't connect (2026-10-18, version 1.0.2; the user: Apple Music still couldn't connect in 1.0.1, and "fold it into this update" for ShazamKit)**: *Track ID* (`docs/track-id.md`): local Expo module `modules/afterglow-shazam` (`matchVideo`: up to three 12 s stretches, middle/start/end, `SHSignatureGenerator.signature(from:)` + `SHSession.result(from:)`, iOS 17+), `src/lib/shazam.ts`; the tagging sheet's **Track** section (`TrackSuggestion`: Shazam hears / Add it / Not it / Remove, "{artist} was playing" via `trackArtistOn`); `Memory.track`, `MemoryTagInput.track`, `ShareInput.track`, `SharedTags.track`, `SharedMemory.track`, `CommunityClipSource.track`; Firestore `trackTitle`/`trackArtist`/`trackUrl` (rules: both or neither, ≤120, link only `https://music.apple.com/`; retag may change them); `TrackLine` on Discover crowd videos and the shared video page. The user ticks ShazamKit beside MusicKit. *Apple Music*: the MusicKit module now returns errors by name (`developerToken`, `authorize` with `MusicSubscription.hasCloudLibraryEnabled`), `musicKitProblem` maps them to new `MusicErrorKind`s `not-ready`, `music-app`, `signed-out`, `no-library`, each with its own line in `connectProblem` (Spanish too). Privacy policy: the Shazam fingerprint and the track on shared videos. Rules emulator 129/0.
- **Camera swipe crash, a smarter new-memory sheet, and a moment after sending (2026-10-18; the user: swiping right to the camera "crashes the app", the new memory page "needs a massive overhaul… only one festival suggestion… a search bar which can auto populate other festivals and shows happening the same day… for artists… all artists playing at time of posting", and "a cute animation after video is sent… remember to take care of yourself")**: *Crash*: `swipeOpens` (`features/camera/record.ts`) was called on the UI thread from the swipe's `onEnd` without a `'worklet'` directive, which closes a release build; it's a worklet now (every other gesture in `src/` was audited: none else calls a plain function). *Festival*: `suggestFestival` (`memories/guess.ts`) suggests one festival only: the one you're going to that night, else the only one where date and place agree (with no place on the video, the only festival that night); the sheet shows it as a card with Change. With none, a search (festivals and shows, `searchShows`) with that night's festivals (`sameNight`) and shows in the town you filmed in (`showsThatNight`) listed under it until you type (`FESTIVAL_RESULTS` 6, `SHOW_RESULTS` 3), and links for a past festival or a one-night show; the wall of festival chips and "Not at a festival or show" are gone (nothing chosen means not at one). *Artists*: `offeredArtists` offers everyone on stage at that minute (your plan first, up to `ON_NOW_SHOWN` 12) once set times are out, your likeliest three before; the artist search is always there. *After sending* (`memories/sent.ts`, `SentMoment.tsx` in the root layout): sharing to Discover or your crew plays one of three looks (rings of light, sparks, a drop of water) with where it went and a care line (water, a breather, your crew, earplugs, food), a success haptic, 2.8 s or a tap; reduced motion fades only. Tests: `memories/__tests__/suggest.test.ts`, `memories.test.tsx`, `webPosts.test.tsx`, `shareToDiscover.test.tsx`.
- **A fresh mix, a cleaner festival page, compact Up next (2026-10-18; the user: "Your mix is always the exact same videos", the Info page has "way too much happening", the checklist is hard to find inside a festival, the Up next cards "take up the entire screen", and the list/grid switch didn't change them)**: *Discover* (`features/recommend/feed.ts`): a clip seen after the 6-hour recent window but within `SEEN_FADE_MS` (7 days) is held back by up to `SEEN_FADE_WEIGHT` 0.35, fading to nothing (`fade`); each opening of the app gets its own shuffle (`seed`, `shuffleOf`, FNV-1a of `seed:clipId`), moving scores by at most ±`SHUFFLE`/2 (0.08), so near-equal matches change order while taste still leads. The seed is the app-open count (`feedSession.v1` in `state/seen.tsx`, `session`); the very first opening has no shuffle (your best matches, in order). Tests: `features/recommend/__tests__/freshMix.test.ts`. *Festival page* (`FestivalPage.tsx`): Info in groups (`styles.group`): **Your trip** (Get ready first when going, crew, Add to calendar), **Tickets and links**, **Where to stay** (one row; the big `StayCard` map opens on tap, `stayOpen`), **More** (poster, map, compare, test festival mode, source); **For you** opens with Get ready when you're going. *Up next* (`NextUpCard.tsx`): compact, no cover photo: name and countdown on one row, the fortnight lights, one line of your top artists with their match, one line of friends going, chips (set times, must-sees, Get ready, which opens `/prep/[id]`) and a small Watch; tapping the card opens the festival. In the grid view the festivals you're going to are tiles under **Going** (`festivals.tsx`). Tests in `src/app/__tests__/going.test.tsx`, `stayCard.test.tsx`.
- **My Sound as a grid, a page for each sound, and a tidier crew page (2026-10-18; the user: the My Sound overview is "very elongated down the page… a grid… and if you click on one, you can see all the nitty gritty", and "the crews page could have a slightly better layout")**: *My Sound* (`features/sound-map/SoundOverview.tsx`): every sound is a tile in a two-column grid (its light, name, a line on how it feels, your share in %, three faces): **Your sounds** (your top three, or "Where you might start"), **Sounds to try next**, **Every sound**; "Find your sound faster" folds into one row that opens the tips; the bubble map button stays. A tile opens `src/app/sound-guide/[tag].tsx`: how much of your sound it is, what it feels like, Listen for, how it differs with its neighbours as chips that open them, your best matches (`artistsIn`, 8), the festivals with the most of it (`festivalsWith`, `mainSound`: at least 3 acts mostly that sound), Watch live sets (Discover) and Open your bubble map. *Crews* (`src/app/crew/[id].tsx`, the member view): a header card (the crew's orb, festival link, name, place and dates, "In 19 days · 3 people", Invite friends and Rename), then chat, where everyone is and the meeting spot, sets you share and the route, **People** (one row each with their pick count, opening to show their starred sets, and Remove for the owner; replaces the stack of picks cards) and Where to stay at the bottom. Tests: `soundOverview.test.tsx`, `crews.test.tsx`, `sound-map/__tests__/soundPage.test.ts`.
- **Festival trail: where you walked on festival days (2026-10-18, version 1.0.3; the user chose "automatically tracked if it's festival day" for the walking map, and asked for an occasional self-care check-in with steps)**: see `docs/trail.md`. `features/care/trail.ts` (pure: `addFixes` filters what the phone reports, `daysOf`/`trailStats`/`boxOf`, `trailFestival`/`trailUntil`/`trailPlan`), `trailStore.ts` (AsyncStorage `trail.v1`, `trailPrefs.v1`, `trailActive.v1`, per account; `recordFixes` is what the background task calls), `trailRecorder.ts` (+ `.web.ts`: the `expo-task-manager` task and `expo-location` start/stop), `TrailKeeper.tsx` (root layout: asks once a festival day, starts and stops), `useTrail.ts`, `TrailMap.tsx` (+ `.web.tsx`), `src/app/trail.tsx` (the screen, from a card on Look after yourself), three "Check in with yourself" reminders at 3, 6 and 9 PM on a festival day while it's on (`trailCheckins`, opening `/trail`). `app.json`: version 1.0.3, `expo-location` plugin with Always permission text, iOS and Android background location and Android foreground service; new package `expo-task-manager`. Privacy policy updated. Tests: `features/care/__tests__/trail.test.ts`, `src/app/__tests__/trail.test.tsx`, `notifications/__tests__/trailCheckins.test.ts`.

- **Crew photo, your own checklist items, and checklist notifications (2026-10-18; the user: "add a cover photo to crews so they can upload a group picture of the crew irl… add their own items to the checklist… send a notification the day before and or day of festival").** Crew photo: `features/crews/CrewCoverPhoto.tsx` at the top of the crew page: a wide photo (any member adds or changes it through the same cropper as the profile cover, `features/profile/pickToCrop.ts`; a quiet "Add a crew photo" row when none; the person who set it or the crew owner can remove it). Stored as `crews/{id}/meta/cover` (`{url, by, at}`, `SocialService.crewCover/setCrewCover`) with the JPEG at `crews/{id}/{uid}/cover.jpg` (the existing storage rule covers it); needs the Firestore rules deploy (`meta/cover` rule, tested in `scripts/firebase-rules.test.mjs`); ending the crew deletes it. Own items: a "Yours" section on the checklist page (`prepCustom.v1`, per account; up to 30 items of 60 characters, no duplicates; a ✕ removes one). Notifications (`prepReminders` in `notifications/reminders.ts`, scheduled by `ReminderSync`): for every festival you're going to, 6 PM the evening before and 8 AM on the first day of each run ("Packed for {festival}?" / "{festival} starts today") saying how many things are left (the count is in the id, so ticking an item reschedules them). Tap opens the checklist. JavaScript only: arrives as an instant update.
- **New logo and app icon (2026-10-19, version 1.0.4; the user sent a picture of an "A" with a sunset and asked for a new logo like it and every instance updated).** A bold gradient "A" of two crossing ribbons (coral to pink to violet to blue) with one of My Sound's glowing orbs in its middle: a glossy violet-to-orange sphere with a neon rim, a faint orbit ring and two small bubbles (the user picked this direction after rejecting an arch-with-sunset first try). Drawn in `scripts/brand-icons.mjs` (`node scripts/brand-icons.mjs`: writes `docs/brand/icon.svg` and renders everything in Chromium): `assets/icon.png`, `splash-icon.png`, `favicon.png`, the Android adaptive background, foreground (art at 64%) and monochrome, `public/apple-touch-icon.png`, `public/og.png`, and a logo with the name and "Find your sound" at `docs/brand/logo.png`. The old orbs icon is gone. The home-screen icon and splash are part of the app build, so this raised `version` to 1.0.4 (a new build); the website, link previews and promo kit (`docs/brand/icon.svg`) change on merge.
- **Discover only shows clips you can watch (2026-10-19; the user: "placeholders for future festivals show up on Discover. If there is no playing video, it should not be shown on the feed!!").** `useRankedFeed` leaves out every clip with no playable media (`clipMedia` undefined: no source, so the drawn stage) and every clip whose video failed to play this session (`features/discover/unplayable.ts`, set by `ClipCard` when the player gives up; left out at the next re-rank, so the clip on screen stays until you move on). The drawn stage remains only as the backdrop while a video loads. Tests: `discover/__tests__/unplayable.test.ts`; `test/afterEnv.ts` forgets the set between tests.
